=== ./cmd/svg-animator/main.go ===
package main

import (
	"flag"
	"fmt"
	"os"

	"gitea.seaoffate.net/blackgrape/svg-animator/internal/generator"
	"gitea.seaoffate.net/blackgrape/svg-animator/internal/web"
)

func main() {
	input := flag.String("in", "", "Path to source image for headless CLI processing (optional)")
	serveAddr := flag.String("serve", ":8088", "HTTP listen address for the interactive web studio")
	outDir := flag.String("outdir", "./dist", "Directory to write output files in CLI mode")
	name := flag.String("name", "", "Custom base name for output files")
	format := flag.String("format", "both", "CLI target format: svg, html, webp, apng, both, or all")
	fps := flag.Int("fps", 24, "Frames per second for rasterized output (webp/apng)")
	preset := flag.String("preset", "ken-burns", "Preset: handheld-drift, film-grain, tilt-shift, chromatic-aberration, motion-blur, exposure-pull, focus-pull, liquid-ripple, perspective-tilt, ken-burns, pan")
	duration := flag.String("duration", "7s", "Animation duration (1s to 60s, e.g. 7s, 14s)")
	loopMode := flag.String("loop", "once", "Playback mode: loop, pingpong, once")

	// Camera Parameters
	cameraMotion := flag.String("motion", "ken-burns", "Camera motion: static, ken-burns, perspective-tilt, handheld-drift")
	startX := flag.Float64("start-x", 50.0, "Focal center X percent (0 to 100)")
	startY := flag.Float64("start-y", 50.0, "Focal center Y percent (0 to 100)")
	endX := flag.Float64("end-x", 60.0, "End focal center X percent")
	endY := flag.Float64("end-y", 40.0, "End focal center Y percent")
	startScale := flag.Float64("start-zoom", 1.0, "Base camera scale (1.0 to 10.0)")
	endScale := flag.Float64("end-zoom", 1.25, "Target camera scale (1.0 to 10.0)")
	startPitch := flag.Float64("start-pitch", 0.0, "Start pitch angle in degrees")
	startYaw := flag.Float64("start-yaw", 0.0, "Start yaw angle in degrees")
	endPitch := flag.Float64("end-pitch", 0.0, "End pitch angle in degrees")
	endYaw := flag.Float64("end-yaw", 0.0, "End yaw angle in degrees")

	// Optical Parameters (Default to neutral/off unless activated by -preset or explicit CLI flag)
	startWave := flag.Float64("start-wave", 0.0, "Start wave displacement strength")
	endWave := flag.Float64("end-wave", 0.0, "End wave displacement strength")
	waveFreq := flag.Float64("wave-freq", 0.02, "Wave frequency / density")
	startBlur := flag.Float64("start-blur", 0.0, "Start blur radius in pixels")
	endBlur := flag.Float64("end-blur", 0.0, "End blur radius in pixels")
	startExposure := flag.Float64("start-exp", 1.0, "Start exposure multiplier")
	endExposure := flag.Float64("end-exp", 1.0, "End exposure multiplier")
	startTemp := flag.Float64("start-temp", 0.0, "Start color temperature")
	endTemp := flag.Float64("end-temp", 0.0, "End color temperature")
	driftSway := flag.Float64("drift-sway", 1.4, "Handheld translation sway percent")
	driftBreathe := flag.Float64("drift-breathe", 1.8, "Handheld breathing scale percent")
	driftRoll := flag.Float64("drift-roll", 0.55, "Handheld micro-tilt roll degrees")

	grainIntensity := flag.Float64("grain-int", 0.0, "Film grain intensity (0.05 to 0.60)")
	grainSize := flag.Float64("grain-size", 0.75, "Film grain size (0.40 to 1.50)")
	gateWeave := flag.Float64("gate-weave", 0.0, "Film gate registration jitter in pixels")
	tiltBlur := flag.Float64("tilt-blur", 0.0, "Tilt-shift blur radius in pixels")
	tiltPos := flag.Float64("tilt-pos", 50.0, "Tilt-shift sharp focal band Y percent")
	tiltBand := flag.Float64("tilt-band", 20.0, "Tilt-shift clear focal band width percent")
	chromaOffset := flag.Float64("chroma-offset", 0.0, "Chromatic aberration fringe offset in pixels")
	chromaAngle := flag.Float64("chroma-angle", 0.0, "Chromatic aberration refraction angle in degrees")
	startMotionBlur := flag.Float64("start-motion-blur", 0.0, "Start motion blur distance in pixels")
	endMotionBlur := flag.Float64("end-motion-blur", 0.0, "End motion blur distance in pixels")
	motionAngle := flag.Float64("motion-angle", 0.0, "Directional motion blur streak angle in degrees")

	// Mitered Frame Flags
	frameStyle := flag.String("frame", "none", "Mitered frame style: none, walnut, gold, matte, obsidian, tiled")
	frameDepth := flag.Float64("frame-depth", 48.0, "Frame moulding depth in pixels (10 to 120)")
	frameShadow := flag.Float64("frame-shadow", 0.55, "Inner aperture drop shadow intensity (0.0 to 1.0)")
	frameSlice := flag.String("frame-slice", "", "Path to custom moulding cross-section slice image (JPEG/PNG)")
	frameSliceWidth := flag.Float64("frame-slice-width", 120.0, "Tiled pattern repeat width in pixels")

	flag.Parse()

	if *input == "" {
		if err := web.StartServer(*serveAddr); err != nil {
			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Server failed: %v\n", err)
			os.Exit(1)
		}
		return
	}

	// Track which flags were explicitly set by the user on the command line
	explicitFlags := make(map[string]bool)
	flag.Visit(func(f *flag.Flag) {
		explicitFlags[f.Name] = true
	})

	// Apply preset defaults only if the corresponding flag was not explicitly passed
	switch *preset {
	case "handheld-drift":
		if !explicitFlags["motion"] {
			*cameraMotion = "handheld-drift"
		}
	case "perspective-tilt":
		if !explicitFlags["motion"] {
			*cameraMotion = "perspective-tilt"
		}
		if !explicitFlags["start-pitch"] {
			*startPitch = 20.0
		}
	case "focus-pull":
		if !explicitFlags["start-blur"] {
			*startBlur = 18.0
		}
	case "liquid-ripple":
		if !explicitFlags["start-wave"] {
			*startWave = 45.0
		}
	case "film-grain":
		if !explicitFlags["grain-int"] {
			*grainIntensity = 0.22
		}
		if !explicitFlags["gate-weave"] {
			*gateWeave = 1.2
		}
	case "tilt-shift":
		if !explicitFlags["tilt-blur"] {
			*tiltBlur = 16.0
		}
	case "chromatic-aberration":
		if !explicitFlags["chroma-offset"] {
			*chromaOffset = 4.5
		}
	case "motion-blur":
		if !explicitFlags["start-motion-blur"] {
			*startMotionBlur = 24.0
		}
	case "exposure-pull":
		if !explicitFlags["start-exp"] {
			*startExposure = 0.35
		}
		if !explicitFlags["end-exp"] {
			*endExposure = 1.05
		}
		if !explicitFlags["start-temp"] {
			*startTemp = -25.0
		}
		if !explicitFlags["end-temp"] {
			*endTemp = 15.0
		}
	}

	opts := generator.Options{
		InputPath:          *input,
		OutputDir:          *outDir,
		BaseName:           *name,
		Format:             *format,
		FPS:                *fps,
		Preset:             *preset,
		Duration:           *duration,
		LoopMode:           *loopMode,
		CameraMotion:       *cameraMotion,
		StartX:             *startX,
		StartY:             *startY,
		EndX:               *endX,
		EndY:               *endY,
		StartScale:         *startScale,
		EndScale:           *endScale,
		StartPitch:         *startPitch,
		StartYaw:           *startYaw,
		EndPitch:           *endPitch,
		EndYaw:             *endYaw,
		StartWaveScale:     *startWave,
		EndWaveScale:       *endWave,
		WaveFreq:           *waveFreq,
		StartBlur:          *startBlur,
		EndBlur:            *endBlur,
		StartExposure:      *startExposure,
		EndExposure:        *endExposure,
		StartTemp:          *startTemp,
		EndTemp:            *endTemp,
		DriftSway:          *driftSway,
		DriftBreathe:       *driftBreathe,
		DriftRoll:          *driftRoll,
		GrainIntensity:     *grainIntensity,
		GrainSize:          *grainSize,
		GateWeave:          *gateWeave,
		TiltBlur:           *tiltBlur,
		TiltPosition:       *tiltPos,
		TiltBandWidth:      *tiltBand,
		ChromaOffset:       *chromaOffset,
		ChromaAngle:        *chromaAngle,
		StartMotionBlur:    *startMotionBlur,
		EndMotionBlur:      *endMotionBlur,
		MotionAngle:        *motionAngle,
		FrameStyle:         *frameStyle,
		FrameDepth:         *frameDepth,
		FrameShadow:        *frameShadow,
		FrameSlicePath:     *frameSlice,
		FrameMouldingWidth: *frameSliceWidth,
	}

	if err := generator.Run(opts); err != nil {
		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "CLI processing error: %v\n", err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}
}-e 

=== ./dist/index.html ===
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <title>index - Animated View</title>
  <style>
    * {
      box-sizing: border-box;
      margin: 0;
      padding: 0;
    }
    body {
      background-color: #0e1117;
      color: #e6edf3;
      font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif;
      min-height: 100vh;
      display: flex;
      flex-direction: column;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      padding: 1.5rem;
    }
    .viewport {
      position: relative;
      width: 100%;
      max-width: 600px;
      aspect-ratio: 600 / 400;
      overflow: hidden;
      border-radius: 8px;
      box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6);
      background-color: #161b22;
    }
    
    @keyframes htmlAnim {
      0%   { transform: scale(1.15) translateX(-6%); }
      50%  { transform: scale(1.15) translateX(6%); }
      100% { transform: scale(1.15) translateX(-6%); }
    }
    
    .animated-img {
      width: 100%;
      height: 100%;
      object-fit: cover;
      transform-origin: center center;
      animation: htmlAnim 4s ease-in-out infinite;
      display: block;
    }
    .meta {
      margin-top: 1rem;
      font-size: 0.85rem;
      color: #8b949e;
      text-align: center;
    }
  </style>
</head>

=== ./internal/rasterizer/rasterizer.go ===
package rasterizer

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"path/filepath"
	"time"

	"github.com/chromedp/chromedp"
)

// ProgressFunc reports the current frame index, total frame count, and active pipeline stage.
type ProgressFunc func(currentFrame, totalFrames int, phase string)

// Export preserves backward compatibility for headless CLI invocations.
func Export(htmlPath, outPath, format string, durationSecs float64, fps int, loopMode string, dims ...int) error {
	return ExportContext(context.Background(), htmlPath, outPath, format, durationSecs, fps, loopMode, nil, dims...)
}

// ExportContext processes an HTML file containing the SVG animation and encodes a single target format.
func ExportContext(ctx context.Context, htmlPath, outPath, format string, durationSecs float64, fps int, loopMode string, onProgress ProgressFunc, dims ...int) error {
	width, height, fps, captureDuration, totalFrames := normalizeParams(durationSecs, fps, loopMode, dims...)

	localTemp := filepath.Join(".", "render_workspace")
	os.MkdirAll(localTemp, 0755)

	frameDir, err := os.MkdirTemp(localTemp, "svg-frames-*")
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create temp frame directory: %w", err)
	}
	defer os.RemoveAll(frameDir)

	if err := captureFrames(ctx, htmlPath, frameDir, captureDuration, fps, width, height, onProgress); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("frame capture failed: %w", err)
	}

	if format == "webp" {
		if onProgress != nil {
			onProgress(totalFrames, totalFrames, "Encoding Animated WebP via FFmpeg...")
		}
		return encodeWebP(ctx, frameDir, outPath, fps, loopMode)
	}

	if onProgress != nil {
		onProgress(totalFrames, totalFrames, "Encoding APNG via FFmpeg...")
	}
	return encodeAPNG(ctx, frameDir, outPath, fps, loopMode)
}

// ExportBothContext captures the SVG frames once and encodes both Animated WebP and APNG from the same frame buffer.
func ExportBothContext(ctx context.Context, htmlPath, webpOutPath, apngOutPath string, durationSecs float64, fps int, loopMode string, onProgress ProgressFunc, dims ...int) error {
	width, height, fps, captureDuration, totalFrames := normalizeParams(durationSecs, fps, loopMode, dims...)

	localTemp := filepath.Join(".", "render_workspace")
	os.MkdirAll(localTemp, 0755)

	frameDir, err := os.MkdirTemp(localTemp, "svg-frames-*")
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create temp frame directory: %w", err)
	}
	defer os.RemoveAll(frameDir)

	if err := captureFrames(ctx, htmlPath, frameDir, captureDuration, fps, width, height, onProgress); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("frame capture failed: %w", err)
	}

	if onProgress != nil {
		onProgress(totalFrames, totalFrames, "Encoding Animated WebP (1/2)...")
	}
	if err := encodeWebP(ctx, frameDir, webpOutPath, fps, loopMode); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if onProgress != nil {
		onProgress(totalFrames, totalFrames, "Encoding APNG (2/2)...")
	}
	if err := encodeAPNG(ctx, frameDir, apngOutPath, fps, loopMode); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	return nil
}

func normalizeParams(durationSecs float64, fps int, loopMode string, dims ...int) (int, int, int, float64, int) {
	width := 1920
	height := 1080
	if len(dims) >= 2 && dims[0] > 0 && dims[1] > 0 {
		width = dims[0]
		height = dims[1]
	}
	if fps <= 0 {
		fps = 24
	}
	if durationSecs <= 0 {
		durationSecs = 7.0
	}

	captureDuration := durationSecs
	if loopMode == "pingpong" {
		captureDuration = durationSecs * 2.0
	}

	totalFrames := int(captureDuration * float64(fps))
	if totalFrames < 1 {
		totalFrames = 1
	}

	return width, height, fps, captureDuration, totalFrames
}

func captureFrames(parentCtx context.Context, htmlPath, frameDir string, duration float64, fps, width, height int, onProgress ProgressFunc) error {
	totalFrames := int(duration * float64(fps))
	if totalFrames < 1 {
		totalFrames = 1
	}

	if onProgress != nil {
		onProgress(0, totalFrames, "Initializing vector rasterization pipeline...")
	}

	opts := append(chromedp.DefaultExecAllocatorOptions[:],
		chromedp.Flag("headless", true),
		chromedp.Flag("disable-gpu", true),
		chromedp.Flag("no-sandbox", true),
		chromedp.Flag("disable-dev-shm-usage", true),
		chromedp.Flag("allow-file-access-from-files", true),
		chromedp.Flag("window-size", fmt.Sprintf("%d,%d", width, height)),
	)

	allocCtx, cancelAlloc := chromedp.NewExecAllocator(parentCtx, opts...)
	defer cancelAlloc()

	ctx, cancel := chromedp.NewContext(allocCtx)
	defer cancel()

	ctx, cancelTimeout := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 15*time.Minute)
	defer cancelTimeout()

	absPath, err := filepath.Abs(htmlPath)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get absolute html path: %w", err)
	}
	fileURL := "file://" + filepath.ToSlash(absPath)

	scrubberJS := `
		function solveCubicBezier(x1, y1, x2, y2, x) {
			if (x <= 0) return 0;
			if (x >= 1) return 1;
			if (x1 === y1 && x2 === y2) return x;
			let t = x;
			for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
				const cx = 3 * x1;
				const bx = 3 * (x2 - x1) - cx;
				const ax = 1 - cx - bx;
				const currentX = ((ax * t + bx) * t + cx) * t - x;
				if (Math.abs(currentX) < 1e-6) break;
				const deriv = (3 * ax * t + 2 * bx) * t + cx;
				if (Math.abs(deriv) < 1e-6) break;
				t -= currentX / deriv;
			}
			const cy = 3 * y1;
			const by = 3 * (y2 - y1) - cy;
			const ay = 1 - cy - by;
			return ((ay * t + by) * t + cy) * t;
		}

		function setAnimTime(ms) {
			const sec = ms / 1000.0;
			
			// 1. Scrub CSS Keyframes (Camera Rig pan/zoom/handheld transforms)
			document.getAnimations().forEach(anim => {
				anim.pause();
				anim.currentTime = ms;
			});
			
			// 2. Scrub standard SVG SMIL animations (glows, data flows, ripples)
			document.querySelectorAll('svg').forEach(svg => { 
				if (svg.setCurrentTime) { 
					svg.pauseAnimations(); 
					svg.setCurrentTime(sec); 
				} 
			});

			// 3. Force-override SVG filters & masks with full keyTimes + keySplines support
			document.querySelectorAll('filter animate, filter animateTransform, mask animate, mask animateTransform').forEach(anim => {
				const parent = anim.parentElement;
				const attr = anim.getAttribute('attributeName');
				let dur = parseFloat(anim.getAttribute('dur'));
				if (isNaN(dur) || dur <= 0) dur = 1.0;
				const begin = parseFloat(anim.getAttribute('begin')) || 0.0;

				const localSec = sec - begin;
				if (localSec < 0) return;

				let progress;
				if (anim.getAttribute('repeatCount') === 'indefinite') {
					progress = (localSec % dur) / dur;
				} else {
					progress = localSec / dur;
					if (progress > 1.0) progress = 1.0;
				}
				if (progress < 0.0) progress = 0.0;
				
				let fromAttr = anim.getAttribute('from');
				let toAttr = anim.getAttribute('to');
				let segProgress = progress;

				const calcMode = anim.getAttribute('calcMode') || 'linear';
				const splinesAttr = anim.getAttribute('keySplines');
				const splines = splinesAttr ? splinesAttr.split(';').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean) : [];
				const keyTimesAttr = anim.getAttribute('keyTimes');
				const keyTimes = keyTimesAttr ? keyTimesAttr.split(';').map(k => parseFloat(k.trim())).filter(k => !isNaN(k)) : [];

				if (anim.hasAttribute('values')) {
					const vals = anim.getAttribute('values').split(';').map(v => v.trim()).filter(v => v !== '');
					const numSegments = vals.length - 1;
					if (numSegments > 0) {
						let segIdx = 0;
						if (keyTimes.length === vals.length) {
							for (let i = 0; i < numSegments; i++) {
								if (progress >= keyTimes[i] && progress <= keyTimes[i + 1]) {
									segIdx = i;
									const span = keyTimes[i + 1] - keyTimes[i];
									segProgress = span > 0 ? (progress - keyTimes[i]) / span : 1.0;
									break;
								} else if (progress > keyTimes[numSegments]) {
									segIdx = numSegments - 1;
									segProgress = 1.0;
								}
							}
						} else {
							let scaled = progress * numSegments;
							if (scaled >= numSegments) {
								segIdx = numSegments - 1;
								segProgress = 1.0;
							} else {
								segIdx = Math.floor(scaled);
								segProgress = scaled - segIdx;
							}
						}

						fromAttr = vals[segIdx];
						toAttr = vals[segIdx + 1];

						if (calcMode === 'spline' && splines[segIdx]) {
							const pts = splines[segIdx].split(/[\s,]+/).map(parseFloat);
							if (pts.length === 4 && !pts.some(isNaN)) {
								segProgress = solveCubicBezier(pts[0], pts[1], pts[2], pts[3], segProgress);
							}
						}
					}
				} else if (calcMode === 'spline' && splines[0]) {
					const pts = splines[0].split(/[\s,]+/).map(parseFloat);
					if (pts.length === 4 && !pts.some(isNaN)) {
						segProgress = solveCubicBezier(pts[0], pts[1], pts[2], pts[3], segProgress);
					}
				}

				if (fromAttr != null && toAttr != null) {
					const fromParts = fromAttr.split(/[\s,]+/);
					const toParts = toAttr.split(/[\s,]+/);
					
					let resultVals = [];
					for (let i = 0; i < fromParts.length; i++) {
						const f = parseFloat(fromParts[i]);
						const t = parseFloat(toParts[i] || toParts[0]);
						if (!isNaN(f) && !isNaN(t)) {
							const val = f + (t - f) * segProgress;
							resultVals.push(val.toFixed(4));
						}
					}
					if (resultVals.length > 0) {
						parent.setAttribute(attr, resultVals.join(' '));
					}
				}
			});

			// 4. Force synchronous style & layout flush so the frame buffer is up to date before capture
			const svgEl = document.querySelector('svg');
			if (svgEl) {
				void svgEl.getBoundingClientRect();
			}
			return true;
		}
	`

	cssOverrideJS := `
		document.body.style.margin = '0';
		document.body.style.padding = '0';
		document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
		document.body.style.backgroundColor = '#000000';
		
		var vp = document.querySelector('.viewport');
		if (vp) {
			vp.style.all = 'unset';
			vp.style.display = 'block';
			vp.style.width = '100vw';
			vp.style.height = '100vh';
		}
		
		var svg = document.querySelector('svg');
		if (svg) {
			svg.style.position = 'absolute';
			svg.style.top = '0';
			svg.style.left = '0';
			svg.style.width = '100vw';
			svg.style.height = '100vh';
			svg.style.margin = '0';
			svg.style.display = 'block';
			svg.setAttribute('preserveAspectRatio', 'none'); 
		}
	`

	fmt.Println(">> Target URL:", fileURL)
	fmt.Println(">> Initializing vector rasterization pipeline...")

	if _, err := chromedp.Run(ctx, chromedp.Navigate(fileURL)); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("failed to navigate: %w", err)
	}

	if _, err := chromedp.Run(ctx, chromedp.WaitReady(`svg`)); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("svg failed to render: %w", err)
	}

	if _, err := chromedp.Run(ctx, chromedp.Evaluate[any](cssOverrideJS)); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("failed to inject css overrides: %w", err)
	}

	if _, err := chromedp.Run(ctx, chromedp.Evaluate[any](scrubberJS)); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("failed to inject scrubber: %w", err)
	}

	fmt.Printf(">> Capturing %d frames at %d FPS (%dx%d)...\n", totalFrames, fps, width, height)

	for i := 0; i < totalFrames; i++ {
		if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("render canceled: %w", err)
		}

		ms := float64(i) * (1000.0 / float64(fps))
		framePath := filepath.Join(frameDir, fmt.Sprintf("frame_%04d.png", i))

		if _, err := chromedp.Run(ctx, chromedp.Evaluate[any](fmt.Sprintf("setAnimTime(%f)", ms))); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("failed scrubbing frame %d: %w", i, err)
		}

		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return fmt.Errorf("render canceled: %w", ctx.Err())
		case <-time.After(35 * time.Millisecond):
		}

		buf, err := chromedp.Run(ctx, chromedp.CaptureScreenshot())
		if err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("failed capturing frame %d: %w", i, err)
		}
		if err := os.WriteFile(framePath, buf, 0644); err != nil {
			return err
		}

		if onProgress != nil {
			onProgress(i+1, totalFrames, fmt.Sprintf("Capturing frame %d of %d", i+1, totalFrames))
		}
	}

	fmt.Println(">> Frame capture complete. Encoding...")
	return nil
}

func encodeWebP(ctx context.Context, frameDir, outPath string, fps int, loopMode string) error {
	inputPattern := filepath.Join(frameDir, "frame_%04d.png")
	args := []string{"-y", "-framerate", fmt.Sprintf("%d", fps), "-i", inputPattern, "-vcodec", "libwebp", "-lossless", "0", "-qscale", "80", "-an"}

	if loopMode == "once" {
		args = append(args, "-loop", "1")
	} else {
		args = append(args, "-loop", "0")
	}

	args = append(args, outPath)
	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "ffmpeg", args...)

	out, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("ffmpeg WebP encode failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, string(out))
	}
	return nil
}

func encodeAPNG(ctx context.Context, frameDir, outPath string, fps int, loopMode string) error {
	inputPattern := filepath.Join(frameDir, "frame_%04d.png")

	args := []string{"-y", "-framerate", fmt.Sprintf("%d", fps), "-i", inputPattern, "-f", "apng"}

	if loopMode == "once" {
		args = append(args, "-plays", "1")
	} else {
		args = append(args, "-plays", "0")
	}

	args = append(args, outPath)
	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "ffmpeg", args...)

	out, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("ffmpeg APNG encode failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, string(out))
	}
	return nil
}-e 

=== ./internal/web/server.go ===
package web

import (
	"context"
	"embed"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"io/fs"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"gitea.seaoffate.net/blackgrape/svg-animator/internal/generator"
	"gitea.seaoffate.net/blackgrape/svg-animator/internal/rasterizer"
)

//go:embed static/*
var staticFS embed.FS

type renderJobStatus struct {
	mu           sync.RWMutex
	CurrentFrame int                `json:"currentFrame"`
	TotalFrames  int                `json:"totalFrames"`
	Percent      int                `json:"percent"`
	Phase        string             `json:"phase"`
	Done         bool               `json:"done"`
	Canceled     bool               `json:"canceled"`
	Error        string             `json:"error,omitempty"`
	cancelFunc   context.CancelFunc
}

type renderedBundle struct {
	dirPath  string
	webpPath string
	apngPath string
}

var (
	activeJobs    sync.Map // map[string]*renderJobStatus
	activeBundles sync.Map // map[string]*renderedBundle
)

type renderStatePayload struct {
	JobID     string  `json:"jobId"`
	Format    string  `json:"format"`
	Duration  float64 `json:"duration"`
	FPS       int     `json:"fps"`
	Width     int     `json:"width"`
	Height    int     `json:"height"`
	SVGMarkup string  `json:"svgMarkup"`
	State     struct {
		Image struct {
			Width  int    `json:"width"`
			Height int    `json:"height"`
			Mime   string `json:"mime"`
			B64    string `json:"b64"`
		} `json:"image"`
		Duration float64 `json:"duration"`
		Loop     string  `json:"loop"`
		Camera   struct {
			Motion        string  `json:"motion"`
			BgColor       string  `json:"bgColor"`
			BgTransparent bool    `json:"bgTransparent"`
			StartX        float64 `json:"startX"`
			StartY        float64 `json:"startY"`
			StartZoom     float64 `json:"startZoom"`
			EndX          float64 `json:"endX"`
			EndY          float64 `json:"endY"`
			EndZoom       float64 `json:"endZoom"`
			Sway          float64 `json:"sway"`
			Breathe       float64 `json:"breathe"`
			TiltX         float64 `json:"tiltX"`
			TiltY         float64 `json:"tiltY"`
		} `json:"camera"`
		Optical struct {
			Type      string  `json:"type"`
			StartBlur float64 `json:"startBlur"`
			EndBlur   float64 `json:"endBlur"`
			FocusDur  float64 `json:"focusDur"`
			TsShape   string  `json:"tsShape"`
			TsPosX    float64 `json:"tsPosX"`
			TsPos     float64 `json:"tsPos"`
			TsWidth   float64 `json:"tsWidth"`
			TsBlur    float64 `json:"tsBlur"`
			MbInt     float64 `json:"mbInt"`
			MbAng     float64 `json:"mbAng"`
		} `json:"optical"`
		Overlays struct {
			Frame      bool    `json:"frame"`
			FrameStyle string  `json:"frameStyle"`
			FrameDepth float64 `json:"frameDepth"`
		} `json:"overlays"`
	} `json:"state"`
}

func StartServer(bindAddr string) error {
	subFS, err := fs.Sub(staticFS, "static")
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("mounting static embedded files: %w", err)
	}

	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	mux.Handle("/", http.FileServer(http.FS(subFS)))
	mux.HandleFunc("/api/export", handleExport)
	mux.HandleFunc("/api/export/progress", handleExportProgress)
	mux.HandleFunc("/api/export/cancel", handleExportCancel)
	mux.HandleFunc("/api/export/bundle", handleExportBundle)

	fmt.Println("========================================================")
	fmt.Printf(" SVG Animator Web Studio listening on http://%s\n", bindAddr)
	fmt.Println("========================================================")

	return http.ListenAndServe(bindAddr, mux)
}

func handleExportProgress(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	jobID := r.URL.Query().Get("jobId")
	if jobID == "" {
		http.Error(w, "Missing jobId", http.StatusBadRequest)
		return
	}

	val, ok := activeJobs.Load(jobID)
	if !ok {
		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
		json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]any{
			"currentFrame": 0,
			"totalFrames":  0,
			"percent":      0,
			"phase":        "Preparing render job...",
			"done":         false,
		})
		return
	}

	job := val.(*renderJobStatus)
	job.mu.RLock()
	resp := map[string]any{
		"currentFrame": job.CurrentFrame,
		"totalFrames":  job.TotalFrames,
		"percent":      job.Percent,
		"phase":        job.Phase,
		"done":         job.Done,
		"canceled":     job.Canceled,
		"error":        job.Error,
	}
	job.mu.RUnlock()

	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(resp)
}

func handleExportCancel(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if r.Method != http.MethodPost {
		http.Error(w, "Method not allowed", http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)
		return
	}

	jobID := r.URL.Query().Get("jobId")
	if jobID != "" {
		if val, ok := activeJobs.Load(jobID); ok {
			job := val.(*renderJobStatus)
			job.mu.Lock()
			job.Canceled = true
			job.Phase = "Render canceled by user."
			if job.cancelFunc != nil {
				job.cancelFunc()
			}
			job.mu.Unlock()
		}
	}

	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]bool{"canceled": true})
}

func handleExportBundle(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	bundleID := r.URL.Query().Get("bundleId")
	format := r.URL.Query().Get("fmt")
	if bundleID == "" || (format != "webp" && format != "apng") {
		http.Error(w, "Invalid bundle request", http.StatusBadRequest)
		return
	}

	val, ok := activeBundles.Load(bundleID)
	if !ok {
		http.Error(w, "Bundle expired or not found", http.StatusNotFound)
		return
	}

	b := val.(*renderedBundle)
	targetPath := b.webpPath
	contentType := "image/webp"
	ext := ".webp"
	if format == "apng" {
		targetPath = b.apngPath
		contentType = "image/png"
		ext = ".png"
	}

	file, err := os.Open(targetPath)
	if err != nil {
		http.Error(w, "Failed to open bundle file", http.StatusInternalServerError)
		return
	}
	defer file.Close()

	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", contentType)
	w.Header().Set("Content-Disposition", fmt.Sprintf(`attachment; filename="export%s"`, ext))
	io.Copy(w, file)
}

func handleExport(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	r.Body = http.MaxBytesReader(w, r.Body, 50*1024*1024)

	if r.Method != http.MethodPost {
		http.Error(w, "Method not allowed", http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)
		return
	}

	var req renderStatePayload
	if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&req); err != nil {
		http.Error(w, "Invalid JSON payload", http.StatusBadRequest)
		return
	}

	renderCtx, cancelRender := context.WithCancel(r.Context())
	defer cancelRender()

	job := &renderJobStatus{
		Phase:      "Starting render pipeline...",
		cancelFunc: cancelRender,
	}
	if req.JobID != "" {
		activeJobs.Store(req.JobID, job)
		defer activeJobs.Delete(req.JobID)
	}

	onProgress := func(current, total int, phase string) {
		job.mu.Lock()
		job.CurrentFrame = current
		job.TotalFrames = total
		if total > 0 {
			pct := int((float64(current) / float64(total)) * 100.0)
			if pct > 100 {
				pct = 100
			}
			job.Percent = pct
		}
		job.Phase = phase
		job.mu.Unlock()
	}

	localTemp := filepath.Join(".", "render_workspace")
	os.MkdirAll(localTemp, 0755)

	tmpDir, err := os.MkdirTemp(localTemp, "svg-api-export-*")
	if err != nil {
		http.Error(w, "Failed to create temp directory", http.StatusInternalServerError)
		return
	}

	keepTmpDir := false
	defer func() {
		if !keepTmpDir {
			os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)
		}
	}()

	st := req.State
	exportWidth := req.Width
	exportHeight := req.Height
	if exportWidth <= 0 {
		exportWidth = st.Image.Width
	}
	if exportHeight <= 0 {
		exportHeight = st.Image.Height
	}

	svgText := req.SVGMarkup
	if svgText == "" {
		frameStyle := "none"
		if st.Overlays.Frame {
			frameStyle = st.Overlays.FrameStyle
		}

		opts := generator.Options{
			Duration:        fmt.Sprintf("%.1fs", req.Duration),
			LoopMode:        st.Loop,
			CameraMotion:    st.Camera.Motion,
			StartX:          st.Camera.StartX,
			StartY:          st.Camera.StartY,
			StartScale:      st.Camera.StartZoom,
			EndX:            st.Camera.EndX,
			EndY:            st.Camera.EndY,
			EndScale:        st.Camera.EndZoom,
			DriftSway:       st.Camera.Sway,
			DriftBreathe:    st.Camera.Breathe,
			StartPitch:      st.Camera.TiltX,
			StartYaw:        st.Camera.TiltY,
			StartBlur:       st.Optical.StartBlur,
			EndBlur:         st.Optical.EndBlur,
			StartExposure:   1.0,
			EndExposure:     1.0,
			TiltBlur:        st.Optical.TsBlur,
			TiltPosition:    st.Optical.TsPos,
			TiltBandWidth:   st.Optical.TsWidth,
			StartMotionBlur: st.Optical.MbInt,
			MotionAngle:     st.Optical.MbAng,
			FrameStyle:      frameStyle,
			FrameDepth:      st.Overlays.FrameDepth,
			FrameShadow:     0.55,
		}

		if st.Optical.Type != "focus-pull" {
			opts.StartBlur = 0.0
			opts.EndBlur = 0.0
		}
		if st.Optical.Type != "motion-blur" {
			opts.StartMotionBlur = 0.0
			opts.MotionAngle = 0.0
		}
		if st.Optical.Type != "tilt-shift" {
			opts.TiltBlur = 0.0
			opts.TiltBandWidth = 0.0
		}

		svgText = generator.BuildStandaloneSVG(st.Image.Width, st.Image.Height, st.Image.Mime, st.Image.B64, opts)
	}

	htmlText := generator.BuildStandaloneHTML("export", exportWidth, exportHeight, svgText)

	htmlPath := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "index.html")
	if err := os.WriteFile(htmlPath, []byte(htmlText), 0644); err != nil {
		http.Error(w, "Failed to write temp HTML", http.StatusInternalServerError)
		return
	}

	// Dual Export Mode: Capture frames once and stream WebP + APNG via binary bundle endpoint
	if req.Format == "both" {
		webpPath := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "output.webp")
		apngPath := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "output.png")

		if err := rasterizer.ExportBothContext(renderCtx, htmlPath, webpPath, apngPath, req.Duration, req.FPS, st.Loop, onProgress, exportWidth, exportHeight); err != nil {
			if errors.Is(renderCtx.Err(), context.Canceled) {
				http.Error(w, "Render canceled", http.StatusRequestTimeout)
				return
			}
			http.Error(w, fmt.Sprintf("Rasterizer error: %v", err), http.StatusInternalServerError)
			return
		}

		keepTmpDir = true
		bundleID := fmt.Sprintf("bundle-%d", time.Now().UnixNano())
		activeBundles.Store(bundleID, &renderedBundle{
			dirPath:  tmpDir,
			webpPath: webpPath,
			apngPath: apngPath,
		})

		// Automatically clean up temporary bundle directory after 3 minutes
		time.AfterFunc(3*time.Minute, func() {
			activeBundles.Delete(bundleID)
			os.RemoveAll(tmpDir)
		})

		job.mu.Lock()
		job.Done = true
		job.Percent = 100
		job.Phase = "Complete"
		job.mu.Unlock()

		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
		json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]string{
			"bundleId": bundleID,
		})
		return
	}

	// Single Format Export Mode (WebP or APNG)
	outExt := ".png"
	if req.Format == "webp" {
		outExt = ".webp"
	}
	outPath := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "output"+outExt)

	if err := rasterizer.ExportContext(renderCtx, htmlPath, outPath, req.Format, req.Duration, req.FPS, st.Loop, onProgress, exportWidth, exportHeight); err != nil {
		if errors.Is(renderCtx.Err(), context.Canceled) {
			http.Error(w, "Render canceled", http.StatusRequestTimeout)
			return
		}
		http.Error(w, fmt.Sprintf("Rasterizer error: %v", err), http.StatusInternalServerError)
		return
	}

	job.mu.Lock()
	job.Done = true
	job.Percent = 100
	job.Phase = "Complete"
	job.mu.Unlock()

	file, err := os.Open(outPath)
	if err != nil {
		http.Error(w, "Failed to open output file", http.StatusInternalServerError)
		return
	}
	defer file.Close()

	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/octet-stream")
	w.Header().Set("Content-Disposition", fmt.Sprintf(`attachment; filename="export%s"`, outExt))
	io.Copy(w, file)
}-e

=== ./internal/web/static/js/ripples.js ===
window.Ripples = {
  list: [],
  activeId: null,
  counter: 0,
  _initialized: false,
  
  init() {
    if (this._initialized) return;
    this._initialized = true;

    document.getElementById('btnAddRipple')?.addEventListener('click', () => this.addRipple());
    document.getElementById('rippleColorPicker')?.addEventListener('change', (e) => { this.updateActive('color', e.target.value); });

    ['outerR', 'innerR', 'rings', 'thick', 'refraction', 'opacity', 'dur', 'delay', 'endDelay'].forEach(key => {
      const slider = document.getElementById(`ripple${key.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + key.slice(1)}Slider`);
      const num = document.getElementById(`ripple${key.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + key.slice(1)}Num`);
      if (slider && num) {
        slider.addEventListener('input', (e) => { num.value = e.target.value; this.updateActive(key, parseFloat(e.target.value)); });
        num.addEventListener('input', (e) => { slider.value = e.target.value; this.updateActive(key, parseFloat(e.target.value)); });
      }
    });
  },

  addRipple() {
    this.counter++;
    const newRipple = {
      id: this.counter, color: '#ffffff', opacity: 0.4, innerR: 0, outerR: 150, rings: 3, thick: 8, refraction: 35,
      dur: 3.0, delay: 0.0, endDelay: 1.0, x: 50, y: 50
    };
    this.list.push(newRipple);
    this.selectRipple(newRipple.id);
    this.triggerAppRender();
  },

  selectRipple(id) {
    if (window.DataFlows) window.DataFlows.deselectFlow(); 
    if (window.Glows) window.Glows.deselectGlow();
    if (window.DiagramNodes) window.DiagramNodes.deselectNode();
    this.activeId = id;
    this.renderList();
    document.body.classList.add('editing-flow'); 
    
    const rp = this.list.find(r => r.id === id);
    if (rp) {
      document.getElementById('rippleEditor').style.display = 'block';
      document.getElementById('editingRippleLabel').innerText = `Editing Ripple ${id}`;
      document.getElementById('rippleColorPicker').value = rp.color;
      
      ['outerR', 'innerR', 'rings', 'thick', 'refraction', 'opacity', 'dur', 'delay', 'endDelay'].forEach(key => {
        const val = rp[key] !== undefined ? rp[key] : 0;
        const sl = document.getElementById(`ripple${key.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + key.slice(1)}Slider`);
        const nu = document.getElementById(`ripple${key.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + key.slice(1)}Num`);
        if (sl) sl.value = val;
        if (nu) nu.value = val;
      });
      
      this.updateUIOverlay(rp);
      document.getElementById('rippleReticleTarget').style.display = 'flex';
    }
  },

  deselectRipple() {
    this.activeId = null;
    this.renderList();
    document.body.classList.remove('editing-flow');
    const editor = document.getElementById('rippleEditor');
    if (editor) editor.style.display = 'none';
    const ret = document.getElementById('rippleReticleTarget');
    if (ret) ret.style.display = 'none';
  },

  updateActive(key, value) {
    if (this.activeId === null) return;
    const rp = this.list.find(r => r.id === this.activeId);
    if (rp) {
      rp[key] = value;
      this.renderList();
      this.triggerAppRender();
      if (key === 'color') this.updateUIOverlay(rp);
    }
  },

  handleStageClick(x, y) {
    if (this.activeId === null) return false;
    const rp = this.list.find(r => r.id === this.activeId);
    if (rp) {
      rp.x = x;
      rp.y = y;
      this.updateUIOverlay(rp);
      this.triggerAppRender();
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  },

  updateUIOverlay(rp) {
    const ret = document.getElementById('rippleReticleTarget');
    if (ret) {
      ret.style.left = `${rp.x}%`;
      ret.style.top = `${rp.y}%`;
      ret.style.borderColor = rp.color;
      ret.style.backgroundColor = `${rp.color}33`;
    }
  },

  removeRipple(id) {
    this.list = this.list.filter(r => r.id !== id);
    if (this.activeId === id) this.deselectRipple();
    else this.renderList();
    this.triggerAppRender();
  },

  renderList() {
    const container = document.getElementById('rippleListContainer');
    if (!container) return;
    if (this.list.length === 0) {
      container.innerHTML = '<div class="stage-hint" id="emptyRippleHint">No ripples active.</div>';
      return;
    }
    container.innerHTML = '';
    this.list.forEach(rp => {
      const div = document.createElement('div');
      div.className = 'flow-list-item' + (this.activeId === rp.id ? ' selected' : '');
      div.style.borderLeftColor = '#58a6ff';
      div.innerHTML = `<span>Ripple ${rp.id}</span><button style="background:transparent; border:none; color:#ff4444; font-weight:bold; cursor:pointer;" onclick="window.Ripples.removeRipple(${rp.id}); event.stopPropagation();">X</button>`;
      div.onclick = () => this.selectRipple(rp.id);
      container.appendChild(div);
    });
  },

  triggerAppRender() {
    if (typeof window.triggerAppRender === 'function') {
      window.triggerAppRender();
    } else {
      document.getElementById('durationSlider')?.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));
    }
  },

  injectIntoSVG(svgString, state) {
    if (this.list.length === 0) return svgString;
    const width = state.image.width;
    const height = state.image.height;
    const isBlank = !!state.image.isBlankCanvas;

    let defs = '';
    let elements = '';

    this.list.forEach(rp => {
      const px = (rp.x / 100) * width;
      const py = (rp.y / 100) * height;
      const totalDur = rp.dur + (rp.endDelay || 0);
      const scaleRefract = 1.0 + (rp.refraction / 1000);
      const maskId = `rippleMask-${rp.id}`;
      const blurId = `rippleBlur-${rp.id}`;

      const blurAmount = Math.max(2, rp.thick * 1.5);
      defs += `<filter id="${blurId}" filterUnits="userSpaceOnUse" x="-20%" y="-20%" width="140%" height="140%"><feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="${blurAmount}" /></filter>`;

      let ringMasks = '';
      let ringHighlights = '';
      let ringShadows = '';
      const easeSpline = "0.1 0.8 0.3 1";
      const holdSpline = "0 0 1 1";
      const rRatio = rp.endDelay > 0 ? (rp.dur / totalDur).toFixed(4) : "1";

      for (let i = 0; i < rp.rings; i++) {
        const stagger = (rp.dur / rp.rings) * i;
        const beginTime = rp.delay + stagger;
        let rAnim = '';
        let opAnimMask = '';
        let opAnimCrest = '';

        if (rp.endDelay > 0) {
          rAnim = `<animate attributeName="r" values="${rp.innerR}; ${rp.outerR}; ${rp.outerR}" keyTimes="0; ${rRatio}; 1" calcMode="spline" keySplines="${easeSpline}; ${holdSpline}" dur="${totalDur}s" begin="${beginTime}s" repeatCount="indefinite" />`;
          opAnimMask = `<animate attributeName="stroke-opacity" values="1; 0; 0" keyTimes="0; ${rRatio}; 1" calcMode="spline" keySplines="${easeSpline}; ${holdSpline}" dur="${totalDur}s" begin="${beginTime}s" repeatCount="indefinite" />`;
          opAnimCrest = `<animate attributeName="stroke-opacity" values="${rp.opacity}; 0; 0" keyTimes="0; ${rRatio}; 1" calcMode="spline" keySplines="${easeSpline}; ${holdSpline}" dur="${totalDur}s" begin="${beginTime}s" repeatCount="indefinite" />`;
        } else {
          rAnim = `<animate attributeName="r" values="${rp.innerR}; ${rp.outerR}" keyTimes="0; 1" calcMode="spline" keySplines="${easeSpline}" dur="${rp.dur}s" begin="${beginTime}s" repeatCount="indefinite" />`;
          opAnimMask = `<animate attributeName="stroke-opacity" values="1; 0" keyTimes="0; 1" calcMode="spline" keySplines="${easeSpline}" dur="${rp.dur}s" begin="${beginTime}s" repeatCount="indefinite" />`;
          opAnimCrest = `<animate attributeName="stroke-opacity" values="${rp.opacity}; 0" keyTimes="0; 1" calcMode="spline" keySplines="${easeSpline}" dur="${rp.dur}s" begin="${beginTime}s" repeatCount="indefinite" />`;
        }

        const shadowOp = (rp.opacity * 0.7).toFixed(2);
        const opAnimShadow = opAnimCrest.replace(`values="${rp.opacity}`, `values="${shadowOp}`);

        ringMasks += `<circle cx="${px}" cy="${py}" r="0" fill="none" stroke="white" stroke-width="${rp.thick * 3}" stroke-opacity="0" filter="url(#${blurId})">${rAnim}${opAnimMask}</circle>`;
        ringHighlights += `<circle cx="${px}" cy="${py}" r="0" fill="none" stroke="${rp.color}" stroke-width="${rp.thick * 0.8}" stroke-opacity="0" filter="url(#${blurId})">${rAnim}${opAnimCrest}</circle>`;
        ringShadows += `<g transform="translate(${px}, ${py}) scale(0.92) translate(${-px}, ${-py})"><circle cx="${px}" cy="${py}" r="0" fill="none" stroke="black" stroke-width="${rp.thick * 1.5}" stroke-opacity="0" filter="url(#${blurId})">${rAnim}${opAnimShadow}</circle></g>`;
      }

      defs += `<mask id="${maskId}">${ringMasks}</mask>`;
      const refractedPhoto = !isBlank && window.Effects
        ? `<g mask="url(#${maskId})" pointer-events="none"><g transform="translate(${px}, ${py}) scale(${scaleRefract}) translate(${-px}, ${-py})">${window.Effects.buildPhotoElement(state.image, width, height)}</g></g>`
        : '';
      elements += `${refractedPhoto}<g pointer-events="none">${ringShadows}</g><g pointer-events="none">${ringHighlights}</g>`;
    });

    return svgString.replace('<!-- INJECT_CAMERA_RIG_ELEMENTS -->', `\n<defs>\n${defs}\n</defs>\n<g id="ripples-layer">\n${elements}\n</g>\n<!-- INJECT_CAMERA_RIG_ELEMENTS -->`);
  }
};-e 

=== ./internal/web/static/js/glows.js ===
window.DataFlows = {
  list: [],
  activeId: null,
  activeNodeIdx: 0,
  counter: 0,
  _initialized: false,

  escapeXML(str) {
    if (!str) return '';
    return String(str)
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
      .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
      .replace(/'/g, '&apos;');
  },

  init() {
    if (this._initialized) return;
    this._initialized = true;

    const btnAdd = document.getElementById('btnAddFlow');
    if (btnAdd) btnAdd.addEventListener('click', () => this.addFlow());

    const btnAddNode = document.getElementById('btnFlowAddNode');
    if (btnAddNode) btnAddNode.addEventListener('click', () => this.addNodeToActive());

    const btnDelNode = document.getElementById('btnFlowDelNode');
    if (btnDelNode) btnDelNode.addEventListener('click', () => this.dropNodeFromActive());

    const sliderNumEvents = ['opacity', 'glow', 'scale', 'speed', 'delay', 'endDelay', 'curvature', 'lineWidth'];
    sliderNumEvents.forEach((key) => {
      const cap = key.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + key.slice(1);
      const slider = document.getElementById('flow' + cap + 'Slider');
      const num = document.getElementById('flow' + cap + 'Num');
      if (slider && num) {
        slider.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
          num.value = e.target.value;
          this.updateActive(key, parseFloat(e.target.value));
          if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordDebounced();
        });
        num.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
          slider.value = e.target.value;
          this.updateActive(key, parseFloat(e.target.value));
          if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordDebounced();
        });
      }
    });

    ['type', 'color', 'showLine', 'hideOnDelay', 'route', 'lineStyle', 'arrow'].forEach((key) => {
      let elId = 'flow' + key.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + key.slice(1);
      if (key === 'color') elId += 'Picker';
      else if (['type', 'route', 'lineStyle', 'arrow'].includes(key)) elId += 'Select';

      const el = document.getElementById(elId);
      if (el) {
        if (key === 'color') {
          el.addEventListener('input', (e) => this.updateActive('color', e.target.value));
        }
        el.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
          this.updateActive(key, e.target.type === 'checkbox' ? e.target.checked : e.target.value);
          if (key === 'type') {
            const cRow = document.getElementById('customPayloadRow');
            if (cRow) cRow.style.display = e.target.value === 'custom' ? 'block' : 'none';
          }
          if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
        });
      }
    });

    const lblIn = document.getElementById('flowLabelInput');
    if (lblIn) {
      lblIn.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
        this.updateActive('label', e.target.value);
        if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordDebounced();
      });
    }

    const nodeSlider = document.getElementById('flowNodeSlider');
    if (nodeSlider) {
      nodeSlider.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
        this.activeNodeIdx = parseInt(e.target.value, 10);
        const nodeNum = document.getElementById('flowNodeNum');
        if (nodeNum) nodeNum.value = this.activeNodeIdx;
        this.updateUIOverlay();
      });
    }

    const customIn = document.getElementById('customPayloadInput');
    if (customIn) {
      customIn.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        const file = e.target.files[0];
        if (!file) return;
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = (ev) => {
          const dataUrl = ev.target.result;
          const img = new Image();
          img.onload = () => {
            if (this.activeId !== null) {
              const f = this.list.find((flow) => flow.id === this.activeId);
              if (f) {
                f.customB64 = dataUrl.split(',')[1];
                f.customMime = file.type;
                f.customW = img.naturalWidth;
                f.customH = img.naturalHeight;
                this.triggerAppRender();
                if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
              }
            }
          };
          img.src = dataUrl;
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
      });
    }
  },

  addFlow() {
    this.counter++;
    const newFlow = {
      id: this.counter,
      nodes: [{ x: 25, y: 50 }, { x: 75, y: 50 }],
      type: 'orb',
      route: 'curve',
      lineStyle: 'dashed',
      arrow: 'none',
      lineWidth: 2.5,
      label: '',
      color: '#00ffcc',
      showLine: true,
      curvature: 0,
      opacity: 1.0,
      glow: 20,
      scale: 1.0,
      speed: 2.0,
      delay: 0.0,
      endDelay: 0.0,
      hideOnDelay: true,
      customB64: null,
      customMime: null
    };
    this.list.push(newFlow);
    this.selectFlow(newFlow.id);
    this.triggerAppRender();
    if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
  },

  addNodeToActive() {
    const f = this.list.find((flow) => flow.id === this.activeId);
    if (!f) return;
    const lastNode = f.nodes[f.nodes.length - 1];
    f.nodes.push({ x: Math.min(95, lastNode.x + 5), y: Math.min(95, lastNode.y + 5) });
    this.activeNodeIdx = f.nodes.length - 1;
    this.refreshNodeSlider(f);
    this.updateUIOverlay();
    this.triggerAppRender();
    if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
  },

  dropNodeFromActive() {
    const f = this.list.find((flow) => flow.id === this.activeId);
    if (!f || f.nodes.length <= 2) {
      alert('A flow must have at least 2 waypoints (Start and End).');
      return;
    }
    f.nodes.splice(this.activeNodeIdx, 1);
    this.activeNodeIdx = Math.max(0, this.activeNodeIdx - 1);
    this.refreshNodeSlider(f);
    this.updateUIOverlay();
    this.triggerAppRender();
    if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
  },

  refreshNodeSlider(f) {
    const slider = document.getElementById('flowNodeSlider');
    const num = document.getElementById('flowNodeNum');
    if (slider && num) {
      slider.max = f.nodes.length - 1;
      slider.value = this.activeNodeIdx;
      num.value = this.activeNodeIdx;
    }
  },

  selectFlow(id) {
    if (window.DiagramNodes) window.DiagramNodes.deselectNode();
    if (window.Glows) window.Glows.deselectGlow();
    if (window.Ripples) window.Ripples.deselectRipple();

    this.activeId = id;
    this.activeNodeIdx = 0;
    this.renderList();
    document.body.classList.add('editing-flow');

    const f = this.list.find((flow) => flow.id === id);
    if (f) {
      const ed = document.getElementById('flowEditor');
      if (ed) ed.style.display = 'block';
      const lbl = document.getElementById('editingFlowLabel');
      if (lbl) lbl.innerText = 'Editing Connector / Flow ' + id;

      const tSel = document.getElementById('flowTypeSelect');
      if (tSel) tSel.value = f.type || 'orb';
      const rSel = document.getElementById('flowRouteSelect');
      if (rSel) rSel.value = f.route || 'curve';
      const lsSel = document.getElementById('flowLineStyleSelect');
      if (lsSel) lsSel.value = f.lineStyle || 'dashed';
      const arSel = document.getElementById('flowArrowSelect');
      if (arSel) arSel.value = f.arrow || 'none';
      const lIn = document.getElementById('flowLabelInput');
      if (lIn) lIn.value = f.label || '';
      const cPick = document.getElementById('flowColorPicker');
      if (cPick) cPick.value = f.color;
      const sLine = document.getElementById('flowShowLine');
      if (sLine) sLine.checked = f.showLine;
      const hDel = document.getElementById('flowHideOnDelay');
      if (hDel) hDel.checked = f.hideOnDelay !== false;
      const cRow = document.getElementById('customPayloadRow');
      if (cRow) cRow.style.display = f.type === 'custom' ? 'block' : 'none';

      this.refreshNodeSlider(f);

      ['opacity', 'glow', 'scale', 'speed', 'delay', 'endDelay', 'curvature', 'lineWidth'].forEach((key) => {
        const val = f[key] !== undefined ? f[key] : (key === 'curvature' ? 0 : (key === 'lineWidth' ? 2.5 : 1));
        const cap = key.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + key.slice(1);
        const sl = document.getElementById('flow' + cap + 'Slider');
        const nu = document.getElementById('flow' + cap + 'Num');
        if (sl) sl.value = val;
        if (nu) nu.value = val;
      });
      this.updateUIOverlay();
    }
  },

  deselectFlow() {
    this.activeId = null;
    this.renderList();
    document.body.classList.remove('editing-flow');
    const editor = document.getElementById('flowEditor');
    if (editor) editor.style.display = 'none';
    const ret = document.getElementById('dynamicFlowReticles');
    if (ret) ret.innerHTML = '';
    const path = document.getElementById('flowCurvePath');
    if (path) path.style.display = 'none';
  },

  updateActive(key, value) {
    if (this.activeId === null) return;
    const f = this.list.find((flow) => flow.id === this.activeId);
    if (f) {
      f[key] = value;
      this.renderList();
      this.triggerAppRender();
      this.updateUIOverlay();
    }
  },

  handleStageClick(x, y) {
    if (this.activeId === null) return false;
    const f = this.list.find((flow) => flow.id === this.activeId);
    if (f && f.nodes[this.activeNodeIdx]) {
      f.nodes[this.activeNodeIdx].x = x;
      f.nodes[this.activeNodeIdx].y = y;
      this.updateUIOverlay();

      const w = (window.Stage && window.Stage.canvasWidth) || 1280;
      const h = (window.Stage && window.Stage.canvasHeight) || 720;
      const newD = this.generatePathString(f.nodes, f.curvature || 0, w, h, f.route || 'curve');
      const livePath = document.getElementById('flowPath-' + f.id);
      if (livePath) {
        livePath.setAttribute('d', newD);
      } else {
        this.triggerAppRender();
      }
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  },

  generatePathString(nodes, curvature, width, height, routeMode = 'curve') {
    if (nodes.length < 2) return '';
    const px = (pct) => Number(((pct / 100) * width).toFixed(2));
    const py = (pct) => Number(((pct / 100) * height).toFixed(2));

    if (routeMode === 'ortho-hv' || routeMode === 'ortho-vh' || routeMode === 'ortho-mid') {
      let d = 'M ' + px(nodes[0].x) + ',' + py(nodes[0].y);
      for (let i = 0; i < nodes.length - 1; i++) {
        const x1 = px(nodes[i].x);
        const y1 = py(nodes[i].y);
        const x2 = px(nodes[i + 1].x);
        const y2 = py(nodes[i + 1].y);
        if (routeMode === 'ortho-hv') {
          d += ' L ' + x2 + ',' + y1 + ' L ' + x2 + ',' + y2;
        } else if (routeMode === 'ortho-vh') {
          d += ' L ' + x1 + ',' + y2 + ' L ' + x2 + ',' + y2;
        } else {
          const midX = Number(((x1 + x2) / 2).toFixed(2));
          d += ' L ' + midX + ',' + y1 + ' L ' + midX + ',' + y2 + ' L ' + x2 + ',' + y2;
        }
      }
      return d;
    }

    if (curvature === 0) {
      return 'M ' + px(nodes[0].x) + ',' + py(nodes[0].y) + ' ' + nodes.slice(1).map((n) => 'L ' + px(n.x) + ',' + py(n.y)).join(' ');
    }

    if (nodes.length === 2) {
      const p1 = nodes[0];
      const p2 = nodes[1];
      const dx = px(p2.x) - px(p1.x);
      const dy = py(p2.y) - py(p1.y);
      const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) || 1;
      const nx = -dy / dist;
      const ny = dx / dist;
      const bend = (curvature / 100) * (dist * 0.4);
      const cx = ((px(p1.x) + px(p2.x)) / 2 + nx * bend).toFixed(2);
      const cy = ((py(p1.y) + py(p2.y)) / 2 + ny * bend).toFixed(2);
      return 'M ' + px(p1.x) + ',' + py(p1.y) + ' Q ' + cx + ',' + cy + ' ' + px(p2.x) + ',' + py(p2.y);
    }

    let d = 'M ' + px(nodes[0].x) + ',' + py(nodes[0].y);
    const t = (curvature / 100) * 0.3;
    for (let i = 0; i < nodes.length - 1; i++) {
      const p0 = i === 0 ? nodes[0] : nodes[i - 1];
      const p1 = nodes[i];
      const p2 = nodes[i + 1];
      const p3 = i + 2 < nodes.length ? nodes[i + 2] : p2;
      const cp1x = (px(p1.x) + (px(p2.x) - px(p0.x)) * t).toFixed(2);
      const cp1y = (py(p1.y) + (py(p2.y) - py(p0.y)) * t).toFixed(2);
      const cp2x = (px(p2.x) - (px(p3.x) - px(p1.x)) * t).toFixed(2);
      const cp2y = (py(p2.y) - (py(p3.y) - py(p1.y)) * t).toFixed(2);
      d += ' C ' + cp1x + ',' + cp1y + ' ' + cp2x + ',' + cp2y + ' ' + px(p2.x) + ',' + py(p2.y);
    }
    return d;
  },

  getMidpoint(nodes, curvature, width, height) {
    if (!nodes || nodes.length < 2) return { x: width / 2, y: height / 2 };
    const px = (pct) => (pct / 100) * width;
    const py = (pct) => (pct / 100) * height;
    const midIdx = Math.floor((nodes.length - 1) / 2);
    const p1 = nodes[midIdx];
    const p2 = nodes[midIdx + 1];
    let mx = (px(p1.x) + px(p2.x)) / 2;
    let my = (py(p1.y) + py(p2.y)) / 2;

    if (nodes.length === 2 && curvature !== 0) {
      const dx = px(p2.x) - px(p1.x);
      const dy = py(p2.y) - py(p1.y);
      const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) || 1;
      const nx = -dy / dist;
      const ny = dx / dist;
      const bend = (curvature / 100) * (dist * 0.2);
      mx += nx * bend;
      my += ny * bend;
    }
    return { x: mx, y: my };
  },

  updateUIOverlay() {
    const f = this.list.find((flow) => flow.id === this.activeId);
    const container = document.getElementById('dynamicFlowReticles');
    const pathEl = document.getElementById('flowCurvePath');
    if (!f || !container || !pathEl) return;

    const w = (window.Stage && window.Stage.canvasWidth) || 1280;
    const h = (window.Stage && window.Stage.canvasHeight) || 720;

    if (f.nodes.length >= 2) {
      pathEl.style.display = 'block';
      pathEl.setAttribute('d', this.generatePathString(f.nodes, f.curvature, w, h, f.route || 'curve'));
      pathEl.setAttribute('stroke', f.color);
    } else {
      pathEl.style.display = 'none';
    }

    const existingReticles = container.querySelectorAll('[data-flow-node-idx]');
    if (existingReticles.length === f.nodes.length) {
      f.nodes.forEach((node, idx) => {
        const isActive = idx === this.activeNodeIdx;
        const el = existingReticles[idx];
        el.className = 'reticle' + (isActive ? ' active-reticle' : '');
        el.style.left = node.x + '%';
        el.style.top = node.y + '%';
        el.style.background = isActive ? (f.color + '4D') : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.65)';
        el.style.borderColor = f.color;
        el.style.zIndex = isActive ? '12' : '11';
      });
    } else {
      let html = '';
      f.nodes.forEach((node, idx) => {
        const isActive = idx === this.activeNodeIdx;
        html +=
          '<div class="reticle' + (isActive ? ' active-reticle' : '') + '" ' +
          'data-flow-node-idx="' + idx + '" ' +
          'style="left:' + node.x + '%; top:' + node.y + '%; pointer-events: auto; ' +
          'background:' + (isActive ? (f.color + '4D') : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.65)') + '; ' +
          'border-color:' + f.color + '; z-index:' + (isActive ? 12 : 11) + '; cursor: grab;">' +
          'N' + idx +
          '</div>';
      });
      container.innerHTML = html;
    }
  },

  removeFlow(id) {
    this.list = this.list.filter((f) => f.id !== id);
    if (this.activeId === id) this.deselectFlow();
    else this.renderList();
    this.triggerAppRender();
    if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
  },

  renderList() {
    const container = document.getElementById('flowListContainer');
    if (!container) return;
    if (this.list.length === 0) {
      container.innerHTML = '<div class="stage-hint" id="emptyFlowHint">No flows active.</div>';
      return;
    }
    container.innerHTML = '';
    this.list.forEach((f) => {
      const div = document.createElement('div');
      div.className = 'flow-list-item' + (this.activeId === f.id ? ' selected' : '');
      div.style.borderLeftColor = f.color;
      const tag = f.label ? this.escapeXML(f.label) : ('Flow ' + f.id + ' [' + f.nodes.length + ' Nodes]');
      div.innerHTML =
        '<span>' + tag + '</span>' +
        '<button type="button" style="background:transparent; border:none; color:#ff4444; font-weight:bold; cursor:pointer;" onclick="window.DataFlows.removeFlow(' + f.id + '); event.stopPropagation();">X</button>';
      div.onclick = () => this.selectFlow(f.id);
      container.appendChild(div);
    });
  },

  triggerAppRender() {
    if (typeof window.triggerAppRender === 'function') {
      window.triggerAppRender();
    } else {
      const durSlider = document.getElementById('durationSlider');
      if (durSlider) durSlider.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));
    }
  },

  injectIntoSVG(svgString, stateOrWidth, heightArg) {
    if (this.list.length === 0) return svgString;
    let width;
    let height;
    if (stateOrWidth && stateOrWidth.image) {
      width = stateOrWidth.image.width;
      height = stateOrWidth.image.height;
    } else {
      width = stateOrWidth;
      height = heightArg;
    }

    let defs = '';
    let elements = '';

    this.list.forEach((f) => {
      const routeMode = f.route || 'curve';
      const pathD = this.generatePathString(f.nodes, f.curvature || 0, width, height, routeMode);
      if (!pathD) return;

      const pathId = 'flowPath-' + f.id;
      const totalDur = f.speed + (f.endDelay || 0);
      const ratio = f.endDelay > 0 ? (f.speed / totalDur).toFixed(4) : '1';
      const lw = f.lineWidth !== undefined ? f.lineWidth : Math.max(1.5, 3 * f.scale);

      const arrowMode = f.arrow || 'none';
      let markerAttrs = '';
      if (arrowMode !== 'none') {
        const markerId = 'flowArrow-' + f.id;
        defs +=
          '<marker id="' + markerId + '" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="7" refY="5" markerWidth="6" markerHeight="6" orient="auto-start-reverse">' +
          '<path d="M 0 1.5 L 9 5 L 0 8.5 z" fill="' + f.color + '" />' +
          '</marker>';
        if (arrowMode === 'end' || arrowMode === 'both') markerAttrs += ' marker-end="url(#' + markerId + ')"';
        if (arrowMode === 'start' || arrowMode === 'both') markerAttrs += ' marker-start="url(#' + markerId + ')"';
      }

      if (f.showLine) {
        const lStyle = f.lineStyle || 'dashed';
        if (lStyle === 'solid') {
          elements += '<path id="' + pathId + '" d="' + pathD + '" fill="none" stroke="' + f.color + '" stroke-width="' + lw + '" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.8"' + markerAttrs + ' />';
        } else if (lStyle === 'dotted') {
          elements += '<path id="' + pathId + '" d="' + pathD + '" fill="none" stroke="' + f.color + '" stroke-width="' + lw + '" stroke-dasharray="' + lw + ',' + (lw * 2.2) + '" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.85"' + markerAttrs + ' />';
        } else if (lStyle === 'marching') {
          elements += '<path id="' + pathId + '" d="' + pathD + '" fill="none" stroke="' + f.color + '" stroke-width="' + lw + '" stroke-dasharray="10,6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.85"' + markerAttrs + '><animate attributeName="stroke-dashoffset" from="32" to="0" dur="1s" repeatCount="indefinite" /></path>';
        } else if (lStyle === 'double') {
          elements += '<path d="' + pathD + '" fill="none" stroke="' + f.color + '" stroke-width="' + (lw * 2.6) + '" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.75"' + markerAttrs + ' />';
          elements += '<path id="' + pathId + '" d="' + pathD + '" fill="none" stroke="#0d1117" stroke-width="' + (lw * 0.9) + '" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" />';
        } else {
          elements += '<path id="' + pathId + '" d="' + pathD + '" fill="none" stroke="' + f.color + '" stroke-width="' + lw + '" stroke-dasharray="8,6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" opacity="0.65"' + markerAttrs + ' />';
        }
      } else {
        elements += '<path id="' + pathId + '" d="' + pathD + '" fill="none" stroke="none" />';
      }

      if (f.label && f.label.trim() !== '') {
        const mid = this.getMidpoint(f.nodes, routeMode === 'curve' ? (f.curvature || 0) : 0, width, height);
        const cleanLabel = this.escapeXML(f.label.trim());
        const badgeW = Math.max(44, f.label.trim().length * 8.2 + 16);
        const badgeH = 22;
        elements +=
          '<g transform="translate(' + mid.x.toFixed(1) + ', ' + mid.y.toFixed(1) + ')" pointer-events="none">' +
          '<rect x="' + (-badgeW / 2).toFixed(1) + '" y="' + (-badgeH / 2).toFixed(1) + '" width="' + badgeW.toFixed(1) + '" height="' + badgeH + '" rx="5" fill="#0d1117" fill-opacity="0.9" stroke="' + f.color + '" stroke-width="1.2" />' +
          '<text x="0" y="4.5" fill="#e6edf3" font-family="monospace" font-size="12" font-weight="600" text-anchor="middle">' + cleanLabel + '</text>' +
          '</g>';
      }

      if (f.type === 'line-only') return;

      let payloadSVG = '';
      const sc = f.scale;

      if (f.type === 'custom' && f.customB64) {
        payloadSVG = '<image href="data:' + f.customMime + ';base64,' + f.customB64 + '" xlink:href="data:' + f.customMime + ';base64,' + f.customB64 + '" x="' + (-f.customW / 2) + '" y="' + (-f.customH / 2) + '" width="' + f.customW + '" height="' + f.customH + '" transform="scale(' + sc + ')" />';
      } else if (f.type === 'orb') {
        const glowFilter = f.glow > 0 ? ('filter="url(#flowBlur-' + f.id + ')"') : '';
        if (f.glow > 0) defs += '<filter id="flowBlur-' + f.id + '" filterUnits="userSpaceOnUse" x="-20%" y="-20%" width="140%" height="140%"><feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="' + (f.glow / 4).toFixed(2) + '" /></filter>';
        payloadSVG = '<circle r="' + (8 * sc).toFixed(2) + '" fill="' + f.color + '" />';
        if (f.glow > 0) payloadSVG += '<circle r="' + (12 * sc).toFixed(2) + '" fill="' + f.color + '" opacity="0.55" ' + glowFilter + ' />';
      } else if (f.type === 'packet') {
        payloadSVG = '<rect x="' + (-8 * sc).toFixed(2) + '" y="' + (-8 * sc).toFixed(2) + '" width="' + (16 * sc).toFixed(2) + '" height="' + (16 * sc).toFixed(2) + '" fill="' + f.color + '" rx="2" />';
      } else if (f.type === 'envelope') {
        payloadSVG = '<g transform="scale(' + sc + ')"><path d="M-12,-8 L12,-8 L12,8 L-12,8 Z M-12,-8 L0,2 L12,-8" fill="#0d1117" stroke="' + f.color + '" stroke-width="2" stroke-linejoin="round"/></g>';
      } else if (f.type === 'database') {
        payloadSVG = '<g transform="scale(' + sc + ')"><ellipse cx="0" cy="-6" rx="10" ry="4" fill="#0d1117" stroke="' + f.color + '" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M-10,-6 L-10,6 A10,4 0 0,0 10,6 L10,-6" fill="#0d1117" stroke="' + f.color + '" stroke-width="2"/><path d="M-10,0 A10,4 0 0,0 10,0" fill="none" stroke="' + f.color + '" stroke-width="2"/></g>';
      } else if (f.type === 'server') {
        payloadSVG = '<g transform="scale(' + sc + ')"><rect x="-10" y="-12" width="20" height="24" fill="#0d1117" stroke="' + f.color + '" stroke-width="2" rx="2"/><line x1="-10" y1="0" x2="10" y2="0" stroke="' + f.color + '" stroke-width="2"/><circle cx="-5" cy="-6" r="1.5" fill="' + f.color + '"/><circle cx="-5" cy="6" r="1.5" fill="' + f.color + '"/></g>';
      } else if (f.type === 'cloud') {
        payloadSVG = '<g transform="scale(' + sc + ')"><path d="M-4,4 A6,6 0 0,1 -6,-6 A7,7 0 0,1 6,-6 A5,5 0 0,1 4,4 Z" fill="#0d1117" stroke="' + f.color + '" stroke-width="2" stroke-linejoin="round"/></g>';
      } else if (f.type === 'lock') {
        payloadSVG = '<g transform="scale(' + sc + ')"><rect x="-7" y="-2" width="14" height="10" fill="#0d1117" stroke="' + f.color + '" stroke-width="2" rx="1"/><path d="M-4,-2 L-4,-6 A4,4 0 0,1 4,-6 L4,-2" fill="none" stroke="' + f.color + '" stroke-width="2"/></g>';
      } else if (f.type === 'warning') {
        payloadSVG = '<g transform="scale(' + sc + ')"><path d="M0,-12 L12,8 L-12,8 Z" fill="#0d1117" stroke="' + f.color + '" stroke-width="2" stroke-linejoin="round"/><line x1="0" y1="-4" x2="0" y2="2" stroke="' + f.color + '" stroke-width="2"/><circle cx="0" cy="5" r="1" fill="' + f.color + '"/></g>';
      }

      let visibilityControl = '';
      let setAnim = '';
      if (f.delay > 0) {
        visibilityControl = 'visibility="hidden"';
        setAnim = '<set attributeName="visibility" to="visible" begin="' + f.delay + 's" fill="freeze" />';
      }

      let motionAnim = '';
      let opAnim = '';
      const holdVal = f.hideOnDelay !== false ? '0' : f.opacity;

      if (f.endDelay > 0) {
        const fadeInEnd = (parseFloat(ratio) * 0.08).toFixed(4);
        const fadeOutStart = (parseFloat(ratio) * 0.92).toFixed(4);
        motionAnim = '<animateMotion calcMode="linear" keyPoints="0; 1; 1" keyTimes="0; ' + ratio + '; 1" dur="' + totalDur + 's" begin="' + f.delay + 's" repeatCount="indefinite" rotate="auto"><mpath href="#' + pathId + '" xlink:href="#' + pathId + '"/></animateMotion>';
        opAnim = '<animate attributeName="opacity" values="0; ' + f.opacity + '; ' + f.opacity + '; ' + holdVal + '; ' + holdVal + '" keyTimes="0; ' + fadeInEnd + '; ' + fadeOutStart + '; ' + ratio + '; 1" dur="' + totalDur + 's" begin="' + f.delay + 's" repeatCount="indefinite" />';
      } else {
        motionAnim = '<animateMotion calcMode="linear" keyPoints="0; 1" keyTimes="0; 1" dur="' + f.speed + 's" begin="' + f.delay + 's" repeatCount="indefinite" rotate="auto"><mpath href="#' + pathId + '" xlink:href="#' + pathId + '"/></animateMotion>';
        opAnim = '<animate attributeName="opacity" values="0; ' + f.opacity + '; ' + f.opacity + '; 0" keyTimes="0; 0.08; 0.92; 1" dur="' + f.speed + 's" begin="' + f.delay + 's" repeatCount="indefinite" />';
      }

      elements += '<g ' + visibilityControl + ' pointer-events="none">' + setAnim + '<g opacity="0">' + opAnim + motionAnim + payloadSVG + '</g></g>';
    });

    return svgString.replace('<!-- INJECT_CAMERA_RIG_ELEMENTS -->', '\n<defs>\n' + defs + '\n</defs>\n<g id="flows-layer">\n' + elements + '\n</g>\n<!-- INJECT_CAMERA_RIG_ELEMENTS -->');
  }
