let cachedPreviewBlob = null;
let cachedPreviewBlobUrl = null;
let cachedFinalWebpBlobUrl = null;
let cachedFinalApngBlobUrl = null;
let cachedFinalSignature = null;
let nativeAspectRatio = 16 / 9;
let modalEventsBound = false;

let activeAbortController = null;
let activeJobId = null;
let progressPollTimer = null;

const PREVIEW_FPS = 24;

function setElVal(id, val) {
  const el = document.getElementById(id);
  if (el) el.value = val;
}

function setElChecked(id, checked) {
  const el = document.getElementById(id);
  if (el) el.checked = !!checked;
}

function getElChecked(id, fallback = true) {
  const el = document.getElementById(id);
  return el ? !!el.checked : fallback;
}

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  bindModalEvents();
  document.getElementById('btnOpenBitmapModal')?.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    openExportModal();
  });
});

function bindModalEvents() {
  if (modalEventsBound) return;
  if (!document.getElementById('exportModal')) return;
  modalEventsBound = true;

  const widthInput = document.getElementById('expWidth');
  const heightInput = document.getElementById('expHeight');
  const lockAspect = document.getElementById('expLockAspect');
  const formatSelect = document.getElementById('expFormat');

  widthInput?.addEventListener('input', () => {
    if (lockAspect && lockAspect.checked && nativeAspectRatio > 0) {
      const w = parseInt(widthInput.value, 10);
      if (!isNaN(w) && w > 0 && heightInput) {
        heightInput.value = Math.max(2, Math.round(w / nativeAspectRatio));
      }
    }
  });

  heightInput?.addEventListener('input', () => {
    if (lockAspect && lockAspect.checked && nativeAspectRatio > 0) {
      const h = parseInt(heightInput.value, 10);
      if (!isNaN(h) && h > 0 && widthInput) {
        widthInput.value = Math.max(2, Math.round(h * nativeAspectRatio));
      }
    }
  });

  formatSelect?.addEventListener('change', updateDownloadButtonLabel);
}

function updateDownloadButtonLabel() {
  const btnDown = document.getElementById('btnDownloadFinal');
  const formatSelect = document.getElementById('expFormat');
  if (!btnDown || !formatSelect) return;

  if (formatSelect.value === 'both') {
    btnDown.innerText = 'Download Both (WebP + APNG)';
  } else if (formatSelect.value === 'apng') {
    btnDown.innerText = 'Download Final APNG';
  } else {
    btnDown.innerText = 'Download Final WebP';
  }
}

function openExportModal(modeArg) {
  if (modeArg === 'still' && window.StillExportStudio) {
    window.StillExportStudio.open();
    return;
  }

  if (!window.currentRenderState && typeof window.triggerAppRender === 'function') {
    window.triggerAppRender();
  }

  if (!window.currentRenderState || !window.currentRenderState.image) {
    alert('Please initialize a canvas or load an image first.');
    return;
  }

  const st = window.currentRenderState;
  const w = st.image.width || 1280;
  const h = st.image.height || 720;
  nativeAspectRatio = w / h;

  setElVal('expWidth', w);
  setElVal('expHeight', h);
  setElVal('expDuration', st.duration || 7);
  setElVal('expLoop', st.loop || 'loop');
  setElVal('expFps', '24');

  if (st.image.filename) {
    const cleanBase = st.image.filename.includes('.') ? st.image.filename.split('.')[0] : st.image.filename;
    setElVal('expFilename', cleanBase + '_animated');
  }

  const hasNodes = window.DiagramNodes ? window.DiagramNodes.list.length > 0 : false;
  const hasFlows = window.DataFlows ? window.DataFlows.list.length > 0 : false;
  const hasGlows = window.Glows ? window.Glows.list.length > 0 : false;
  const hasRipples = window.Ripples ? window.Ripples.list.length > 0 : false;

  setElChecked('expEnableNodes', hasNodes);
  setElChecked('expEnableFlows', hasFlows);
  setElChecked('expEnableGlows', hasGlows);
  setElChecked('expEnableRipples', hasRipples);
  setElChecked('expEnableCamera', st.camera.motion !== 'static');
  setElChecked('expEnableOptical', st.optical.type !== 'none');
  setElChecked('expEnableFrame', !!st.overlays.frame);

  updateDownloadButtonLabel();
  const modal = document.getElementById('exportModal');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'flex';
}

function closeExportModal() {
  if (activeAbortController) {
    cancelCurrentRender();
  }
  const modal = document.getElementById('exportModal');
  if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
}

function setExportResolution(preset) {
  if (!window.currentRenderState || !window.currentRenderState.image) return;
  const nw = window.currentRenderState.image.width || 1280;
  const nh = window.currentRenderState.image.height || 720;
  nativeAspectRatio = nw / nh;

  if (preset === 'native') {
    setElVal('expWidth', nw);
    setElVal('expHeight', nh);
  } else if (preset === 'half') {
    setElVal('expWidth', Math.max(64, Math.round(nw / 2)));
    setElVal('expHeight', Math.max(64, Math.round(nh / 2)));
  } else if (typeof preset === 'number') {
    setElVal('expWidth', preset);
    setElVal('expHeight', Math.max(64, Math.round(preset / nativeAspectRatio)));
  }
}

function applyExportProfile(profile) {
  if (profile === 'camera-once') {
    setElVal('expLoop', 'once');
    setElChecked('expEnableCamera', true);
    setElChecked('expEnableOptical', true);
    setElChecked('expEnableNodes', true);
    setElChecked('expEnableFlows', false);
    setElChecked('expEnableGlows', false);
    setElChecked('expEnableRipples', false);
  } else if (profile === 'overlays-loop') {
    setElVal('expLoop', 'loop');
    setElChecked('expEnableCamera', false);
    setElChecked('expEnableOptical', false);
    setElChecked('expEnableNodes', true);
    setElChecked('expEnableFlows', true);
    setElChecked('expEnableGlows', true);
    setElChecked('expEnableRipples', true);
  } else if (profile === 'all') {
    setElChecked('expEnableNodes', true);
    setElChecked('expEnableFlows', true);
    setElChecked('expEnableGlows', true);
    setElChecked('expEnableRipples', true);
    setElChecked('expEnableCamera', true);
    setElChecked('expEnableOptical', true);
    setElChecked('expEnableFrame', true);
  }
}

function getFittedPreviewDimensions(targetWidth, targetHeight) {
  const container = document.getElementById('previewContainer');
  let maxW = 680;
  let maxH = 440;

  if (container) {
    const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (rect.width > 64 && rect.height > 64) {
      maxW = Math.max(240, Math.floor(rect.width - 24));
      maxH = Math.max(180, Math.floor(rect.height - 24));
    }
  }

  const scaleW = maxW / targetWidth;
  const scaleH = maxH / targetHeight;
  const scale = Math.min(1.0, scaleW, scaleH);

  const w = Math.max(64, Math.round((targetWidth * scale) / 2) * 2);
  const h = Math.max(64, Math.round((targetHeight * scale) / 2) * 2);
  return { width: w, height: h };
}

function buildExportPayload(forPreview = false, jobId = '') {
  const payloadState = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(window.currentRenderState));

  const fullWidth = Math.max(64, parseInt(document.getElementById('expWidth')?.value, 10) || payloadState.image.width);
  const fullHeight = Math.max(64, parseInt(document.getElementById('expHeight')?.value, 10) || payloadState.image.height);
  const targetDuration = Math.max(0.5, parseFloat(document.getElementById('expDuration')?.value) || payloadState.duration || 7);
  const targetLoop = document.getElementById('expLoop')?.value || 'once';
  const selectedFormat = document.getElementById('expFormat')?.value || 'webp';
  const selectedFps = parseInt(document.getElementById('expFps')?.value, 10) || 24;

  let renderWidth = fullWidth;
  let renderHeight = fullHeight;
  let renderFps = selectedFps;
  let renderFormat = selectedFormat;

  if (forPreview) {
    const fitted = getFittedPreviewDimensions(fullWidth, fullHeight);
    renderWidth = fitted.width;
    renderHeight = fitted.height;
    renderFps = PREVIEW_FPS;
    if (renderFormat === 'both') {
      renderFormat = 'webp';
    }
  }

  payloadState.duration = targetDuration;
  payloadState.loop = targetLoop;

  const enableNodes = getElChecked('expEnableNodes', true);
  const enableFlows = getElChecked('expEnableFlows', true);
  const enableGlows = getElChecked('expEnableGlows', true);
  const enableRipples = getElChecked('expEnableRipples', true);
  const enableCamera = getElChecked('expEnableCamera', true);
  const enableOptical = getElChecked('expEnableOptical', true);
  const enableFrame = getElChecked('expEnableFrame', true);

  if (!enableCamera) payloadState.camera.motion = 'static';
  if (!enableOptical) payloadState.optical.type = 'none';
  if (!enableFrame) payloadState.overlays.frame = false;

  const overrides = {
    includeNodes: enableNodes,
    includeFlows: enableFlows,
    includeGlows: enableGlows,
    includeRipples: enableRipples
  };

  const rawSvg = window.buildFullSVG(payloadState, overrides);
  const svgMarkup = window.serializeSVGForExport(rawSvg);

  return {
    jobId: jobId,
    format: renderFormat,
    duration: targetDuration,
    fps: renderFps,
    width: renderWidth,
    height: renderHeight,
    svgMarkup: svgMarkup,
    state: payloadState
  };
}

function startProgressPolling(jobId, totalFramesExpected) {
  const overlay = document.getElementById('renderProgressOverlay');
  const phaseText = document.getElementById('progressPhaseText');
  const barFill = document.getElementById('progressBarFill');
  const frameCount = document.getElementById('progressFrameCount');
  const pctText = document.getElementById('progressPercent');

  if (overlay) overlay.style.display = 'flex';
  if (phaseText) phaseText.innerText = 'Allocating animation frame buffers...';
  if (barFill) barFill.style.width = '0%';
  if (frameCount) frameCount.innerText = 'Frame 0 / ' + totalFramesExpected;
  if (pctText) pctText.innerText = '0%';

  if (progressPollTimer) clearInterval(progressPollTimer);

  progressPollTimer = setInterval(async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/export/progress?jobId=' + encodeURIComponent(jobId));
      if (!res.ok) return;
      const data = await res.json();
      const tot = data.totalFrames || totalFramesExpected;
      const cur = data.currentFrame || 0;
      const pct = data.percent || 0;

      if (phaseText && data.phase) phaseText.innerText = data.phase;
      if (barFill) barFill.style.width = pct + '%';
      if (frameCount) frameCount.innerText = 'Frame ' + cur + ' / ' + tot;
      if (pctText) pctText.innerText = pct + '%';
    } catch (_) {}
  }, 200);
}

function stopProgressPolling() {
  if (progressPollTimer) {
    clearInterval(progressPollTimer);
    progressPollTimer = null;
  }
  const overlay = document.getElementById('renderProgressOverlay');
  if (overlay) overlay.style.display = 'none';
}

async function cancelCurrentRender() {
  const jobIdToCancel = activeJobId;
  if (activeAbortController) {
    activeAbortController.abort();
    activeAbortController = null;
  }
  stopProgressPolling();

  if (jobIdToCancel) {
    activeJobId = null;
    try {
      await fetch('/api/export/cancel?jobId=' + encodeURIComponent(jobIdToCancel), { method: 'POST' });
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  const placeholder = document.getElementById('previewPlaceholder');
  const previewImg = document.getElementById('previewImage');
  const previewMeta = document.getElementById('previewMeta');

  if (cachedPreviewBlobUrl && previewImg) {
    previewImg.style.display = 'block';
    if (placeholder) placeholder.style.display = 'none';
  } else if (placeholder) {
    placeholder.innerText = 'Render canceled by user.';
    placeholder.style.display = 'block';
  }
  if (previewMeta) {
    previewMeta.innerText = 'Render aborted.';
  }
}

function setRenderButtonsState(isRendering) {
  const btnPrev = document.getElementById('btnGenPreview');
  const btnReplay = document.getElementById('btnReplayPreview');
  const btnCancel = document.getElementById('btnCancelRender');
  const btnDown = document.getElementById('btnDownloadFinal');

  if (btnPrev) btnPrev.disabled = isRendering;
  if (btnDown) btnDown.disabled = isRendering;
  if (btnReplay) btnReplay.disabled = isRendering || !cachedPreviewBlob;
  if (btnCancel) btnCancel.style.display = isRendering ? 'inline-block' : 'none';
}

async function requestBitmapRender(payload, signal) {
  const response = await fetch('/api/export', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    signal: signal
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    const errText = await response.text();
    throw new Error(errText || 'Server failed to render');
  }

  // Matches internal/web/server.go binary bundle streaming endpoint (/api/export/bundle)
  if (payload.format === 'both') {
    const data = await response.json();
    if (data.bundleId) {
      const bId = encodeURIComponent(data.bundleId);
      const [webpRes, apngRes] = await Promise.all([
        fetch('/api/export/bundle?bundleId=' + bId + '&fmt=webp', { signal: signal }),
        fetch('/api/export/bundle?bundleId=' + bId + '&fmt=apng', { signal: signal })
      ]);
      if (!webpRes.ok || !apngRes.ok) {
        throw new Error('Failed to stream rendered bundle files from server');
      }
      const webpBlob = await webpRes.blob();
      const apngBlob = await apngRes.blob();
      return { mode: 'both', webpBlob: webpBlob, apngBlob: apngBlob };
    }

    // Legacy fallback if base64 fields are returned
    const webpBlob = await (await fetch('data:image/webp;base64,' + data.webpB64)).blob();
    const apngBlob = await (await fetch('data:image/png;base64,' + data.apngB64)).blob();
    return { mode: 'both', webpBlob: webpBlob, apngBlob: apngBlob };
  }

  const singleBlob = await response.blob();
  return { mode: 'single', blob: singleBlob };
}

async function generatePreview() {
  const placeholder = document.getElementById('previewPlaceholder');
  const previewImg = document.getElementById('previewImage');
  const previewMeta = document.getElementById('previewMeta');

  activeJobId = 'job-' + Date.now() + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7);
  activeAbortController = new AbortController();

  const payload = buildExportPayload(true, activeJobId);
  const totalFrames = Math.max(1, Math.round(payload.duration * payload.fps * (payload.state.loop === 'pingpong' ? 2 : 1)));

  if (placeholder) placeholder.style.display = 'none';
  if (previewImg) previewImg.style.display = 'none';
  setRenderButtonsState(true);
  startProgressPolling(activeJobId, totalFrames);

  if (cachedPreviewBlobUrl) {
    URL.revokeObjectURL(cachedPreviewBlobUrl);
    cachedPreviewBlobUrl = null;
  }
  cachedPreviewBlob = null;

  const startTime = performance.now();

  try {
    const result = await requestBitmapRender(payload, activeAbortController.signal);
    const blob = result.blob;
    cachedPreviewBlob = blob;
    cachedPreviewBlobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

    stopProgressPolling();
    if (previewImg) {
      previewImg.src = cachedPreviewBlobUrl;
      previewImg.style.display = 'block';
    }

    const elapsed = ((performance.now() - startTime) / 1000).toFixed(1);
    const sizeKB = (blob.size / 1024).toFixed(1);
    if (previewMeta) {
      previewMeta.innerText = 'Preview: ' + payload.format.toUpperCase() + ' • ' + payload.width + '×' + payload.height + 'px (' + totalFrames + ' frames @ ' + PREVIEW_FPS + ' FPS) • ' + sizeKB + ' KB (' + elapsed + 's)';
    }
  } catch (err) {
    stopProgressPolling();
    if (placeholder) {
      if (err.name === 'AbortError') {
        placeholder.innerText = 'Render canceled.';
      } else {
        placeholder.innerText = 'Preview failed: ' + err.message;
        if (previewMeta) previewMeta.innerText = 'Render error.';
      }
      placeholder.style.display = 'block';
    }
  } finally {
    activeAbortController = null;
    activeJobId = null;
    setRenderButtonsState(false);
  }
}

function replayPreview() {
  const previewImg = document.getElementById('previewImage');
  if (!previewImg || !cachedPreviewBlob) return;

  if (cachedPreviewBlobUrl) {
    URL.revokeObjectURL(cachedPreviewBlobUrl);
  }

  cachedPreviewBlobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(cachedPreviewBlob);
  previewImg.style.display = 'none';
  previewImg.src = '';
  void previewImg.offsetWidth;
  previewImg.src = cachedPreviewBlobUrl;
  previewImg.style.display = 'block';
}

function triggerFileDownload(url, filename) {
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = filename;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  document.body.removeChild(a);
}

async function downloadFinal() {
  const btnDown = document.getElementById('btnDownloadFinal');
  const previewMeta = document.getElementById('previewMeta');
  const previewImg = document.getElementById('previewImage');
  const placeholder = document.getElementById('previewPlaceholder');
  const baseFilename = (document.getElementById('expFilename')?.value || 'animation_export').replace(/\.(webp|png|apng)$/i, '');

  const signatureCheckPayload = buildExportPayload(false, '');
  const currentSig = JSON.stringify(signatureCheckPayload);

  try {
    const isCacheValid = cachedFinalSignature === currentSig &&
      ((signatureCheckPayload.format === 'both' && cachedFinalWebpBlobUrl && cachedFinalApngBlobUrl) ||
       (signatureCheckPayload.format === 'webp' && cachedFinalWebpBlobUrl) ||
       (signatureCheckPayload.format === 'apng' && cachedFinalApngBlobUrl));

    if (!isCacheValid) {
      activeJobId = 'job-' + Date.now() + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 7);
      activeAbortController = new AbortController();

      const payload = buildExportPayload(false, activeJobId);
      const totalFrames = Math.max(1, Math.round(payload.duration * payload.fps * (payload.state.loop === 'pingpong' ? 2 : 1)));

      if (btnDown) btnDown.innerText = 'Rendering (' + payload.width + '×' + payload.height + ')...';
      if (placeholder) placeholder.style.display = 'none';
      if (previewImg) previewImg.style.display = 'none';
      setRenderButtonsState(true);
      startProgressPolling(activeJobId, totalFrames);

      const startTime = performance.now();
      const result = await requestBitmapRender(payload, activeAbortController.signal);
      stopProgressPolling();

      if (cachedFinalWebpBlobUrl) { URL.revokeObjectURL(cachedFinalWebpBlobUrl); cachedFinalWebpBlobUrl = null; }
      if (cachedFinalApngBlobUrl) { URL.revokeObjectURL(cachedFinalApngBlobUrl); cachedFinalApngBlobUrl = null; }
      if (cachedPreviewBlobUrl) { URL.revokeObjectURL(cachedPreviewBlobUrl); cachedPreviewBlobUrl = null; }

      const elapsed = ((performance.now() - startTime) / 1000).toFixed(1);

      if (result.mode === 'both') {
        cachedFinalWebpBlobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(result.webpBlob);
        cachedFinalApngBlobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(result.apngBlob);
        cachedPreviewBlob = result.webpBlob;
        cachedPreviewBlobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(result.webpBlob);

        const webpKB = (result.webpBlob.size / 1024).toFixed(1);
        const apngKB = (result.apngBlob.size / 1024).toFixed(1);
        if (previewMeta) {
          previewMeta.innerText = 'Final Render: WebP (' + webpKB + ' KB) + APNG (' + apngKB + ' KB) • ' + payload.width + '×' + payload.height + 'px • ' + payload.fps + ' FPS (' + elapsed + 's)';
        }
      } else {
        const singleUrl = URL.createObjectURL(result.blob);
        if (payload.format === 'webp') {
          cachedFinalWebpBlobUrl = singleUrl;
        } else {
          cachedFinalApngBlobUrl = singleUrl;
        }
        cachedPreviewBlob = result.blob;
        cachedPreviewBlobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(result.blob);

        const sizeKB = (result.blob.size / 1024).toFixed(1);
        if (previewMeta) {
          previewMeta.innerText = 'Final Render: ' + payload.format.toUpperCase() + ' • ' + payload.width + '×' + payload.height + 'px • ' + payload.fps + ' FPS • ' + sizeKB + ' KB (' + elapsed + 's)';
        }
      }

      cachedFinalSignature = currentSig;

      if (previewImg) {
        previewImg.src = cachedPreviewBlobUrl;
        previewImg.style.display = 'block';
      }
    }

    if (signatureCheckPayload.format === 'both') {
      triggerFileDownload(cachedFinalWebpBlobUrl, baseFilename + '.webp');
      setTimeout(() => {
        triggerFileDownload(cachedFinalApngBlobUrl, baseFilename + '.png');
      }, 250);
    } else if (signatureCheckPayload.format === 'webp') {
      triggerFileDownload(cachedFinalWebpBlobUrl, baseFilename + '.webp');
    } else {
      triggerFileDownload(cachedFinalApngBlobUrl, baseFilename + '.png');
    }
  } catch (err) {
    stopProgressPolling();
    if (err.name === 'AbortError') {
      if (placeholder) {
        placeholder.innerText = 'Render canceled.';
        placeholder.style.display = 'block';
      }
    } else {
      alert('Export failed: ' + err.message);
    }
  } finally {
    activeAbortController = null;
    activeJobId = null;
    updateDownloadButtonLabel();
    setRenderButtonsState(false);
  }
}

=== ./internal/web/static/js/effects.js ===
window.Effects = {
  buildPhotoElement(imgInfo, w, h, extraAttrs = '') {
    const imgSrc = imgInfo.b64
      ? `data:${imgInfo.mime || 'image/jpeg'};base64,${imgInfo.b64}`
      : (imgInfo.src || imgInfo.dataUrl || imgInfo.dataURL || imgInfo.data || '');

    const fitMode = imgInfo.photoFit || 'match';
    const natW = imgInfo.naturalWidth || w;
    const natH = imgInfo.naturalHeight || h;

    if (fitMode === 'contain') {
      return `<image href="${imgSrc}" xlink:href="${imgSrc}" x="0" y="0" width="${w}" height="${h}" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" ${extraAttrs} />`;
    }
    if (fitMode === 'stretch') {
      return `<image href="${imgSrc}" xlink:href="${imgSrc}" x="0" y="0" width="${w}" height="${h}" preserveAspectRatio="none" ${extraAttrs} />`;
    }
    if (fitMode === 'cover') {
      const cropZoom = Math.max(1.0, (imgInfo.cropZoom || 100) / 100);
      const cropX = imgInfo.cropX !== undefined ? imgInfo.cropX : 50;
      const cropY = imgInfo.cropY !== undefined ? imgInfo.cropY : 50;

      const baseScale = Math.max(w / natW, h / natH) * cropZoom;
      const renderW = natW * baseScale;
      const renderH = natH * baseScale;
      const overflowX = Math.max(0, renderW - w);
      const overflowY = Math.max(0, renderH - h);
      const offsetX = -(overflowX * (cropX / 100));
      const offsetY = -(overflowY * (cropY / 100));

      return `<image href="${imgSrc}" xlink:href="${imgSrc}" x="${offsetX.toFixed(2)}" y="${offsetY.toFixed(2)}" width="${renderW.toFixed(2)}" height="${renderH.toFixed(2)}" preserveAspectRatio="none" ${extraAttrs} />`;
    }

    return `<image href="${imgSrc}" xlink:href="${imgSrc}" x="0" y="0" width="${w}" height="${h}" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice" ${extraAttrs} />`;
  },

  build(state) {
    if (!state.image) return '';

    const w = state.image.width || 1280;
    const h = state.image.height || 720;
    const dur = state.duration || 7;
    const loop = state.loop || 'once';
    const c = state.camera || {};
    const opt = state.optical || {};

    const isBlank = !!state.image.isBlankCanvas;

    const calcTransform = (zx, zy, zz, rollDeg = 0) => {
      const safeZoom = Math.max(1.0, zz || 1.0);
      const vw = w / safeZoom;
      const vh = h / safeZoom;

      let vx = ((zx !== undefined ? zx : 50) / 100) * w - (vw / 2);
      let vy = ((zy !== undefined ? zy : 50) / 100) * h - (vh / 2);

      if (vx < 0) vx = 0;
      if (vy < 0) vy = 0;
      if (vx + vw > w) vx = w - vw;
      if (vy + vh > h) vy = h - vh;

      const tx = -vx * safeZoom;
      const ty = -vy * safeZoom;
      const rot = rollDeg !== 0 ? ` rotateZ(${rollDeg.toFixed(3)}deg)` : '';
      return `translate3d(${tx.toFixed(3)}px, ${ty.toFixed(3)}px, 0px)${rot} scale(${safeZoom.toFixed(4)})`;
    };

    const tStart = calcTransform(c.startX, c.startY, c.startZoom);
    const tEnd = calcTransform(c.endX, c.endY, c.endZoom);

    let cameraCSS = '';
    if (c.motion === 'static') {
      cameraCSS = `
        .camera-rig {
          transform-origin: 0px 0px;
          transform: ${tStart};
        }`;
    } else if (c.motion === 'perspective-tilt') {
      const pitch = c.tiltX || 0;
      const yaw = c.tiltY || 0;
      cameraCSS = `
        .camera-stage-3d {
          transform-origin: ${w / 2}px ${h / 2}px;
          transform: perspective(1000px) rotateX(${pitch}deg) rotateY(${yaw}deg);
        }
        .camera-rig {
          transform-origin: 0px 0px;
          transform: ${tStart};
        }`;
    } else if (c.motion === 'ken-burns') {
      let animTiming = `${dur}s cubic-bezier(0.37, 0, 0.63, 1) 1 normal forwards`;
      if (loop === 'pingpong') {
        animTiming = `${dur}s cubic-bezier(0.42, 0, 0.58, 1) infinite alternate`;
      } else if (loop === 'loop') {
        animTiming = `${dur}s cubic-bezier(0.37, 0, 0.63, 1) infinite normal`;
      }
      cameraCSS = `
        @keyframes camKenBurns {
          0%   { transform: ${tStart}; }
          100% { transform: ${tEnd}; }
        }
        .camera-rig {
          transform-origin: 0px 0px;
          will-change: transform;
          animation: camKenBurns ${animTiming};
        }`;
    } else if (c.motion === 'handheld-drift') {
      const sway = c.sway !== undefined ? c.sway : 1.4;
      const breathe = (c.breathe !== undefined ? c.breathe : 1.8) / 100.0;
      const baseZoom = Math.max(1.06, c.startZoom || 1.1);

      const k0 = calcTransform(c.startX, c.startY, baseZoom, 0.0);
      const k1 = calcTransform(c.startX + sway * 0.50, c.startY - sway * 0.35, baseZoom * (1.0 + breathe * 0.35), 0.18);
      const k2 = calcTransform(c.startX + sway * 0.70, c.startY + sway * 0.15, baseZoom * (1.0 + breathe * 0.75), -0.12);
      const k3 = calcTransform(c.startX + sway * 0.25, c.startY + sway * 0.55, baseZoom * (1.0 + breathe * 0.45), -0.28);
      const k4 = calcTransform(c.startX - sway * 0.45, c.startY + sway * 0.30, baseZoom * (1.0 + breathe * 0.90), 0.15);
      const k5 = calcTransform(c.startX - sway * 0.65, c.startY - sway * 0.25, baseZoom * (1.0 + breathe * 0.50), 0.25);
      const k6 = calcTransform(c.startX - sway * 0.30, c.startY - sway * 0.55, baseZoom * (1.0 + breathe * 0.20), -0.18);
      const k7 = calcTransform(c.startX + sway * 0.20, c.startY - sway * 0.25, baseZoom * (1.0 + breathe * 0.10), -0.08);

      cameraCSS = `
        @keyframes camHandheld {
          0%    { transform: ${k0}; animation-timing-function: ease-in-out; }
          12.5% { transform: ${k1}; animation-timing-function: ease-in-out; }
          25%   { transform: ${k2}; animation-timing-function: ease-in-out; }
          37.5% { transform: ${k3}; animation-timing-function: ease-in-out; }
          50%   { transform: ${k4}; animation-timing-function: ease-in-out; }
          62.5% { transform: ${k5}; animation-timing-function: ease-in-out; }
          75%   { transform: ${k6}; animation-timing-function: ease-in-out; }
          87.5% { transform: ${k7}; animation-timing-function: ease-in-out; }
          100%  { transform: ${k0}; }
        }
        .camera-rig {
          transform-origin: 0px 0px;
          will-change: transform;
          animation: camHandheld ${dur}s linear infinite normal;
        }`;
    }

    let filterDefs = `<clipPath id="viewportClip"><rect x="0" y="0" width="${w}" height="${h}" /></clipPath>`;
    let filterAttr = '';
    let extraOverlays = '';

    const bgColor = c.bgColor || '#0d1117';
    const bg = c.bgTransparent ? 'transparent' : bgColor;
    const patternType = state.image.pattern || 'blueprint';
    const gridColor = state.image.gridColor || '#1e293b';
    const gridSize = state.image.gridSize || 40;

    if (isBlank) {
      if (patternType === 'blueprint') {
        const major = gridSize * 4;
        filterDefs += `
          <pattern id="bgMinorGrid" width="${gridSize}" height="${gridSize}" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
            <path d="M ${gridSize} 0 L 0 0 0 ${gridSize}" fill="none" stroke="${gridColor}" stroke-width="0.75" stroke-opacity="0.55" />
          </pattern>
          <pattern id="bgMajorGrid" width="${major}" height="${major}" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
            <rect width="${major}" height="${major}" fill="url(#bgMinorGrid)" />
            <path d="M ${major} 0 L 0 0 0 ${major}" fill="none" stroke="${gridColor}" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-opacity="0.95" />
          </pattern>`;
      } else if (patternType === 'dots') {
        filterDefs += `
          <pattern id="bgDotGrid" width="${gridSize}" height="${gridSize}" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
            <circle cx="${gridSize / 2}" cy="${gridSize / 2}" r="1.6" fill="${gridColor}" />
          </pattern>`;
      } else if (patternType === 'graph') {
        const sub = Math.max(8, Math.round(gridSize / 4));
        filterDefs += `
          <pattern id="bgGraphSub" width="${sub}" height="${sub}" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
            <path d="M ${sub} 0 L 0 0 0 ${sub}" fill="none" stroke="${gridColor}" stroke-width="0.5" stroke-opacity="0.35" />
          </pattern>
          <pattern id="bgGraphGrid" width="${gridSize}" height="${gridSize}" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse">
            <rect width="${gridSize}" height="${gridSize}" fill="url(#bgGraphSub)" />
            <path d="M ${gridSize} 0 L 0 0 0 ${gridSize}" fill="none" stroke="${gridColor}" stroke-width="1.1" stroke-opacity="0.8" />
          </pattern>`;
      } else if (patternType === 'dark-radial') {
        filterDefs += `
          <radialGradient id="bgRadialGrad" cx="50%" cy="50%" r="70%">
            <stop offset="0%" stop-color="${gridColor}" stop-opacity="0.65" />
            <stop offset="100%" stop-color="${bgColor}" stop-opacity="1" />
          </radialGradient>`;
      }
    }

    if (opt.type === 'focus-pull') {
      filterDefs += `<filter id="opt-focus" x="-20%" y="-20%" width="140%" height="140%"><feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="${opt.startBlur}">`;
      if (opt.startBlur !== opt.endBlur) {
        const fDur = opt.focusDur || dur;
        if (loop === 'pingpong') {
          filterDefs += `<animate attributeName="stdDeviation" values="${opt.startBlur}; ${opt.endBlur}; ${opt.startBlur}" keyTimes="0; 0.5; 1" calcMode="spline" keySplines="0.42 0 0.58 1; 0.42 0 0.58 1" dur="${fDur * 2}s" repeatCount="indefinite" fill="freeze" />`;
        } else {
          const repeat = loop === 'once' ? '1' : 'indefinite';
          filterDefs += `<animate attributeName="stdDeviation" values="${opt.startBlur}; ${opt.endBlur}" keyTimes="0; 1" calcMode="spline" keySplines="0.42 0 0.58 1" dur="${fDur}s" repeatCount="${repeat}" fill="freeze" />`;
        }
      }
      filterDefs += `</feGaussianBlur></filter>`;
      filterAttr = `filter="url(#opt-focus)"`;

    } else if (opt.type === 'tilt-shift' && !isBlank) {
      const tShape = opt.tsShape || 'horizontal';
      const cw = opt.tsWidth / 2;
      const feather = Math.max(8, opt.tsWidth * 0.45);

      filterDefs += `<filter id="opt-ts-blur" x="-20%" y="-20%" width="140%" height="140%">
        <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="${opt.tsBlur}" />
      </filter>`;

      if (tShape === 'horizontal') {
        const topClear = Math.max(0, opt.tsPos - cw);
        const topSolid = Math.max(0, topClear - feather);
        const botClear = Math.min(100, opt.tsPos + cw);
        const botSolid = Math.min(100, botClear + feather);
        filterDefs += `
          <linearGradient id="ts-grad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="0%" y2="100%">
            <stop offset="0%" stop-color="white" stop-opacity="1" />
            <stop offset="${topSolid.toFixed(1)}%" stop-color="white" stop-opacity="1" />
            <stop offset="${topClear.toFixed(1)}%" stop-color="white" stop-opacity="0" />
            <stop offset="${botClear.toFixed(1)}%" stop-color="white" stop-opacity="0" />
            <stop offset="${botSolid.toFixed(1)}%" stop-color="white" stop-opacity="1" />
            <stop offset="100%" stop-color="white" stop-opacity="1" />
          </linearGradient>`;
      } else if (tShape === 'vertical') {
        const leftClear = Math.max(0, opt.tsPosX - cw);
        const leftSolid = Math.max(0, leftClear - feather);
        const rightClear = Math.min(100, opt.tsPosX + cw);
        const rightSolid = Math.min(100, rightClear + feather);
        filterDefs += `
          <linearGradient id="ts-grad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="0%">
            <stop offset="0%" stop-color="white" stop-opacity="1" />
            <stop offset="${leftSolid.toFixed(1)}%" stop-color="white" stop-opacity="1" />
            <stop offset="${leftClear.toFixed(1)}%" stop-color="white" stop-opacity="0" />
            <stop offset="${rightClear.toFixed(1)}%" stop-color="white" stop-opacity="0" />
            <stop offset="${rightSolid.toFixed(1)}%" stop-color="white" stop-opacity="1" />
            <stop offset="100%" stop-color="white" stop-opacity="1" />
          </linearGradient>`;
      } else if (tShape === 'radial') {
        const core = Math.max(0, opt.tsWidth);
        const outer = Math.min(100, core + feather * 1.4);
        filterDefs += `
          <radialGradient id="ts-grad" cx="${opt.tsPosX}%" cy="${opt.tsPos}%" r="70%">
            <stop offset="0%" stop-color="white" stop-opacity="0" />
            <stop offset="${core.toFixed(1)}%" stop-color="white" stop-opacity="0" />
            <stop offset="${outer.toFixed(1)}%" stop-color="white" stop-opacity="1" />
            <stop offset="100%" stop-color="white" stop-opacity="1" />
          </radialGradient>`;
      }

      filterDefs += `<mask id="ts-mask"><rect x="0" y="0" width="${w}" height="${h}" fill="url(#ts-grad)" /></mask>`;
      extraOverlays = this.buildPhotoElement(state.image, w, h, `filter="url(#opt-ts-blur)" mask="url(#ts-mask)" pointer-events="none"`);

    } else if (opt.type === 'motion-blur') {
      const rad = opt.mbAng * (Math.PI / 180);
      const bx = Math.abs(Math.cos(rad) * opt.mbInt).toFixed(2);
      const by = Math.abs(Math.sin(rad) * opt.mbInt).toFixed(2);
      filterDefs += `<filter id="opt-mb-blur" x="-20%" y="-20%" width="140%" height="140%"><feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="${bx} ${by}" /></filter>`;
      filterAttr = `filter="url(#opt-mb-blur)"`;
    }

    let baseSurfaceMarkup = '';
    if (isBlank) {
      let baseRects = c.bgTransparent ? '' : `<rect x="0" y="0" width="${w}" height="${h}" fill="${bgColor}" />`;
      if (patternType === 'blueprint') {
        baseRects += `<rect x="0" y="0" width="${w}" height="${h}" fill="url(#bgMajorGrid)" />`;
      } else if (patternType === 'dots') {
        baseRects += `<rect x="0" y="0" width="${w}" height="${h}" fill="url(#bgDotGrid)" />`;
      } else if (patternType === 'graph') {
        baseRects += `<rect x="0" y="0" width="${w}" height="${h}" fill="url(#bgGraphGrid)" />`;
      } else if (patternType === 'dark-radial') {
        baseRects += `<rect x="0" y="0" width="${w}" height="${h}" fill="url(#bgRadialGrad)" />`;
      }
      baseSurfaceMarkup = `<g ${filterAttr}>${baseRects}</g>`;
    } else {
      const bgRectIfContain = (state.image.photoFit === 'contain' && !c.bgTransparent)
        ? `<rect x="0" y="0" width="${w}" height="${h}" fill="${bgColor}" />`
        : '';
      baseSurfaceMarkup = `<g ${filterAttr}>${bgRectIfContain}${this.buildPhotoElement(state.image, w, h)}</g>`;
    }

    let frameOverlay = '';
    if (state.overlays && state.overlays.frame) {
      const fd = state.overlays.frameDepth || 40;
      let fcol = '#3e2723';
      if (state.overlays.frameStyle === 'gold') fcol = '#d4af37';
      else if (state.overlays.frameStyle === 'matte') fcol = '#f5f5f5';
      else if (state.overlays.frameStyle === 'obsidian') fcol = '#111111';

      frameOverlay = `
        <rect x="${fd / 2}" y="${fd / 2}" width="${w - fd}" height="${h - fd}" fill="none" stroke="${fcol}" stroke-width="${fd}" pointer-events="none" />
        <rect x="${fd}" y="${fd}" width="${w - (fd * 2)}" height="${h - (fd * 2)}" fill="none" stroke="rgba(0,0,0,0.5)" stroke-width="4" pointer-events="none" />
      `;
    }

    // preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" locks the HxW proportions so downloaded SVGs and HTML files never stretch or distort
    return `
    <svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" width="${w}" height="${h}" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" shape-rendering="geometricPrecision" text-rendering="geometricPrecision" style="background-color: ${bg}; width: 100%; height: 100%;">
        <defs>
            ${filterDefs}
            <style>
              ${cameraCSS}
            </style>
        </defs>
        
        <!-- INNER CAMERA LENS -->
        <g clip-path="url(#viewportClip)">
          <g class="camera-stage-3d">
            <g class="camera-rig">
              ${baseSurfaceMarkup}
              ${extraOverlays}
              <!-- INJECT_CAMERA_RIG_ELEMENTS -->
            </g>
          </g>
        </g>

        ${frameOverlay}
    </svg>
    `;
  }

=== ./internal/web/static/js/history.js ===
window.HistoryManager = {
  undoStack: [],
  redoStack: [],
  maxDepth: 50,
  isRestoring: false,
  debounceTimer: null,
  _initialized: false,

  trackedControlIds: [
    'canvasPatternSelect',
    'canvasPresetSelect',
    'canvasWidthInput',
    'canvasHeightInput',
    'photoFitSelect',
    'photoCropZoomSlider',
    'photoCropZoomNum',
    'photoCropXSlider',
    'photoCropXNum',
    'photoCropYSlider',
    'photoCropYNum',
    'canvasBgColorPicker',
    'canvasBgTransparent',
    'canvasGridColorPicker',
    'canvasGridSizeSlider',
    'canvasGridSizeNum',
    'chkSnapToGrid',
    'snapGridSizeSlider',
    'snapGridSizeNum',
    'loopSelect',
    'durationSlider',
    'durationNum',
    'cameraMotionSelect',
    'staticZoomSlider',
    'staticZoomNum',
    'staticXSlider',
    'staticXNum',
    'staticYSlider',
    'staticYNum',
    'startXZoomSlider',
    'startXZoomNum',
    'startXSlider',
    'startXNum',
    'startYSlider',
    'startYNum',
    'endXZoomSlider',
    'endXZoomNum',
    'endXSlider',
    'endXNum',
    'endYSlider',
    'endYNum',
    'handZoomSlider',
    'handZoomNum',
    'handXSlider',
    'handXNum',
    'handYSlider',
    'handYNum',
    'driftSwaySlider',
    'driftSwayNum',
    'driftBreatheSlider',
    'driftBreatheNum',
    'perspZoomSlider',
    'perspZoomNum',
    'perspXSlider',
    'perspXNum',
    'perspYSlider',
    'perspYNum',
    'perspTiltXSlider',
    'perspTiltXNum',
    'perspTiltYSlider',
    'perspTiltYNum',
    'effectSelect',
    'startBlurSlider',
    'startBlurNum',
    'endBlurSlider',
    'endBlurNum',
    'focusDurSlider',
    'focusDurNum',
    'tsShapeSelect',
    'tsPosXSlider',
    'tsPosXNum',
    'tsPosSlider',
    'tsPosNum',
    'tsWidthSlider',
    'tsWidthNum',
    'tsBlurSlider',
    'tsBlurNum',
    'mbIntSlider',
    'mbIntNum',
    'mbAngSlider',
    'mbAngNum',
    'chkModuleFrame',
    'frameStyleSelect',
    'frameDepthSlider',
    'frameDepthNum'
  ],

  init() {
    if (this._initialized) return;
    this._initialized = true;

    const btnUndo = document.getElementById('btnUndo');
    const btnRedo = document.getElementById('btnRedo');
    if (btnUndo) btnUndo.addEventListener('click', () => this.undo());
    if (btnRedo) btnRedo.addEventListener('click', () => this.redo());

    window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      const tag = document.activeElement ? document.activeElement.tagName : '';
      if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA') {
        const type = document.activeElement.type;
        if (type === 'text') return;
      }
      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && !e.shiftKey && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'z') {
        e.preventDefault();
        this.undo();
      } else if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && (e.key.toLowerCase() === 'y' || (e.shiftKey && e.key.toLowerCase() === 'z'))) {
        e.preventDefault();
        this.redo();
      }
    });

    // Record initial baseline state once the app is ready
    setTimeout(() => {
      this.recordImmediate();
    }, 80);
  },

  captureSnapshot() {
    const controls = {};
    this.trackedControlIds.forEach((id) => {
      const el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (el) {
        controls[id] = el.type === 'checkbox' ? el.checked : el.value;
      }
    });

    return JSON.stringify({
      controls: controls,
      nodes: window.DiagramNodes ? window.DiagramNodes.list : [],
      nodesCounter: window.DiagramNodes ? window.DiagramNodes.counter : 0,
      flows: window.DataFlows ? window.DataFlows.list : [],
      flowsCounter: window.DataFlows ? window.DataFlows.counter : 0,
      glows: window.Glows ? window.Glows.list : [],
      glowsCounter: window.Glows ? window.Glows.counter : 0,
      ripples: window.Ripples ? window.Ripples.list : [],
      ripplesCounter: window.Ripples ? window.Ripples.counter : 0
    });
  },

  recordImmediate() {
    if (this.isRestoring) return;
    if (this.debounceTimer) {
      clearTimeout(this.debounceTimer);
      this.debounceTimer = null;
    }
    const snap = this.captureSnapshot();
    if (this.undoStack.length > 0 && this.undoStack[this.undoStack.length - 1] === snap) {
      return;
    }
    this.undoStack.push(snap);
    if (this.undoStack.length > this.maxDepth) {
      this.undoStack.shift();
    }
    this.redoStack = [];
    this.updateButtonsUI();
  },

  recordDebounced(delayMs = 280) {
    if (this.isRestoring) return;
    if (this.debounceTimer) clearTimeout(this.debounceTimer);
    this.debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      this.recordImmediate();
    }, delayMs);
  },

  applySnapshot(snapStr) {
    if (!snapStr) return;
    this.isRestoring = true;
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(snapStr);
      if (data.controls) {
        Object.keys(data.controls).forEach((id) => {
          const el = document.getElementById(id);
          if (el) {
            if (el.type === 'checkbox') el.checked = !!data.controls[id];
            else el.value = data.controls[id];
          }
        });
      }

      if (window.DiagramNodes) {
        window.DiagramNodes.list = data.nodes || [];
        window.DiagramNodes.counter = data.nodesCounter || 0;
        window.DiagramNodes.deselectNode();
      }
      if (window.DataFlows) {
        window.DataFlows.list = data.flows || [];
        window.DataFlows.counter = data.flowsCounter || 0;
        window.DataFlows.deselectFlow();
      }
      if (window.Glows) {
        window.Glows.list = data.glows || [];
        window.Glows.counter = data.glowsCounter || 0;
        window.Glows.deselectGlow();
      }
      if (window.Ripples) {
        window.Ripples.list = data.ripples || [];
        window.Ripples.counter = data.ripplesCounter || 0;
        window.Ripples.deselectRipple();
      }

      if (typeof window.syncUIAfterHistoryRestore === 'function') {
        window.syncUIAfterHistoryRestore();
      } else if (typeof window.triggerAppRender === 'function') {
        window.triggerAppRender();
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('History restore error:', err);
    } finally {
      this.isRestoring = false;
      this.updateButtonsUI();
    }
  },

  undo() {
    if (this.undoStack.length <= 1) return;
    const current = this.undoStack.pop();
    this.redoStack.push(current);
    const prev = this.undoStack[this.undoStack.length - 1];
    this.applySnapshot(prev);
  },

  redo() {
    if (this.redoStack.length === 0) return;
    const next = this.redoStack.pop();
    this.undoStack.push(next);
    this.applySnapshot(next);
  },

  updateButtonsUI() {
    const btnUndo = document.getElementById('btnUndo');
    const btnRedo = document.getElementById('btnRedo');
    if (btnUndo) btnUndo.disabled = this.undoStack.length <= 1;
    if (btnRedo) btnRedo.disabled = this.redoStack.length === 0;
  }

['dragenter', 'dragover', 'dragleave', 'drop'].forEach((evt) => {
  document.addEventListener(evt, (e) => e.preventDefault(), false);

window.Stage = {
  activePlacement: 'start',
  isDragging: false,
  isPanningStage: false,
  isDraggingPhotoCrop: false,
  dragPhotoCropEnabled: false,
  spacePressed: false,
  canvasWidth: 1280,
  canvasHeight: 720,
  zoomMode: 'fit',
  zoomScale: 1.0,
  panX: 0,
  panY: 0,
  panStartClientX: 0,
  panStartClientY: 0,
  cropDragLastX: 0,
  cropDragLastY: 0,
  nodeGrabOffsetX: 0,
  nodeGrabOffsetY: 0,

  snapPctToGrid(xPct, yPct) {
    const snapChk = document.getElementById('chkSnapToGrid');
    if (!snapChk || !snapChk.checked) {
      return {
        x: Math.round(Math.min(100, Math.max(0, xPct)) * 10) / 10,
        y: Math.round(Math.min(100, Math.max(0, yPct)) * 10) / 10
      };
    }

    const snapSizeEl = document.getElementById('snapGridSizeNum') || document.getElementById('snapGridSizeSlider');
    const gridPx = Math.max(2, parseFloat(snapSizeEl ? snapSizeEl.value : 20) || 20);

    const w = this.canvasWidth || 1280;
    const h = this.canvasHeight || 720;

    const rawPxX = (xPct / 100) * w;
    const rawPxY = (yPct / 100) * h;

    const snappedPxX = Math.min(w, Math.max(0, Math.round(rawPxX / gridPx) * gridPx));
    const snappedPxY = Math.min(h, Math.max(0, Math.round(rawPxY / gridPx) * gridPx));

    return {
      x: Number(((snappedPxX / w) * 100).toFixed(3)),
      y: Number(((snappedPxY / h) * 100).toFixed(3))
    };
  },

  init(onPlaceCallback, onFileLoadedCallback) {
    this.previewBox = document.getElementById('previewBox');
    this.svgContainer = document.getElementById('svgContainer');
    this.overlayLayer = document.getElementById('stageOverlayLayer');
    this.trajectorySvg = document.getElementById('trajectorySvg');
    this.borderBadge = document.getElementById('canvasBorderBadge');
    this.uploadDrop = document.getElementById('uploadDrop');
    this.fileInput = document.getElementById('fileInput');
    this.onPlaceCallback = onPlaceCallback;

    if (this.trajectorySvg && !document.getElementById('nodeSelectionBox')) {
      const ns = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
      const rect = document.createElementNS(ns, 'rect');
      rect.setAttribute('id', 'nodeSelectionBox');
      rect.setAttribute('rx', '8');
      rect.setAttribute('fill', 'none');
      rect.setAttribute('stroke', '#a371f7');
      rect.setAttribute('stroke-width', '2');
      rect.setAttribute('stroke-dasharray', '6,4');
      rect.style.display = 'none';
      this.trajectorySvg.appendChild(rect);
    }

    this.initZoomControls();

    // Prevent Spacebar from activating focused buttons when used to pan the stage
    window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      const activeTag = document.activeElement ? document.activeElement.tagName : '';
      if (e.code === 'Space' && !['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(activeTag)) {
        e.preventDefault();
        this.spacePressed = true;
        if (this.previewBox) this.previewBox.classList.add('panning-stage');
      }
    });

    window.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => {
      if (e.code === 'Space') {
        this.spacePressed = false;
        if (!this.isPanningStage && this.previewBox) {
          this.previewBox.classList.remove('panning-stage');
        }
      }
    });

    if (this.previewBox) {
      this.previewBox.addEventListener('wheel', (e) => {
        if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) {
          e.preventDefault();
          const currentPct = Math.round(this.getEffectiveScale() * 100);
          const delta = e.deltaY < 0 ? 15 : -15;
          this.setManualZoomPct(Math.min(300, Math.max(25, currentPct + delta)));
        }
      }, { passive: false });

      this.previewBox.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
        if (e.button === 1 || (e.button === 0 && this.spacePressed)) {
          e.preventDefault();
          this.isPanningStage = true;
          this.panStartClientX = e.clientX - this.panX;
          this.panStartClientY = e.clientY - this.panY;
          this.previewBox.classList.add('panning-stage');
          try { this.previewBox.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (_) {}
          return;
        }

        if (e.button !== 0) return;

        if (this.dragPhotoCropEnabled && typeof this.onPhotoCropDrag === 'function') {
          this.isDraggingPhotoCrop = true;
          this.cropDragLastX = e.clientX;
          this.cropDragLastY = e.clientY;
          try { this.previewBox.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (_) {}
          return;
        }

        const coords = this.clientToCanvasPct(e.clientX, e.clientY);

        const flowReticle = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-flow-node-idx]') : null;
        if (flowReticle && window.DataFlows) {
          const idx = parseInt(flowReticle.getAttribute('data-flow-node-idx'), 10);
          if (!isNaN(idx)) {
            window.DataFlows.activeNodeIdx = idx;
            const activeFlow = window.DataFlows.list.find((fl) => fl.id === window.DataFlows.activeId);
            if (activeFlow) window.DataFlows.refreshNodeSlider(activeFlow);
            window.DataFlows.updateUIOverlay();
          }
          this.nodeGrabOffsetX = 0;
          this.nodeGrabOffsetY = 0;
          this.isDragging = true;
          try { this.previewBox.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (_) {}
          const snappedFlow = this.snapPctToGrid(coords.x, coords.y);
          this.onPlaceCallback(this.activePlacement, snappedFlow.x, snappedFlow.y);
          return;
        }

        const otherOverlayActive =
          (window.DataFlows && window.DataFlows.activeId !== null) ||
          (window.Glows && window.Glows.activeId !== null) ||
          (window.Ripples && window.Ripples.activeId !== null);

        if (!otherOverlayActive && window.DiagramNodes) {
          let clickedNode = null;
          const nodeGroup = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-node-id]') : null;
          if (nodeGroup) {
            const clickedId = parseInt(nodeGroup.getAttribute('data-node-id'), 10);
            clickedNode = window.DiagramNodes.list.find((n) => n.id === clickedId) || null;
          }
          if (!clickedNode && typeof window.DiagramNodes.findNodeAtPct === 'function') {
            clickedNode = window.DiagramNodes.findNodeAtPct(coords.x, coords.y);
          }

          if (clickedNode) {
            if (window.DiagramNodes.activeId !== clickedNode.id) {
              window.DiagramNodes.selectNode(clickedNode.id, true);
            }
            this.nodeGrabOffsetX = clickedNode.x - coords.x;
            this.nodeGrabOffsetY = clickedNode.y - coords.y;
          } else {
            this.nodeGrabOffsetX = 0;
            this.nodeGrabOffsetY = 0;
          }
        } else {
          this.nodeGrabOffsetX = 0;
          this.nodeGrabOffsetY = 0;
        }

        this.isDragging = true;
        try { this.previewBox.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (_) {}

        const rawTargetX = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, coords.x + this.nodeGrabOffsetX));
        const rawTargetY = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, coords.y + this.nodeGrabOffsetY));
        const snapped = this.snapPctToGrid(rawTargetX, rawTargetY);
        this.onPlaceCallback(this.activePlacement, snapped.x, snapped.y);
      });

      this.previewBox.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
        if (this.isPanningStage) {
          this.panX = e.clientX - this.panStartClientX;
          this.panY = e.clientY - this.panStartClientY;
          this.syncOverlayBounds(this.canvasWidth, this.canvasHeight);
          return;
        }

        if (this.isDraggingPhotoCrop && typeof this.onPhotoCropDrag === 'function') {
          const dx = e.clientX - this.cropDragLastX;
          const dy = e.clientY - this.cropDragLastY;
          this.cropDragLastX = e.clientX;
          this.cropDragLastY = e.clientY;
          this.onPhotoCropDrag(dx, dy);
          return;
        }

        if (!this.isDragging) return;
        const coords = this.clientToCanvasPct(e.clientX, e.clientY);
        const rawTargetX = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, coords.x + this.nodeGrabOffsetX));
        const rawTargetY = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, coords.y + this.nodeGrabOffsetY));
        const snapped = this.snapPctToGrid(rawTargetX, rawTargetY);
        this.onPlaceCallback(this.activePlacement, snapped.x, snapped.y);
      });

      const endDrag = (e) => {
        if (this.isPanningStage) {
          this.isPanningStage = false;
          if (!this.spacePressed && this.previewBox) {
            this.previewBox.classList.remove('panning-stage');
          }
        }
        const wasDragging = this.isDragging || this.isDraggingPhotoCrop;
        this.isDraggingPhotoCrop = false;
        this.isDragging = false;
        this.nodeGrabOffsetX = 0;
        this.nodeGrabOffsetY = 0;
        try { this.previewBox.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (_) {}

        if (wasDragging) {
          if (typeof window.triggerAppRender === 'function') {
            window.triggerAppRender();
          }
          if (window.HistoryManager) {
            window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
          }
        }
      };

      this.previewBox.addEventListener('pointerup', endDrag);
      this.previewBox.addEventListener('pointercancel', endDrag);

      if (window.ResizeObserver) {
        const ro = new ResizeObserver(() => {
          this.syncOverlayBounds(this.canvasWidth, this.canvasHeight);
          if (window.DataFlows && window.DataFlows.activeId !== null) {
            window.DataFlows.updateUIOverlay();
          }
          if (window.DiagramNodes && window.DiagramNodes.activeId !== null) {
            const activeNode = window.DiagramNodes.list.find((n) => n.id === window.DiagramNodes.activeId);
            if (activeNode) window.DiagramNodes.updateUIOverlay(activeNode);
          }
        });
        ro.observe(this.previewBox);
      }
    }

    if (this.uploadDrop) {
      this.uploadDrop.addEventListener('dragover', () => this.uploadDrop.classList.add('drag-active'));
      this.uploadDrop.addEventListener('dragleave', () => this.uploadDrop.classList.remove('drag-active'));
      this.uploadDrop.addEventListener('drop', (e) => {
        this.uploadDrop.classList.remove('drag-active');
        if (e.dataTransfer && e.dataTransfer.files.length) {
          this.loadFile(e.dataTransfer.files[0], onFileLoadedCallback);
        }
      });
    }

    if (this.fileInput) {
      this.fileInput.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
        if (e.target.files.length) {
          this.loadFile(e.target.files[0], onFileLoadedCallback);
          this.fileInput.value = '';
        }
      });
    }
  },

  initZoomControls() {
    const slider = document.getElementById('stageZoomSlider');
    const btnIn = document.getElementById('btnZoomIn');
    const btnOut = document.getElementById('btnZoomOut');
    const btnFit = document.getElementById('btnZoomFit');
    const btn100 = document.getElementById('btnZoom100');

    if (slider) {
      slider.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
        this.setManualZoomPct(parseInt(e.target.value, 10));
      });
    }
    if (btnIn) {
      btnIn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const cur = Math.round(this.getEffectiveScale() * 100);
        this.setManualZoomPct(Math.min(300, cur + 20));
      });
    }
    if (btnOut) {
      btnOut.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const cur = Math.round(this.getEffectiveScale() * 100);
        this.setManualZoomPct(Math.max(25, cur - 20));
      });
    }
    if (btnFit) {
      btnFit.addEventListener('click', () => {
        this.zoomMode = 'fit';
        this.panX = 0;
        this.panY = 0;
        this.syncOverlayBounds(this.canvasWidth, this.canvasHeight);
      });
    }
    if (btn100) {
      btn100.addEventListener('click', () => {
        this.panX = 0;
        this.panY = 0;
        this.setManualZoomPct(100);
      });
    }
  },

  setManualZoomPct(pct) {
    this.zoomMode = 'manual';
    this.zoomScale = pct / 100;
    const slider = document.getElementById('stageZoomSlider');
    if (slider) slider.value = pct;
    this.syncOverlayBounds(this.canvasWidth, this.canvasHeight);
  },

  getFitScale() {
    if (!this.previewBox) return 1.0;
    const rect = this.previewBox.getBoundingClientRect();
    const pad = 28;
    const availW = Math.max(64, rect.width - pad * 2);
    const availH = Math.max(64, rect.height - pad * 2);
    return Math.min(availW / this.canvasWidth, availH / this.canvasHeight);
  },

  getEffectiveScale() {
    return this.zoomMode === 'fit' ? this.getFitScale() : this.zoomScale;
  },

  syncOverlayBounds(canvasW, canvasH) {
    if (!this.previewBox) return;
    if (canvasW > 0 && canvasH > 0) {
      this.canvasWidth = canvasW;
      this.canvasHeight = canvasH;
    }

    const rect = this.previewBox.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (rect.width <= 0 || rect.height <= 0) return;

    const scale = this.getEffectiveScale();
    const renderedW = this.canvasWidth * scale;
    const renderedH = this.canvasHeight * scale;
    const offsetX = ((rect.width - renderedW) / 2) + this.panX;
    const offsetY = ((rect.height - renderedH) / 2) + this.panY;

    if (this.svgContainer) {
      this.svgContainer.style.left = offsetX + 'px';
      this.svgContainer.style.top = offsetY + 'px';
      this.svgContainer.style.width = renderedW + 'px';
      this.svgContainer.style.height = renderedH + 'px';
    }

    if (this.overlayLayer) {
      this.overlayLayer.style.left = offsetX + 'px';
      this.overlayLayer.style.top = offsetY + 'px';
      this.overlayLayer.style.width = renderedW + 'px';
      this.overlayLayer.style.height = renderedH + 'px';
    }

    if (this.trajectorySvg) {
      this.trajectorySvg.setAttribute('viewBox', '0 0 ' + this.canvasWidth + ' ' + this.canvasHeight);
    }

    const pct = Math.round(scale * 100);
    const zoomLabel = document.getElementById('stageZoomLabel');
    const zoomSlider = document.getElementById('stageZoomSlider');
    if (zoomLabel) zoomLabel.innerText = this.zoomMode === 'fit' ? ('Fit (' + pct + '%)') : (pct + '%');
    if (zoomSlider && this.zoomMode === 'fit') zoomSlider.value = Math.min(300, Math.max(25, pct));
    if (this.borderBadge) {
      const snapOn = document.getElementById('chkSnapToGrid')?.checked;
      const snapStep = document.getElementById('snapGridSizeNum')?.value || 20;
      const snapTag = snapOn ? (' &bull; 🧲 Grid ' + snapStep + 'px') : '';
      this.borderBadge.innerHTML = this.canvasWidth + ' &times; ' + this.canvasHeight + ' px &bull; ' + pct + '%' + snapTag;
    }
  },

  clientToCanvasPct(clientX, clientY) {
    const targetBox = this.svgContainer || this.overlayLayer || this.previewBox;
    if (!targetBox) return { x: 50, y: 50 };
    const rect = targetBox.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (rect.width <= 0 || rect.height <= 0) return { x: 50, y: 50 };

    const rawX = ((clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 100;
    const rawY = ((clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 100;
    return {
      x: Math.round(Math.min(100, Math.max(0, rawX)) * 10) / 10,
      y: Math.round(Math.min(100, Math.max(0, rawY)) * 10) / 10
    };
  },

  setActive(target) {
    this.activePlacement = target;
    const btnStart = document.getElementById('btnModeStart');
    const btnEnd = document.getElementById('btnModeEnd');
    const retS = document.getElementById('reticleStart');
    const retE = document.getElementById('reticleEnd');
    if (btnStart && btnEnd && retS && retE) {
      btnStart.className = target === 'start' ? 'mode-btn active-start' : 'mode-btn';
      btnEnd.className = target === 'end' ? 'mode-btn active-end' : 'mode-btn';
      retS.classList.toggle('active-reticle', target === 'start');
      retE.classList.toggle('active-reticle', target === 'end');
    }
  },

  updateReticles(sX, sY, eX, eY) {
    const retS = document.getElementById('reticleStart');
    const retE = document.getElementById('reticleEnd');
    const line = document.getElementById('flightLine');
    if (retS) {
      retS.style.left = sX + '%';
      retS.style.top = sY + '%';
    }
    if (retE) {
      retE.style.left = eX + '%';
      retE.style.top = eY + '%';
    }
    if (line) {
      line.setAttribute('x1', sX + '%');
      line.setAttribute('y1', sY + '%');
      line.setAttribute('x2', eX + '%');
      line.setAttribute('y2', eY + '%');
    }
  },

  loadFile(file, callback) {
    if (!file) return;
    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/') && !/\.(jpe?g|png|webp)$/i.test(file.name)) {
      alert('Please choose a valid image file (JPEG, PNG, WEBP).');
      return;
    }
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      const dataUrl = e.target.result;
      const img = new Image();
      img.onload = () => {
        callback({
          filename: file.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, ''),
          mime: file.type || 'image/jpeg',
          b64: dataUrl.split(',')[1],
          width: img.naturalWidth,
          height: img.naturalHeight,
          naturalWidth: img.naturalWidth,
          naturalHeight: img.naturalHeight
        });
      };
      img.onerror = () => alert('Failed to decode image data.');
      img.src = dataUrl;
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  }
