window.DiagramNodes = {
  list: [],
  activeId: null,
  counter: 0,
  _initialized: false,

  escapeXML(str) {
    if (!str) return '';
    return String(str)
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
      .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
      .replace(/'/g, '&apos;');
  },

  getNodeEffectiveProps(n) {
    return {
      width: n.width || 170,
      height: n.height || 80,
      fillColor: n.fillColor || '#161b22',
      strokeColor: n.strokeColor || '#58a6ff',
      textColor: n.textColor || '#e6edf3',
      fillOpacity: n.fillOpacity !== undefined ? n.fillOpacity : 0.92,
      strokeWidth: n.strokeWidth !== undefined ? n.strokeWidth : 2,
      strokeStyle: n.strokeStyle || 'solid',
      fontSize: n.fontSize || 15
    };
  },

  findNodeAtPct(xPct, yPct) {
    const canvasW = (window.Stage && window.Stage.canvasWidth) || 1280;
    const canvasH = (window.Stage && window.Stage.canvasHeight) || 720;
    const px = (xPct / 100) * canvasW;
    const py = (yPct / 100) * canvasH;

    for (let i = this.list.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const n = this.list[i];
      const eff = this.getNodeEffectiveProps(n);
      const cx = (n.x / 100) * canvasW;
      const cy = (n.y / 100) * canvasH;
      const hw = (eff.width / 2) + 8;
      const hh = (eff.height / 2) + 8;
      if (px >= cx - hw && px <= cx + hw && py >= cy - hh && py <= cy + hh) {
        return n;
      }
    }
    return null;
  },

  init() {
    if (this._initialized) return;
    this._initialized = true;

    if (window.NodePresets) window.NodePresets.init();

    const btnAdd = document.getElementById('btnAddNode');
    if (btnAdd) btnAdd.addEventListener('click', () => this.addNode());

    ['shape', 'strokeStyle', 'anim'].forEach((key) => {
      const cap = key.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + key.slice(1);
      const el = document.getElementById('node' + cap + 'Select');
      if (el) {
        el.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
          this.updateActive(key, e.target.value);
          if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
        });
      }
    });

    ['fillColor', 'strokeColor', 'textColor'].forEach((key) => {
      const cap = key.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + key.slice(1);
      const el = document.getElementById('node' + cap + 'Picker');
      if (el) {
        el.addEventListener('input', (e) => this.updateActive(key, e.target.value));
        el.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
          this.updateActive(key, e.target.value);
          if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
        });
      }
    });

    ['text', 'subtext'].forEach((key) => {
      const cap = key.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + key.slice(1);
      const el = document.getElementById('node' + cap + 'Input');
      if (el) {
        el.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
          this.updateActive(key, e.target.value);
          if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordDebounced();
        });
      }
    });

    ['width', 'height', 'fillOpacity', 'strokeWidth', 'fontSize'].forEach((key) => {
      const cap = key.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + key.slice(1);
      const slider = document.getElementById('node' + cap + 'Slider');
      const num = document.getElementById('node' + cap + 'Num');
      if (slider && num) {
        slider.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
          num.value = e.target.value;
          this.updateActive(key, parseFloat(e.target.value));
          if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordDebounced();
        });
        num.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
          slider.value = e.target.value;
          this.updateActive(key, parseFloat(e.target.value));
          if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordDebounced();
        });
      }
    });
  },

  applyPresetToActive() {
    if (window.NodePresets) window.NodePresets.applyToActiveNode();
  },

  updateActiveDimBadge(node) {
    const badge = document.getElementById('activeNodeDimBadge');
    if (!badge || !node) return;
    badge.innerHTML = 'W: ' + Math.round(node.width || 170) + ' &times; H: ' + Math.round(node.height || 80) + ' px';
  },

  refreshAfterChange() {
    const activeNode = this.list.find((n) => n.id === this.activeId);
    if (activeNode) {
      this.updateActiveDimBadge(activeNode);
    }
    this.renderList();
    this.triggerAppRender();
    if (activeNode) {
      this.updateUIOverlay(activeNode);
    }
  },

  addNode() {
    this.counter++;
    const offset = ((this.counter - 1) % 6) * 6;
    const newNode = {
      id: this.counter,
      shape: 'rounded',
      text: 'Node ' + this.counter,
      subtext: '',
      x: 32 + offset,
      y: 32 + offset,
      width: 170,
      height: 80,
      fillColor: '#161b22',
      fillOpacity: 0.92,
      strokeColor: '#58a6ff',
      strokeWidth: 2,
      strokeStyle: 'solid',
      textColor: '#e6edf3',
      fontSize: 15,
      anim: 'none'
    };

    const autoApplyChk = document.getElementById('chkAutoApplyPreset');
    if ((!autoApplyChk || autoApplyChk.checked) && window.NodePresets) {
      window.NodePresets.applyToNodeObject(newNode);
    }

    if (window.Stage && typeof window.Stage.snapPctToGrid === 'function') {
      const snapped = window.Stage.snapPctToGrid(newNode.x, newNode.y);
      newNode.x = snapped.x;
      newNode.y = snapped.y;
    }

    this.list.push(newNode);
    this.selectNode(newNode.id, true);
    this.triggerAppRender();
    if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
  },

  selectNode(id, openAccordion = false) {
    if (window.DataFlows) window.DataFlows.deselectFlow();
    if (window.Glows) window.Glows.deselectGlow();
    if (window.Ripples) window.Ripples.deselectRipple();

    this.activeId = id;
    this.renderList();
    document.body.classList.add('editing-flow');

    const node = this.list.find((n) => n.id === id);
    if (!node) return;

    if (openAccordion && window.openAccordionById) {
      const presetsEl = document.getElementById('acc-presets');
      const presetsOpen = presetsEl ? presetsEl.classList.contains('open') : false;
      if (!presetsOpen) {
        window.openAccordionById('acc-nodes');
      }
    }

    const editor = document.getElementById('nodeEditor');
    if (editor) editor.style.display = 'block';

    const lbl = document.getElementById('editingNodeLabel');
    if (lbl) lbl.innerText = 'Editing Shape ' + id;

    this.updateActiveDimBadge(node);

    const shSel = document.getElementById('nodeShapeSelect');
    if (shSel) shSel.value = node.shape || 'rounded';
    const txtIn = document.getElementById('nodeTextInput');
    if (txtIn) txtIn.value = node.text || '';
    const subIn = document.getElementById('nodeSubtextInput');
    if (subIn) subIn.value = node.subtext || '';
    const fPick = document.getElementById('nodeFillColorPicker');
    if (fPick) fPick.value = node.fillColor || '#161b22';
    const sPick = document.getElementById('nodeStrokeColorPicker');
    if (sPick) sPick.value = node.strokeColor || '#58a6ff';
    const tPick = document.getElementById('nodeTextColorPicker');
    if (tPick) tPick.value = node.textColor || '#e6edf3';
    const stSel = document.getElementById('nodeStrokeStyleSelect');
    if (stSel) stSel.value = node.strokeStyle || 'solid';
    const anSel = document.getElementById('nodeAnimSelect');
    if (anSel) anSel.value = node.anim || 'none';

    ['width', 'height', 'fillOpacity', 'strokeWidth', 'fontSize'].forEach((key) => {
      const cap = key.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + key.slice(1);
      const sl = document.getElementById('node' + cap + 'Slider');
      const nu = document.getElementById('node' + cap + 'Num');
      if (sl) sl.value = node[key];
      if (nu) nu.value = node[key];
    });

    this.updateUIOverlay(node);
  },

  deselectNode() {
    this.activeId = null;
    this.renderList();
    document.body.classList.remove('editing-flow');
    const ed = document.getElementById('nodeEditor');
    if (ed) ed.style.display = 'none';
    const selBox = document.getElementById('nodeSelectionBox');
    if (selBox) selBox.style.display = 'none';
  },

  updateActive(key, value) {
    if (this.activeId === null) return;
    const node = this.list.find((n) => n.id === this.activeId);
    if (node) {
      node[key] = value;
      this.refreshAfterChange();
    }
  },

  handleStageClick(x, y) {
    if (this.activeId === null) return false;
    const node = this.list.find((n) => n.id === this.activeId);
    if (node) {
      node.x = x;
      node.y = y;
      this.updateUIOverlay(node);

      const canvasW = (window.Stage && window.Stage.canvasWidth) || 1280;
      const canvasH = (window.Stage && window.Stage.canvasHeight) || 720;
      const cx = (node.x / 100) * canvasW;
      const cy = (node.y / 100) * canvasH;
      const liveGroup = document.querySelector('#svgContainer [data-node-id="' + node.id + '"]');
      if (liveGroup) {
        liveGroup.setAttribute('transform', 'translate(' + cx.toFixed(2) + ', ' + cy.toFixed(2) + ')');
      } else {
        this.triggerAppRender();
      }
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  },

  updateUIOverlay(node) {
    const selBox = document.getElementById('nodeSelectionBox');
    if (!selBox || !node) return;

    const eff = this.getNodeEffectiveProps(node);
    const canvasW = (window.Stage && window.Stage.canvasWidth) || 1280;
    const canvasH = (window.Stage && window.Stage.canvasHeight) || 720;
    const cx = (node.x / 100) * canvasW;
    const cy = (node.y / 100) * canvasH;
    const pad = 10;
    const w = eff.width + pad * 2;
    const h = eff.height + pad * 2;

    selBox.setAttribute('x', (cx - w / 2).toFixed(1));
    selBox.setAttribute('y', (cy - h / 2).toFixed(1));
    selBox.setAttribute('width', w.toFixed(1));
    selBox.setAttribute('height', h.toFixed(1));
    selBox.setAttribute('stroke', eff.strokeColor || '#a371f7');
    selBox.style.display = 'block';
  },

  removeNode(id) {
    this.list = this.list.filter((n) => n.id !== id);
    if (this.activeId === id) this.deselectNode();
    else this.renderList();
    this.triggerAppRender();
    if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
  },

  renderList() {
    const container = document.getElementById('nodeListContainer');
    if (!container) return;
    if (this.list.length === 0) {
      container.innerHTML = '<div class="stage-hint">No shapes or text boxes active.</div>';
      return;
    }
    container.innerHTML = '';
    this.list.forEach((node) => {
      const eff = this.getNodeEffectiveProps(node);
      const div = document.createElement('div');
      div.className = 'flow-list-item' + (this.activeId === node.id ? ' selected' : '');
      div.style.borderLeftColor = eff.strokeColor;
      const labelPreview = (node.text || ('Node ' + node.id)).split('|')[0].substring(0, 15);
      div.innerHTML =
        '<span>' + this.escapeXML(labelPreview) +
        ' <small style="color:#8b949e; font-family:monospace;">(' + node.shape + ' &bull; ' + eff.width + '&times;' + eff.height + ')</small></span>' +
        '<div style="display:flex; gap:0.35rem; align-items:center;">' +
        '<button type="button" title="Snap this shape to active Preset settings" style="background:rgba(137,87,229,0.22); border:1px solid rgba(163,113,247,0.5); color:#d2a8ff; padding:0.12rem 0.38rem; font-size:0.68rem; border-radius:4px; cursor:pointer;" onclick="window.DiagramNodes.selectNode(' + node.id + '); window.DiagramNodes.applyPresetToActive(); event.stopPropagation();">⚡ Preset</button>' +
        '<button type="button" style="background:transparent; border:none; color:#ff4444; font-weight:bold; cursor:pointer; padding:0.1rem 0.25rem;" onclick="window.DiagramNodes.removeNode(' + node.id + '); event.stopPropagation();">X</button>' +
        '</div>';
      div.onclick = () => this.selectNode(node.id);
      container.appendChild(div);
    });
  },

  triggerAppRender() {
    if (typeof window.triggerAppRender === 'function') {
      window.triggerAppRender();
    } else {
      const durSlider = document.getElementById('durationSlider');
      if (durSlider) durSlider.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));
    }
  },

  injectIntoSVG(svgString, state) {
    if (this.list.length === 0) return svgString;
    const canvasW = state.image.width || 1280;
    const canvasH = state.image.height || 720;

    const defs =
      '<filter id="nodeDropShadow" filterUnits="userSpaceOnUse" x="-10%" y="-10%" width="120%" height="120%">' +
      '<feDropShadow dx="0" dy="4" stdDeviation="6" flood-color="#000000" flood-opacity="0.45" />' +
      '</filter>';
    let elements = '';

    this.list.forEach((n) => {
      const eff = this.getNodeEffectiveProps(n);
      const cx = (n.x / 100) * canvasW;
      const cy = (n.y / 100) * canvasH;
      const w = eff.width;
      const h = eff.height;
      const hw = w / 2;
      const hh = h / 2;

      let dashAttr = '';
      if (eff.strokeStyle === 'dashed') {
        dashAttr = 'stroke-dasharray="8,5"';
      } else if (eff.strokeStyle === 'dotted') {
        dashAttr = 'stroke-dasharray="2,5" stroke-linecap="round"';
      }

      const commonStyle =
        'fill="' + eff.fillColor + '" fill-opacity="' + eff.fillOpacity + '" stroke="' + eff.strokeColor + '" stroke-width="' + eff.strokeWidth + '" ' + dashAttr;
      let shapeSVG = '';

      if (n.shape === 'textbox') {
        shapeSVG = '<rect x="' + (-hw) + '" y="' + (-hh) + '" width="' + w + '" height="' + h + '" fill="transparent" stroke="none" />';
      } else if (n.shape === 'rect') {
        shapeSVG = '<rect x="' + (-hw) + '" y="' + (-hh) + '" width="' + w + '" height="' + h + '" rx="2" ' + commonStyle + ' filter="url(#nodeDropShadow)" />';
      } else if (n.shape === 'rounded') {
        shapeSVG = '<rect x="' + (-hw) + '" y="' + (-hh) + '" width="' + w + '" height="' + h + '" rx="12" ' + commonStyle + ' filter="url(#nodeDropShadow)" />';
      } else if (n.shape === 'pill') {
        const r = Math.min(hw, hh);
        shapeSVG = '<rect x="' + (-hw) + '" y="' + (-hh) + '" width="' + w + '" height="' + h + '" rx="' + r + '" ' + commonStyle + ' filter="url(#nodeDropShadow)" />';
      } else if (n.shape === 'circle') {
        const r = Math.min(hw, hh);
        shapeSVG = '<circle cx="0" cy="0" r="' + r + '" ' + commonStyle + ' filter="url(#nodeDropShadow)" />';
      } else if (n.shape === 'diamond') {
        shapeSVG = '<polygon points="0,' + (-hh) + ' ' + hw + ',0 0,' + hh + ' ' + (-hw) + ',0" stroke-linejoin="round" ' + commonStyle + ' filter="url(#nodeDropShadow)" />';
      } else if (n.shape === 'hexagon') {
        const inset = w * 0.22;
        shapeSVG =
          '<polygon points="' + (-hw + inset) + ',' + (-hh) + ' ' + (hw - inset) + ',' + (-hh) + ' ' + hw + ',0 ' +
          (hw - inset) + ',' + hh + ' ' + (-hw + inset) + ',' + hh + ' ' + (-hw) + ',0" stroke-linejoin="round" ' + commonStyle + ' filter="url(#nodeDropShadow)" />';
      } else if (n.shape === 'parallelogram') {
        const skew = w * 0.16;
        shapeSVG =
          '<polygon points="' + (-hw + skew) + ',' + (-hh) + ' ' + hw + ',' + (-hh) + ' ' + (hw - skew) + ',' + hh + ' ' + (-hw) + ',' + hh + '" stroke-linejoin="round" ' + commonStyle + ' filter="url(#nodeDropShadow)" />';
      } else if (n.shape === 'cylinder') {
        const ry = Math.max(8, Math.min(22, h * 0.18));
        const bodyTop = -hh + ry;
        const bodyBot = hh - ry;
        shapeSVG =
          '<g filter="url(#nodeDropShadow)">' +
          '<path d="M ' + (-hw) + ',' + bodyTop + ' L ' + (-hw) + ',' + bodyBot + ' A ' + hw + ',' + ry + ' 0 0,0 ' + hw + ',' + bodyBot + ' L ' + hw + ',' + bodyTop + ' Z" ' + commonStyle + ' />' +
          '<ellipse cx="0" cy="' + bodyTop + '" rx="' + hw + ',' + ry + '" ' + commonStyle + ' />' +
          '</g>';
      } else if (n.shape === 'cloud') {
        shapeSVG =
          '<path d="M ' + (-hw * 0.55) + ',' + (hh * 0.65) +
          ' C ' + (-hw * 0.95) + ',' + (hh * 0.65) + ' ' + (-hw) + ',' + (hh * 0.05) + ' ' + (-hw * 0.72) + ',' + (-hh * 0.2) +
          ' C ' + (-hw * 0.75) + ',' + (-hh * 0.75) + ' ' + (-hw * 0.15) + ',' + (-hh * 0.95) + ' ' + (hw * 0.08) + ',' + (-hh * 0.6) +
          ' C ' + (hw * 0.45) + ',' + (-hh * 0.9) + ' ' + (hw * 0.92) + ',' + (-hh * 0.5) + ' ' + (hw * 0.8) + ',' + (-hh * 0.05) +
          ' C ' + (hw * 1.05) + ',' + (hh * 0.25) + ' ' + (hw * 0.85) + ',' + (hh * 0.65) + ' ' + (hw * 0.5) + ',' + (hh * 0.65) + ' Z"' +
          ' stroke-linejoin="round" ' + commonStyle + ' filter="url(#nodeDropShadow)" />';
      } else if (n.shape === 'callout') {
        const fold = 16;
        shapeSVG =
          '<g filter="url(#nodeDropShadow)">' +
          '<polygon points="' + (-hw) + ',' + (-hh) + ' ' + (hw - fold) + ',' + (-hh) + ' ' + hw + ',' + (-hh + fold) + ' ' + hw + ',' + hh + ' ' + (-hw) + ',' + hh + '" stroke-linejoin="round" ' + commonStyle + ' />' +
          '<polyline points="' + (hw - fold) + ',' + (-hh) + ' ' + (hw - fold) + ',' + (-hh + fold) + ' ' + hw + ',' + (-hh + fold) + '" fill="none" stroke="' + eff.strokeColor + '" stroke-width="' + eff.strokeWidth + '" />' +
          '</g>';
      } else if (n.shape === 'axes') {
        let gridLines = '';
        const steps = 4;
        for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
          const gx = -hw + (w / steps) * i;
          const gy = hh - (h / steps) * i;
          gridLines += '<line x1="' + gx + '" y1="' + (-hh) + '" x2="' + gx + '" y2="' + hh + '" stroke="' + eff.strokeColor + '" stroke-opacity="0.2" stroke-width="1" stroke-dasharray="4,4" />';
          gridLines += '<line x1="' + (-hw) + '" y1="' + gy + '" x2="' + hw + '" y2="' + gy + '" stroke="' + eff.strokeColor + '" stroke-opacity="0.2" stroke-width="1" stroke-dasharray="4,4" />';
        }
        shapeSVG =
          '<rect x="' + (-hw) + '" y="' + (-hh) + '" width="' + w + '" height="' + h + '" fill="' + eff.fillColor + '" fill-opacity="' + eff.fillOpacity + '" stroke="none" />' +
          gridLines +
          '<polyline points="' + (-hw) + ',' + (-hh) + ' ' + (-hw) + ',' + hh + ' ' + hw + ',' + hh + '" fill="none" stroke="' + eff.strokeColor + '" stroke-width="' + Math.max(2, eff.strokeWidth) + '" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" />';
      }

      const lines = (n.text || '').split('|').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
      const hasSub = n.subtext && n.subtext.trim() !== '';
      const fSize = eff.fontSize;
      const subSize = Math.max(10, Math.round(fSize * 0.78));
      const lineHeight = fSize * 1.25;

      const totalTextHeight = lines.length * lineHeight + (hasSub ? subSize * 1.4 : 0);
      const startY = -(totalTextHeight / 2) + (fSize * 0.82);

      let textSVG = '';
      if (n.shape === 'axes') {
        if (lines.length > 0) {
          textSVG += '<text x="' + (-hw) + '" y="' + (-hh - 10) + '" fill="' + eff.textColor + '" font-family="-apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, \'Segoe UI\', Roboto, sans-serif" font-size="' + fSize + '" font-weight="600" text-anchor="start">' + this.escapeXML(lines.join(' ')) + '</text>';
        }
        if (hasSub) {
          textSVG += '<text x="' + hw + '" y="' + (hh + subSize + 8) + '" fill="' + eff.textColor + '" fill-opacity="0.75" font-family="monospace" font-size="' + subSize + '" text-anchor="end">' + this.escapeXML(n.subtext) + '</text>';
        }
      } else {
        lines.forEach((line, idx) => {
          const ly = startY + idx * lineHeight;
          textSVG += '<text x="0" y="' + ly.toFixed(1) + '" fill="' + eff.textColor + '" font-family="-apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, \'Segoe UI\', Roboto, sans-serif" font-size="' + fSize + '" font-weight="600" text-anchor="middle">' + this.escapeXML(line) + '</text>';
        });

        if (hasSub) {
          const subY = startY + lines.length * lineHeight + (subSize * 0.2);
          textSVG += '<text x="0" y="' + subY.toFixed(1) + '" fill="' + eff.textColor + '" fill-opacity="0.75" font-family="monospace" font-size="' + subSize + '" text-anchor="middle">' + this.escapeXML(n.subtext) + '</text>';
        }
      }

      let animTags = '';
      if (n.anim === 'pulse-border') {
        animTags = '<animate attributeName="opacity" values="1; 0.65; 1" dur="2.6s" repeatCount="indefinite" calcMode="spline" keySplines="0.42 0 0.58 1; 0.42 0 0.58 1" />';
      } else if (n.anim === 'float') {
        animTags = '<animateTransform attributeName="transform" type="translate" additive="sum" values="0,0; 0,-6; 0,0" dur="3.2s" repeatCount="indefinite" calcMode="spline" keySplines="0.42 0 0.58 1; 0.42 0 0.58 1" />';
      } else if (n.anim === 'fade-in') {
        animTags = '<animate attributeName="opacity" from="0" to="1" dur="1.2s" fill="freeze" />';
      }

      elements += '<g data-node-id="' + n.id + '" transform="translate(' + cx.toFixed(2) + ', ' + cy.toFixed(2) + ')" style="cursor: move;">' + animTags + shapeSVG + textSVG + '</g>';
    });

    return svgString.replace('<!-- INJECT_CAMERA_RIG_ELEMENTS -->', '\n<defs>\n' + defs + '\n</defs>\n<g id="nodes-layer">\n' + elements + '\n</g>\n<!-- INJECT_CAMERA_RIG_ELEMENTS -->');
  }

window.StillExportStudio = {

  aspectRatio: 16 / 9,
  cachedBlob: null,

  solveCubicBezier(x1, y1, x2, y2, x) {
    if (x <= 0) return 0;
    if (x >= 1) return 1;
    if (x1 === y1 && x2 === y2) return x;
    let t = x;
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
      const cx = 3 * x1;
      const bx = 3 * (x2 - x1) - cx;
      const ax = 1 - cx - bx;
      const currentX = ((ax * t + bx) * t + cx) * t - x;
      if (Math.abs(currentX) < 1e-6) break;
      const deriv = (3 * ax * t + 2 * bx) * t + cx;
      if (Math.abs(deriv) < 1e-6) break;
      t -= currentX / deriv;
    }
    const cy = 3 * y1;
    const by = 3 * (y2 - y1) - cy;
    const ay = 1 - cy - by;
    return ((ay * t + by) * t + cy) * t;
  },

  schedulePreview(delayMs = 60) {
    if (this.isGeneratingFinal) return;
    if (this.previewTimer) clearTimeout(this.previewTimer);

  },

  getScreenPreviewDimensions(targetW, targetH) {
    const container = document.getElementById('stillPreviewContainer');
    let maxW = 720;
    let maxH = 460;

    if (container) {
      const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (rect.width > 100 && rect.height > 100) {
        maxW = Math.max(320, Math.floor(rect.width - 20));
        maxH = Math.max(220, Math.floor(rect.height - 20));
      }
    }

    const pw = Math.max(64, Math.round((targetW * scale) / 2) * 2);
    const ph = Math.max(64, Math.round((targetH * scale) / 2) * 2);
    return { width: pw, height: ph };
  },

    document.getElementById('stillEnableNodes').checked = window.DiagramNodes ? window.DiagramNodes.list.length > 0 : true;
    document.getElementById('stillEnableFlows').checked = window.DataFlows ? window.DataFlows.list.length > 0 : true;
    document.getElementById('stillEnablePayloads').checked = window.DataFlows ? window.DataFlows.list.length > 0 : true;
    document.getElementById('stillEnableGlows').checked = window.Glows ? window.Glows.list.length > 0 : true;
    document.getElementById('stillEnableRipples').checked = window.Ripples ? window.Ripples.list.length > 0 : true;
    document.getElementById('stillEnableCamera').checked = st.camera.motion !== 'static';
    document.getElementById('stillEnableOptical').checked = st.optical.type !== 'none';
    document.getElementById('stillEnableFrame').checked = !!st.overlays.frame;

    this.updateButtonLabel();
    document.getElementById('stillExportModal').style.display = 'flex';

    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      this.renderPreview();
    });
  },

  close() {
    if (this.isGeneratingFinal) return;
    document.getElementById('stillExportModal').style.display = 'none';
  },

  evaluateSMILAnimate(anim, sec) {
    let dur = parseFloat(anim.getAttribute('dur'));
    if (isNaN(dur) || dur <= 0) dur = 1.0;
    const begin = parseFloat(anim.getAttribute('begin')) || 0.0;
    const localSec = sec - begin;
    if (localSec < 0) return null;

    let progress;
    if (anim.getAttribute('repeatCount') === 'indefinite') {
      progress = (localSec % dur) / dur;
    } else {
      progress = Math.min(1.0, localSec / dur);
    }
    if (progress < 0) progress = 0;

    let fromStr = anim.getAttribute('from');
    let toStr = anim.getAttribute('to');
    let segProgress = progress;

    const calcMode = anim.getAttribute('calcMode') || 'linear';
    const splinesAttr = anim.getAttribute('keySplines');
    const splines = splinesAttr ? splinesAttr.split(';').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean) : [];
    const keyTimesAttr = anim.getAttribute('keyTimes');
    const keyTimes = keyTimesAttr ? keyTimesAttr.split(';').map((k) => parseFloat(k.trim())).filter((k) => !isNaN(k)) : [];

    if (anim.hasAttribute('values')) {
      const vals = anim.getAttribute('values').split(';').map((v) => v.trim()).filter((v) => v !== '');
      const numSegments = vals.length - 1;
      if (numSegments > 0) {
        let segIdx = 0;
        if (keyTimes.length === vals.length) {
          for (let i = 0; i < numSegments; i++) {
            if (progress >= keyTimes[i] && progress <= keyTimes[i + 1]) {
              segIdx = i;
              const span = keyTimes[i + 1] - keyTimes[i];
              segProgress = span > 0 ? (progress - keyTimes[i]) / span : 1.0;
              break;
            } else if (progress > keyTimes[numSegments]) {
              segIdx = numSegments - 1;
              segProgress = 1.0;
            }
          }
        } else {
          const scaled = progress * numSegments;
          if (scaled >= numSegments) {
            segIdx = numSegments - 1;
            segProgress = 1.0;
          } else {
            segIdx = Math.floor(scaled);
            segProgress = scaled - segIdx;
          }
        }
        fromStr = vals[segIdx];
        toStr = vals[segIdx + 1];

        if (calcMode === 'spline' && splines[segIdx]) {
          const pts = splines[segIdx].split(/[\s,]+/).map(parseFloat);
          if (pts.length === 4 && !pts.some(isNaN)) {
            segProgress = this.solveCubicBezier(pts[0], pts[1], pts[2], pts[3], segProgress);
          }
        }
      }
    } else if (calcMode === 'spline' && splines[0]) {
      const pts = splines[0].split(/[\s,]+/).map(parseFloat);
      if (pts.length === 4 && !pts.some(isNaN)) {
        segProgress = this.solveCubicBezier(pts[0], pts[1], pts[2], pts[3], segProgress);
      }
    }

    if (fromStr == null || toStr == null) return null;

    const fromParts = fromStr.split(/[\s,]+/);
    const toParts = toStr.split(/[\s,]+/);
    const out = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < fromParts.length; i++) {
      const f = parseFloat(fromParts[i]);
      const t = parseFloat(toParts[i] !== undefined ? toParts[i] : toParts[0]);
      if (!isNaN(f) && !isNaN(t)) {
        out.push((f + (t - f) * segProgress).toFixed(3));
      }
    }
    return out.length > 0 ? out.join(' ') : null;
  },

  bakeStaticSVGAtTime(rawSvg, snapshotSec, renderW, renderH, forcePeakVis, includeLines, includePayloads) {
    const sandbox = document.createElement('div');
    sandbox.style.cssText =
      'position:fixed; left:-9999px; top:-9999px; width:' + this.baseWidth + 'px; height:' + this.baseHeight + 'px; visibility:hidden; pointer-events:none;';
    sandbox.innerHTML = rawSvg;
    document.body.appendChild(sandbox);

    try {
      const svgEl = sandbox.querySelector('svg');
      if (!svgEl) return rawSvg;

      if (svgEl.getAnimations) {
        svgEl.getAnimations({ subtree: true }).forEach((anim) => {
          anim.pause();
          anim.currentTime = snapshotSec * 1000;
        });
      }
      const camRig = svgEl.querySelector('.camera-rig');
      if (camRig) {
        const comp = window.getComputedStyle(camRig);
        if (comp && comp.transform && comp.transform !== 'none') {
          camRig.style.transform = comp.transform;
          camRig.style.animation = 'none';
        }
      }

      svgEl.querySelectorAll('set').forEach((setEl) => {
        const parent = setEl.parentElement;
        const attr = setEl.getAttribute('attributeName');
        const toVal = setEl.getAttribute('to');
        const begin = parseFloat(setEl.getAttribute('begin')) || 0;
        if (parent && attr && toVal && (snapshotSec >= begin || forcePeakVis)) {
          parent.setAttribute(attr, toVal);
        }
        setEl.remove();
      });

      svgEl.querySelectorAll('animateMotion').forEach((motionEl) => {
        const payloadGroup = motionEl.parentElement;
        if (!payloadGroup) return;

        if (!includePayloads) {
          payloadGroup.remove();
          return;
        }

        const mpath = motionEl.querySelector('mpath');
        const href = mpath ? (mpath.getAttribute('href') || mpath.getAttribute('xlink:href') || '') : '';
        const pathId = href.replace(/^#/, '');
        const pathEl = pathId ? svgEl.querySelector('#' + pathId) : null;

        if (pathEl && typeof pathEl.getTotalLength === 'function') {
          const totalLen = pathEl.getTotalLength();
          const dur = parseFloat(motionEl.getAttribute('dur')) || 2.0;
          const begin = parseFloat(motionEl.getAttribute('begin')) || 0.0;
          let localSec = snapshotSec - begin;
          if (localSec < 0) localSec = forcePeakVis ? (dur * 0.5) : 0;

          let prog = (localSec % dur) / dur;
          const ktAttr = motionEl.getAttribute('keyTimes');
          if (ktAttr) {
            const kts = ktAttr.split(';').map((s) => parseFloat(s.trim())).filter((n) => !isNaN(n));
            if (kts.length === 3 && kts[1] > 0) {
              prog = prog <= kts[1] ? (prog / kts[1]) : 1.0;
            }
          }
          if (snapshotSec === 0 && forcePeakVis && prog === 0) {
            prog = 0.5;
          }

          const dist = Math.min(totalLen, Math.max(0, prog * totalLen));
          const pt = pathEl.getPointAtLength(dist);
          let transformStr = 'translate(' + pt.x.toFixed(2) + ', ' + pt.y.toFixed(2) + ')';

          if (motionEl.getAttribute('rotate') === 'auto' && totalLen > 1) {
            const d1 = Math.max(0, dist - 1.5);
            const d2 = Math.min(totalLen, dist + 1.5);
            const p1 = pathEl.getPointAtLength(d1);
            const p2 = pathEl.getPointAtLength(d2);
            const angle = Math.atan2(p2.y - p1.y, p2.x - p1.x) * (180 / Math.PI);
            transformStr += ' rotate(' + angle.toFixed(2) + ')';
          }

          payloadGroup.setAttribute('transform', transformStr);
        }
        motionEl.remove();
      });

      // If user unchecked "Connectors & DataFlow Lines" but kept "Traveling Payloads", hide the track lines & midpoint badges
      if (!includeLines) {
        const flowsLayer = svgEl.querySelector('#flows-layer');
        if (flowsLayer) {
          Array.from(flowsLayer.children).forEach((child) => {
            if (child.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'path') {
              child.setAttribute('stroke', 'none');
              child.removeAttribute('marker-start');
              child.removeAttribute('marker-end');
            } else if (child.tagName.toLowerCase() === 'g' && child.getAttribute('transform')?.startsWith('translate(')) {
              // Midpoint text badge group
              child.remove();
            }
          });
        }
      }

      svgEl.querySelectorAll('animate').forEach((anim) => {
        const parent = anim.parentElement;
        const attr = anim.getAttribute('attributeName');
        if (!parent || !attr) {
          anim.remove();
          return;
        }

        const effectiveTime = (snapshotSec === 0 && forcePeakVis) ? ((parseFloat(anim.getAttribute('dur')) || 2.0) * 0.5) : snapshotSec;
        let val = this.evaluateSMILAnimate(anim, effectiveTime);

        if (forcePeakVis && (attr === 'opacity' || attr === 'stroke-opacity')) {
          const inGlowOrFlow = parent.closest('#glows-layer') || parent.closest('#flows-layer');
          const numVal = parseFloat(val);
          if (inGlowOrFlow && (val === null || isNaN(numVal) || numVal < 0.15)) {
            const valsAttr = anim.getAttribute('values') || '';
            const nums = valsAttr.split(';').map((s) => parseFloat(s.trim())).filter((n) => !isNaN(n));
            const maxOp = nums.length > 0 ? Math.max(...nums) : 1.0;
            val = String(maxOp > 0 ? maxOp : 1.0);
          }
        }

        if (val !== null) {
          parent.setAttribute(attr, val);
        }
        anim.remove();
      });

      svgEl.querySelectorAll('animateTransform').forEach((animT) => {
        animT.remove();
      });

      svgEl.setAttribute('width', String(renderW));
      svgEl.setAttribute('height', String(renderH));

      return window.serializeSVGForExport(svgEl.outerHTML);
    } finally {
      document.body.removeChild(sandbox);
    }
  },

  async buildStillBlob(mimeType, forPreview = false) {
    const payloadState = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(window.currentRenderState));
    const fullTargetW = Math.max(64, parseInt(document.getElementById('stillWidthInput')?.value, 10) || this.baseWidth);
    const fullTargetH = Math.max(64, parseInt(document.getElementById('stillHeightInput')?.value, 10) || this.baseHeight);

    let renderW = fullTargetW;
    let renderH = fullTargetH;
    if (forPreview) {
      const fitted = this.getScreenPreviewDimensions(fullTargetW, fullTargetH);
      renderW = fitted.width;
      renderH = fitted.height;
    }

    const qual = Math.min(1, Math.max(0.1, (parseFloat(document.getElementById('stillQualityInput')?.value || 95) / 100)));
    const snapSec = Math.max(0, parseFloat(document.getElementById('stillTimeNum')?.value || 0));
    const forcePeakVis = !!document.getElementById('stillForcePeakVis')?.checked;

    const enableNodes = !!document.getElementById('stillEnableNodes')?.checked;
    const enableFlows = !!document.getElementById('stillEnableFlows')?.checked;
    const enablePayloads = !!document.getElementById('stillEnablePayloads')?.checked;
    const enableGlows = !!document.getElementById('stillEnableGlows')?.checked;
    const enableRipples = !!document.getElementById('stillEnableRipples')?.checked;
    const enableCamera = !!document.getElementById('stillEnableCamera')?.checked;
    const enableOptical = !!document.getElementById('stillEnableOptical')?.checked;
    const enableFrame = !!document.getElementById('stillEnableFrame')?.checked;

    if (!enableCamera) payloadState.camera.motion = 'static';
    if (!enableOptical) payloadState.optical.type = 'none';
    if (!enableFrame) payloadState.overlays.frame = false;

    const rawSvg = window.buildFullSVG(payloadState, {
      includeNodes: enableNodes,
      includeFlows: enableFlows || enablePayloads,
      includeGlows: enableGlows,
      includeRipples: enableRipples
    });

    const bakedSvg = this.bakeStaticSVGAtTime(rawSvg, snapSec, renderW, renderH, forcePeakVis, enableFlows, enablePayloads);
    const svgBlob = new Blob([bakedSvg], { type: 'image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8' });
    const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(svgBlob);

    try {
      const img = new Image();
      await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        img.onload = resolve;
        img.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Failed to rasterize baked SVG'));
        img.src = blobUrl;
      });

      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
      canvas.width = renderW;
      canvas.height = renderH;
      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');

      if (mimeType === 'image/jpeg' || !payloadState.camera.bgTransparent) {
        ctx.fillStyle = payloadState.camera.bgColor || '#0d1117';
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, renderW, renderH);
      }

      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, renderW, renderH);

      const outBlob = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        canvas.toBlob((b) => (b ? resolve(b) : reject(new Error('Canvas toBlob failed'))), mimeType, qual);
      });

      return {
        blob: outBlob,
        renderWidth: renderW,
        renderHeight: renderH,
        finalWidth: fullTargetW,
        finalHeight: fullTargetH,
        snapSec: snapSec
      };
    } finally {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl);
    }
  },

  async renderPreview() {
    if (this.isGeneratingFinal) return;
    const placeholder = document.getElementById('stillPreviewPlaceholder');
    const previewImg = document.getElementById('stillPreviewImage');
    const previewMeta = document.getElementById('stillPreviewMeta');
    const fmt = document.getElementById('stillFormatSelect')?.value || 'png';
    const mime = fmt === 'jpeg' ? 'image/jpeg' : (fmt === 'webp' ? 'image/webp' : 'image/png');

    try {
      const startTime = performance.now();
      const res = await this.buildStillBlob(mime, true);

      if (this.cachedBlobUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(this.cachedBlobUrl);
      this.cachedBlob = res.blob;
      this.cachedBlobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(res.blob);

      if (placeholder) placeholder.style.display = 'none';
      if (previewImg) {
        previewImg.src = this.cachedBlobUrl;
        previewImg.style.display = 'block';
      }

      const elapsed = (performance.now() - startTime).toFixed(0);
      const dpiMult = parseFloat(document.getElementById('stillDpiSelect')?.value || '1') || 1;
      const dpiEst = Math.round(96 * dpiMult);
      if (previewMeta) {
        previewMeta.textContent =
          'Screen Preview: ' + res.renderWidth + '×' + res.renderHeight + ' px (' + elapsed + ' ms) @ t=' + res.snapSec.toFixed(2) + 's' +
          '  •  Final Download Target: ' + res.finalWidth + '×' + res.finalHeight + ' px (~' + dpiEst + ' DPI)';
      }
    } catch (err) {
      if (placeholder) {
        placeholder.textContent = 'Still preview error: ' + err.message;
        placeholder.style.display = 'block';
      }
    }
  },

  setGeneratingState(active, titleText = '', subText = '') {
    this.isGeneratingFinal = active;
    const overlay = document.getElementById('stillGeneratingOverlay');
    const titleEl = document.getElementById('stillGeneratingTitle');
    const subEl = document.getElementById('stillGeneratingSub');
    const btnDown = document.getElementById('btnDownloadStillFinal');
    const btnRefresh = document.getElementById('btnStillRefreshPreview');
    const btnCloseBottom = document.getElementById('btnCloseStillBottom');

    if (overlay) overlay.style.display = active ? 'flex' : 'none';
    if (titleEl && titleText) titleEl.textContent = titleText;
    if (subEl && subText) subEl.textContent = subText;

    if (btnDown) {
      btnDown.disabled = active;
      if (active) {
        btnDown.textContent = '⏳ Generating your image...';
      } else {
        this.updateButtonLabel();
      }
    }
    if (btnRefresh) btnRefresh.disabled = active;
    if (btnCloseBottom) btnCloseBottom.disabled = active;
  },

  triggerDownload(url, filename) {
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = filename;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
  },

  async download() {
    if (this.isGeneratingFinal) return;

    const fmt = document.getElementById('stillFormatSelect')?.value || 'png';
    const baseName = (document.getElementById('stillFilename')?.value || 'diagram_still').replace(/\.(png|webp|jpe?g)$/i, '');
    const outW = parseInt(document.getElementById('stillWidthInput')?.value, 10) || this.baseWidth;
    const outH = parseInt(document.getElementById('stillHeightInput')?.value, 10) || this.baseHeight;
    const previewMeta = document.getElementById('stillPreviewMeta');

    const list = fmt === 'all'
      ? [
          { ext: 'png', mime: 'image/png', label: 'PNG' },
          { ext: 'webp', mime: 'image/webp', label: 'WebP' },
          { ext: 'jpg', mime: 'image/jpeg', label: 'JPEG' }
        ]
      : [
          {
            ext: fmt === 'jpeg' ? 'jpg' : fmt,
            mime: fmt === 'jpeg' ? 'image/jpeg' : (fmt === 'webp' ? 'image/webp' : 'image/png'),
            label: fmt.toUpperCase()
          }
        ];

    try {
      this.setGeneratingState(
        true,
        '⏳ Generating your high-resolution image (' + outW + ' × ' + outH + ' px)...',
        'Rasterizing vector shapes, connectors, and effects at full print resolution.'
      );

      await new Promise((resolve) => requestAnimationFrame(() => setTimeout(resolve, 35)));

      const startTime = performance.now();

      for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
        const item = list[i];
        if (list.length > 1) {
          this.setGeneratingState(
            true,
            '⏳ Generating ' + item.label + ' (' + (i + 1) + ' of ' + list.length + ') at ' + outW + ' × ' + outH + ' px...',
            'Encoding high-resolution flat image...'
          );
          await new Promise((resolve) => requestAnimationFrame(() => setTimeout(resolve, 20)));
        }

        const res = await this.buildStillBlob(item.mime, false);
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(res.blob);
        const sizeKB = (res.blob.size / 1024).toFixed(1);
        const elapsed = ((performance.now() - startTime) / 1000).toFixed(2);

        if (previewMeta) {
          previewMeta.textContent =
            '✅ Generated Final ' + item.label + ': ' + res.finalWidth + '×' + res.finalHeight + ' px • ' + sizeKB + ' KB (' + elapsed + 's)';
        }

        this.triggerDownload(url, baseName + '.' + item.ext);
        setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 4000);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      alert('Still image export failed: ' + err.message);
    } finally {
      this.setGeneratingState(false);
    }
  }
};

  window.StillExportStudio.init();
});

  function initAccordions() {
    document.querySelectorAll('.accordion-header').forEach((header) => {
      header.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const section = header.parentElement;
        const panel = section.closest('.panel');
        const isOpen = section.classList.contains('open');
        const soloChk = document.getElementById('chkSoloPanels');
        const soloMode = soloChk ? soloChk.checked : false;

        if (!isOpen && soloMode && panel) {
          panel.querySelectorAll('.accordion-section.open').forEach((other) => {
            if (other !== section) other.classList.remove('open');
          });
        }
        section.classList.toggle('open', !isOpen);
      });
    });
  }

  window.openAccordionById = function(accId) {
    const section = document.getElementById(accId);
    if (!section) return;
    const panel = section.closest('.panel');
    const soloChk = document.getElementById('chkSoloPanels');
    const soloMode = soloChk ? soloChk.checked : false;
    if (soloMode && panel) {
      panel.querySelectorAll('.accordion-section.open').forEach((other) => {
        if (other !== section) other.classList.remove('open');
      });
    }
    section.classList.add('open');
  };

  window.toggleAllAccordions = function(panelId, expand) {
    const panel = document.getElementById(panelId);
    if (!panel) return;
    if (expand) {
      const soloChk = document.getElementById('chkSoloPanels');
      if (soloChk) soloChk.checked = false;
    }
    panel.querySelectorAll('.accordion-section').forEach((sec) => {
      sec.classList.toggle('open', expand);
    });
  };

  initAccordions();

  // Wire mutual exclusion & automatic Undo History hooks across all overlay modules
  function wireModuleHooks() {
    if (window.Glows && !window.Glows._mutualHooked) {
      const origGlow = window.Glows.selectGlow.bind(window.Glows);
      window.Glows.selectGlow = (id) => {
        if (window.DiagramNodes) window.DiagramNodes.deselectNode();
        if (window.DataFlows) window.DataFlows.deselectFlow();
        if (window.Ripples) window.Ripples.deselectRipple();
        origGlow(id);
      };
      const origAddGlow = window.Glows.addGlow.bind(window.Glows);
      window.Glows.addGlow = () => { origAddGlow(); if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate(); };
      const origRemGlow = window.Glows.removeGlow.bind(window.Glows);
      window.Glows.removeGlow = (id) => { origRemGlow(id); if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate(); };
      window.Glows._mutualHooked = true;
    }

    if (window.Ripples && !window.Ripples._mutualHooked) {
      const origRipple = window.Ripples.selectRipple.bind(window.Ripples);
      window.Ripples.selectRipple = (id) => {
        if (window.DiagramNodes) window.DiagramNodes.deselectNode();
        if (window.DataFlows) window.DataFlows.deselectFlow();
        if (window.Glows) window.Glows.deselectGlow();
        origRipple(id);
      };
      const origAddRipple = window.Ripples.addRipple.bind(window.Ripples);
      window.Ripples.addRipple = () => { origAddRipple(); if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate(); };
      const origRemRipple = window.Ripples.removeRipple.bind(window.Ripples);
      window.Ripples.removeRipple = (id) => { origRemRipple(id); if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate(); };
      window.Ripples._mutualHooked = true;
    }

    if (window.DataFlows && !window.DataFlows._mutualHooked) {
      const origFlow = window.DataFlows.selectFlow.bind(window.DataFlows);
      window.DataFlows.selectFlow = (id) => {
        if (window.DiagramNodes) window.DiagramNodes.deselectNode();
        if (window.Glows) window.Glows.deselectGlow();
        if (window.Ripples) window.Ripples.deselectRipple();
        origFlow(id);
      };
      const origAddFlow = window.DataFlows.addFlow.bind(window.DataFlows);
      window.DataFlows.addFlow = () => { origAddFlow(); if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate(); };
      const origRemFlow = window.DataFlows.removeFlow.bind(window.DataFlows);
      window.DataFlows.removeFlow = (id) => { origRemFlow(id); if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate(); };
      window.DataFlows._mutualHooked = true;
    }
  }

  function setDragPhotoCropMode(enabled) {
    if (!window.Stage) return;
    window.Stage.dragPhotoCropEnabled = enabled;
    const btn = document.getElementById('btnDragPhotoCrop');
    const previewBox = document.getElementById('previewBox');
    if (btn) {
      btn.classList.toggle('active-tool-btn', enabled);
      btn.innerText = enabled
        ? '🖐️ Dragging Photo Inside Frame (Active — Click to Lock)'
        : '🖐️ Drag Photo Inside Frame (Off)';
    }
    if (previewBox) {
      previewBox.classList.toggle('dragging-photo-crop', enabled);
    }
  }

  function updateOrientationAndPaperUI() {
    const wEl = document.getElementById('canvasWidthInput');
    const hEl = document.getElementById('canvasHeightInput');
    const btnLand = document.getElementById('btnOrientLandscape');
    const btnPort = document.getElementById('btnOrientPortrait');
    const hintEl = document.getElementById('paperPrintHint');
    if (!wEl || !hEl) return;

    const w = parseInt(wEl.value || 1280, 10);
    const h = parseInt(hEl.value || 720, 10);
    if (btnLand) btnLand.classList.toggle('active-orient', w >= h);
    if (btnPort) btnPort.classList.toggle('active-orient', h > w);

    const key = w + 'x' + h;
    if (hintEl) {
      if (paperSpecs[key]) {
        hintEl.style.display = 'block';
        hintEl.innerHTML = '🖨️ <strong>' + paperSpecs[key].name + '</strong>: 300 DPI Print Export = ' + paperSpecs[key].dpi300;
      } else {
        hintEl.style.display = 'none';
      }
    }
  }

  function applyCanvasDimensionSwap(targetMode) {
    const wEl = document.getElementById('canvasWidthInput');
    const hEl = document.getElementById('canvasHeightInput');
    if (!wEl || !hEl) return;

    const w = parseInt(wEl.value || 1280, 10);
    const h = parseInt(hEl.value || 720, 10);

    if (targetMode === 'landscape' && w >= h) return;
    if (targetMode === 'portrait' && h >= w) return;

    wEl.value = h;
    hEl.value = w;

    const fitSelect = document.getElementById('photoFitSelect');
    const patSelect = document.getElementById('canvasPatternSelect');
    if (loadedPhoto && patSelect && patSelect.value === 'photo' && fitSelect && fitSelect.value === 'match') {
      fitSelect.value = 'cover';
      syncBackgroundUIVisibility();
    }

    syncPresetDropdownFromInputs();
    updateOrientationAndPaperUI();
    render();
    if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
  }

  function syncBackgroundUIVisibility() {
    const patternSelect = document.getElementById('canvasPatternSelect');
    const pattern = patternSelect ? patternSelect.value : 'blueprint';
    const fitSelect = document.getElementById('photoFitSelect');
    const fitMode = fitSelect ? fitSelect.value : 'match';

    const photoSizingControls = document.getElementById('photoSizingControls');
    const photoCropSliders = document.getElementById('photoCropSliders');
    const btnMatchPhoto = document.getElementById('btnMatchPhotoSize');
    const bgColorRow = document.getElementById('canvasBgColorRow');
    const gridControlsRow = document.getElementById('gridControlsRow');
    const gridSizeWrap = document.getElementById('gridSizeControlWrap');
    const gridColorLabel = document.getElementById('gridColorLabel');

    const isPhotoMode = pattern === 'photo' && !!loadedPhoto;

    if (photoSizingControls) photoSizingControls.style.display = isPhotoMode ? 'block' : 'none';
    if (photoCropSliders) {
      const showCrop = isPhotoMode && fitMode === 'cover';
      photoCropSliders.style.display = showCrop ? 'block' : 'none';
      if (!showCrop) setDragPhotoCropMode(false);
    }
    if (btnMatchPhoto) btnMatchPhoto.style.display = isPhotoMode ? 'inline-block' : 'none';

    if (bgColorRow) {
      const showBgColor = !isPhotoMode || fitMode === 'contain';
      bgColorRow.style.display = showBgColor ? 'flex' : 'none';
    }

    if (gridControlsRow) {
      const usesGridOrRadial = !isPhotoMode && ['blueprint', 'dots', 'graph', 'dark-radial'].includes(pattern);
      gridControlsRow.style.display = usesGridOrRadial ? 'block' : 'none';
      if (gridColorLabel) {
        gridColorLabel.innerText = pattern === 'dark-radial' ? 'Inner Vignette Colour' : 'Background Grid Colour';
      }
      if (gridSizeWrap) {
        gridSizeWrap.style.display = ['blueprint', 'dots', 'graph'].includes(pattern) ? 'flex' : 'none';
      }
    }
  }

  function syncPresetDropdownFromInputs() {
    const wEl = document.getElementById('canvasWidthInput');
    const hEl = document.getElementById('canvasHeightInput');
    const w = parseInt(wEl ? wEl.value : 1280, 10);
    const h = parseInt(hEl ? hEl.value : 720, 10);
    const presetSelect = document.getElementById('canvasPresetSelect');
    if (!presetSelect) return;
    const key = w + 'x' + h;
    const revKey = h + 'x' + w;
    const match = Array.from(presetSelect.options).find((opt) => opt.value === key || opt.value === revKey);
    presetSelect.value = match ? match.value : 'custom';
  }

  function buildCurrentCanvasState() {
    const patternSelect = document.getElementById('canvasPatternSelect');
    let pattern = patternSelect ? patternSelect.value : 'blueprint';

    if (pattern === 'photo' && !loadedPhoto) {
      pattern = 'blueprint';
      if (patternSelect) patternSelect.value = 'blueprint';
    }

    const widthInput = document.getElementById('canvasWidthInput');
    const heightInput = document.getElementById('canvasHeightInput');
    const fitSelect = document.getElementById('photoFitSelect');
    const fitMode = fitSelect ? fitSelect.value : 'match';

    if (pattern === 'photo' && loadedPhoto) {
      if (fitMode === 'match') {
        if (widthInput) widthInput.value = loadedPhoto.naturalWidth;
        if (heightInput) heightInput.value = loadedPhoto.naturalHeight;
        syncPresetDropdownFromInputs();
      }
      const w = Math.max(64, parseInt(widthInput ? widthInput.value : loadedPhoto.naturalWidth, 10));
      const h = Math.max(64, parseInt(heightInput ? heightInput.value : loadedPhoto.naturalHeight, 10));

      const czEl = document.getElementById('photoCropZoomSlider');
      const cxEl = document.getElementById('photoCropXSlider');
      const cyEl = document.getElementById('photoCropYSlider');

      return {
        filename: loadedPhoto.filename || 'photo_export',
        isBlankCanvas: false,
        pattern: 'photo',
        photoFit: fitMode,
        cropZoom: parseFloat(czEl ? czEl.value : 100),
        cropX: parseFloat(cxEl ? cxEl.value : 50),
        cropY: parseFloat(cyEl ? cyEl.value : 50),
        width: w,
        height: h,
        naturalWidth: loadedPhoto.naturalWidth,
        naturalHeight: loadedPhoto.naturalHeight,
        mime: loadedPhoto.mime,
        b64: loadedPhoto.b64
      };
    }

    const w = Math.max(200, parseInt(widthInput ? widthInput.value : 1280, 10));
    const h = Math.max(200, parseInt(heightInput ? heightInput.value : 720, 10));
    const gcEl = document.getElementById('canvasGridColorPicker');
    const gsEl = document.getElementById('canvasGridSizeSlider');

    return {
      filename: 'diagram_canvas',
      isBlankCanvas: true,
      pattern: pattern,
      gridColor: gcEl ? gcEl.value : '#1e293b',
      gridSize: parseFloat(gsEl ? gsEl.value : 40),
      width: w,
      height: h,
      mime: 'image/svg+xml',
      b64: ''
    };
  }

  window.buildFullSVG = function(state, overrides = {}) {
    if (!state || !window.Effects) return '';
    const includeFlows = overrides.includeFlows !== false;
    const includeGlows = overrides.includeGlows !== false;
    const includeRipples = overrides.includeRipples !== false;
    const includeNodes = overrides.includeNodes !== false;

    let svgString = window.Effects.build(state);
    if (includeRipples && window.Ripples) svgString = window.Ripples.injectIntoSVG(svgString, state);
    if (includeGlows && window.Glows) svgString = window.Glows.injectIntoSVG(svgString, state.image.width, state.image.height);
    if (includeFlows && window.DataFlows) svgString = window.DataFlows.injectIntoSVG(svgString, state);
    if (includeNodes && window.DiagramNodes) svgString = window.DiagramNodes.injectIntoSVG(svgString, state);
    return svgString;
  };

  window.serializeSVGForExport = function(rawSvg) {
    if (!rawSvg) throw new Error('SVG generation returned empty.');

    const parser = new DOMParser();
    const xmlDoc = parser.parseFromString(rawSvg, 'image/svg+xml');

    const parserError = xmlDoc.getElementsByTagName('parsererror');
    if (parserError.length > 0) {
      throw new Error('XML Parsing Failed: ' + parserError[0].textContent);
    }

    const svgRoot = xmlDoc.documentElement;
    const XLINK_NS = 'http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink';

    if (!svgRoot.hasAttribute('xmlns:xlink')) {
      svgRoot.setAttribute('xmlns:xlink', XLINK_NS);
    }

    const elementsWithHref = xmlDoc.querySelectorAll('[href]');
    elementsWithHref.forEach((el) => {
      const hrefVal = el.getAttribute('href');
      if (hrefVal) {
        el.setAttributeNS(XLINK_NS, 'xlink:href', hrefVal);
      }
    });

    const serializer = new XMLSerializer();
    return '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="no"?>\n' + serializer.serializeToString(svgRoot);
  };

  function getExportSVG() {
    const rawSvg = window.buildFullSVG(window.currentRenderState);
    return window.serializeSVGForExport(rawSvg);
  }

  function getCleanBaseName() {
    if (!currentImage || !currentImage.filename) return 'export';
    return currentImage.filename.includes('.') ? currentImage.filename.split('.')[0] : currentImage.filename;
  }

  Stage.init((placement, x, y) => {
    if (window.DataFlows && window.DataFlows.activeId !== null) {
      window.DataFlows.handleStageClick(x, y);
      return;
    }
    if (window.Glows && window.Glows.activeId !== null) {
      window.Glows.handleStageClick(x, y);
      return;
    }
    if (window.Ripples && window.Ripples.activeId !== null) {
      window.Ripples.handleStageClick(x, y);
      return;
    }
    if (window.DiagramNodes && window.DiagramNodes.activeId !== null) {
      window.DiagramNodes.handleStageClick(x, y);
      return;
    }

    const camSel = document.getElementById('cameraMotionSelect');
    const mode = camSel ? camSel.value : 'static';
    if (mode === 'ken-burns') {
      if (placement === 'start') {
        syncVal('startX', x);
        syncVal('startY', y);
      } else {
        syncVal('endX', x);
        syncVal('endY', y);
      }
    } else if (mode === 'static') {
      syncVal('staticX', x);
      syncVal('staticY', y);
    } else if (mode === 'handheld-drift') {
      syncVal('handX', x);
      syncVal('handY', y);
    } else if (mode === 'perspective-tilt') {
      syncVal('perspX', x);
      syncVal('perspY', y);
    }
    render();
  }, (imgData) => {
    loadedPhoto = imgData;
    const patternSelect = document.getElementById('canvasPatternSelect');
    if (patternSelect) patternSelect.value = 'photo';

    const fitSelect = document.getElementById('photoFitSelect');
    if (fitSelect) fitSelect.value = 'match';

    const widthInput = document.getElementById('canvasWidthInput');
    const heightInput = document.getElementById('canvasHeightInput');
    if (widthInput) widthInput.value = imgData.naturalWidth;
    if (heightInput) heightInput.value = imgData.naturalHeight;
    syncPresetDropdownFromInputs();
    updateOrientationAndPaperUI();

    const dropLabel = document.getElementById('uploadDropLabel');
    const dropMeta = document.getElementById('uploadPhotoMeta');
    if (dropLabel) dropLabel.innerText = '📷 ' + imgData.filename;
    if (dropMeta) dropMeta.innerText = 'Native: ' + imgData.naturalWidth + ' \u00d7 ' + imgData.naturalHeight + 'px (Click to replace)';

    syncBackgroundUIVisibility();
    render();
    if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
  });

  // Record Undo History whenever a canvas drag completes
  document.getElementById('previewBox')?.addEventListener('pointerup', () => {
    if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordDebounced(120);
  });

  document.getElementById('btnOrientLandscape')?.addEventListener('click', () => applyCanvasDimensionSwap('landscape'));
  document.getElementById('btnOrientPortrait')?.addEventListener('click', () => applyCanvasDimensionSwap('portrait'));
  document.getElementById('btnOrientSwap')?.addEventListener('click', () => applyCanvasDimensionSwap('swap'));

  document.getElementById('chkSnapToGrid')?.addEventListener('change', () => {
    if (window.Stage) window.Stage.syncOverlayBounds(window.Stage.canvasWidth, window.Stage.canvasHeight);
    if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
  });

  document.getElementById('btnSyncSnapToBgGrid')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const bgGridVal = parseFloat(document.getElementById('canvasGridSizeNum')?.value || 40) || 40;
    const snapSlider = document.getElementById('snapGridSizeSlider');
    const snapNum = document.getElementById('snapGridSizeNum');
    if (snapSlider) snapSlider.value = bgGridVal;
    if (snapNum) snapNum.value = bgGridVal;
    const chk = document.getElementById('chkSnapToGrid');
    if (chk) chk.checked = true;
    if (window.Stage) window.Stage.syncOverlayBounds(window.Stage.canvasWidth, window.Stage.canvasHeight);
    if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
  });

  window.Stage.onPhotoCropDrag = (dx, dy) => {
    if (!loadedPhoto || !currentImage) return;
    const w = currentImage.width;
    const h = currentImage.height;
    const natW = loadedPhoto.naturalWidth || w;
    const natH = loadedPhoto.naturalHeight || h;
    const czEl = document.getElementById('photoCropZoomSlider');
    const cropZoom = Math.max(1.0, parseFloat(czEl ? czEl.value : 100) / 100);
    const scale = window.Stage.getEffectiveScale() || 1.0;

    const baseScale = Math.max(w / natW, h / natH) * cropZoom;
    const overflowX = Math.max(1, (natW * baseScale) - w);
    const overflowY = Math.max(1, (natH * baseScale) - h);

    const cxEl = document.getElementById('photoCropXSlider');
    const cyEl = document.getElementById('photoCropYSlider');
    const curX = parseFloat(cxEl ? cxEl.value : 50);
    const curY = parseFloat(cyEl ? cyEl.value : 50);

    const nextX = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, curX - ((dx / scale) / overflowX) * 100));
    const nextY = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, curY - ((dy / scale) / overflowY) * 100));

    syncVal('photoCropX', Math.round(nextX));
    syncVal('photoCropY', Math.round(nextY));
    render();
  };

  document.getElementById('btnDragPhotoCrop')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    setDragPhotoCropMode(!window.Stage.dragPhotoCropEnabled);
  });

  const presetSel = document.getElementById('canvasPresetSelect');
  if (presetSel) {
    presetSel.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      const val = e.target.value;
      if (val === 'custom') return;
      const parts = val.split('x').map(Number);
      let pw = parts[0];
      let ph = parts[1];
      if (pw > 0 && ph > 0) {
        const isCurrentlyPortrait = document.getElementById('btnOrientPortrait')?.classList.contains('active-orient');
        if (isCurrentlyPortrait && pw > ph) {
          const tmp = pw;
          pw = ph;
          ph = tmp;
        }
        document.getElementById('canvasWidthInput').value = pw;
        document.getElementById('canvasHeightInput').value = ph;

        // If user selected a Paper Size, auto-select 300 DPI in Still Export Studio so it prints at 300 DPI by default
        const dpiSel = document.getElementById('stillDpiSelect');
        if (dpiSel && paperSpecs[pw + 'x' + ph]) {
          dpiSel.value = '3.125';
        }

        const fitSelect = document.getElementById('photoFitSelect');
        const patSelect = document.getElementById('canvasPatternSelect');
        if (loadedPhoto && patSelect && patSelect.value === 'photo' && fitSelect && fitSelect.value === 'match') {
          fitSelect.value = 'cover';
          syncBackgroundUIVisibility();
        }
        updateOrientationAndPaperUI();
        render();
        if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
      }
    });
  }

  const patSel = document.getElementById('canvasPatternSelect');
  if (patSel) {
    patSel.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      if (e.target.value === 'photo' && !loadedPhoto) {
        document.getElementById('fileInput')?.click();
      }
      syncBackgroundUIVisibility();
      render();
      if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
    });
  }

  const fitSel = document.getElementById('photoFitSelect');
  if (fitSel) {
    fitSel.addEventListener('change', () => {
      syncBackgroundUIVisibility();
      render();
      if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
    });
  }

  const btnMatch = document.getElementById('btnMatchPhotoSize');
  if (btnMatch) {
    btnMatch.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (!loadedPhoto) return;
      document.getElementById('canvasWidthInput').value = loadedPhoto.naturalWidth;
      document.getElementById('canvasHeightInput').value = loadedPhoto.naturalHeight;
      document.getElementById('photoFitSelect').value = 'match';
      syncPresetDropdownFromInputs();
      updateOrientationAndPaperUI();
      syncBackgroundUIVisibility();
      render();
      if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
    });
  }

  ['canvasWidthInput', 'canvasHeightInput'].forEach((id) => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) {
      el.addEventListener('input', () => {
        syncPresetDropdownFromInputs();
        updateOrientationAndPaperUI();
        const fitSelect = document.getElementById('photoFitSelect');
        const patSelect = document.getElementById('canvasPatternSelect');
        if (loadedPhoto && patSelect && patSelect.value === 'photo' && fitSelect && fitSelect.value === 'match') {
          fitSelect.value = 'cover';
          syncBackgroundUIVisibility();
        }
        render();
        if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordDebounced();
      });
    }
  });

  ['canvasBgColorPicker', 'canvasGridColorPicker'].forEach((id) => {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) {
      el.addEventListener('input', render);
      el.addEventListener('change', () => {
        render();
        if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
      });
    }
  });

  const bgTrans = document.getElementById('canvasBgTransparent');
  if (bgTrans) {
    bgTrans.addEventListener('change', () => {
      render();
      if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
    });
  }

  function syncVal(id, val) {
    const el = document.getElementById(id + 'Slider');
    const num = document.getElementById(id + 'Num');
    if (el) el.value = val;
    if (num) num.value = val;
  }

  document.getElementById('btnModeStart')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (window.DiagramNodes) window.DiagramNodes.deselectNode();
    if (window.DataFlows) window.DataFlows.deselectFlow();
    if (window.Glows) window.Glows.deselectGlow();
    if (window.Ripples) window.Ripples.deselectRipple();
    if (window.Stage) Stage.setActive('start');
  });

  document.getElementById('btnModeEnd')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (window.DiagramNodes) window.DiagramNodes.deselectNode();
    if (window.DataFlows) window.DataFlows.deselectFlow();
    if (window.Glows) window.Glows.deselectGlow();
    if (window.Ripples) window.Ripples.deselectRipple();
    if (window.Stage) Stage.setActive('end');
  });

  document.querySelectorAll('.slider-row').forEach((row) => {
    const slider = row.querySelector('input[type="range"]');
    const num = row.querySelector('input[type="number"]');
    if (slider && num) {
      num.value = slider.value;
      slider.addEventListener('input', () => {
        num.value = slider.value;
        render();
        if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordDebounced();
      });
      num.addEventListener('input', () => {
        slider.value = num.value;
        render();
        if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordDebounced();
      });
    }
  });

  const camMotionSel = document.getElementById('cameraMotionSelect');
  if (camMotionSel) {
    camMotionSel.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      const v = e.target.value;
      document.getElementById('panel-cam-static').style.display = v === 'static' ? 'block' : 'none';
      document.getElementById('panel-cam-kenburns').style.display = v === 'ken-burns' ? 'block' : 'none';
      document.getElementById('panel-cam-handheld').style.display = v === 'handheld-drift' ? 'block' : 'none';
      document.getElementById('panel-cam-perspective').style.display = v === 'perspective-tilt' ? 'block' : 'none';
      render();
      if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
    });
  }

  const effSel = document.getElementById('effectSelect');
  if (effSel) {
    effSel.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      const v = e.target.value;
      document.getElementById('panel-opt-focus').style.display = v === 'focus-pull' ? 'block' : 'none';
      document.getElementById('panel-opt-tiltshift').style.display = v === 'tilt-shift' ? 'block' : 'none';
      document.getElementById('panel-opt-motionblur').style.display = v === 'motion-blur' ? 'block' : 'none';
      render();
      if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
    });
  }

  const chkFrame = document.getElementById('chkModuleFrame');
  if (chkFrame) {
    chkFrame.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      const group = document.getElementById('groupFrame');
      if (group) group.style.display = e.target.checked ? 'flex' : 'none';
      render();
      if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
    });
  }

  document.querySelectorAll('#settingsPanel select, #settingsPanel input[type="checkbox"], #settingsPanel input[type="color"]').forEach((el) => {
    el.addEventListener('change', () => {
      render();
      if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.recordImmediate();
    });
    if (el.type === 'color') el.addEventListener('input', render);
  });

  window.syncUIAfterHistoryRestore = function() {
    syncBackgroundUIVisibility();
    updateOrientationAndPaperUI();
    const cm = document.getElementById('cameraMotionSelect')?.value || 'static';
    document.getElementById('panel-cam-static').style.display = cm === 'static' ? 'block' : 'none';
    document.getElementById('panel-cam-kenburns').style.display = cm === 'ken-burns' ? 'block' : 'none';
    document.getElementById('panel-cam-handheld').style.display = cm === 'handheld-drift' ? 'block' : 'none';
    document.getElementById('panel-cam-perspective').style.display = cm === 'perspective-tilt' ? 'block' : 'none';

    const ef = document.getElementById('effectSelect')?.value || 'none';
    document.getElementById('panel-opt-focus').style.display = ef === 'focus-pull' ? 'block' : 'none';
    document.getElementById('panel-opt-tiltshift').style.display = ef === 'tilt-shift' ? 'block' : 'none';
    document.getElementById('panel-opt-motionblur').style.display = ef === 'motion-blur' ? 'block' : 'none';

    const fr = document.getElementById('chkModuleFrame')?.checked;
    const grp = document.getElementById('groupFrame');
    if (grp) grp.style.display = fr ? 'flex' : 'none';

    render();
  };

  function getNumVal(id, fallback) {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!el) return fallback;
    const v = parseFloat(el.value);
    return isNaN(v) ? fallback : v;
  }

  function render() {
    currentImage = buildCurrentCanvasState();
    if (!currentImage) return;

    const dim = document.getElementById('imgDimensions');
    if (dim) {
      dim.innerHTML = currentImage.width + ' &times; ' + currentImage.height + (currentImage.isBlankCanvas ? ' (Canvas)' : '');
    }

    if (window.Stage) {
      window.Stage.syncOverlayBounds(currentImage.width, currentImage.height);
    }

    const camSel = document.getElementById('cameraMotionSelect');
    const camMode = camSel ? camSel.value : 'static';
    let sx = 50;
    let sy = 50;
    let sz = 1.0;
    let ex = 60;
    let ey = 40;
    let ez = 1.3;
    let pSway = 1.4;
    let pBreathe = 1.8;
    let pTiltX = 0;
    let pTiltY = 0;

    if (camMode === 'static') {
      sx = ex = getNumVal('staticXSlider', 50);
      sy = ey = getNumVal('staticYSlider', 50);
      sz = ez = getNumVal('staticZoomSlider', 1.0);
    } else if (camMode === 'ken-burns') {
      sx = getNumVal('startXSlider', 50);
      sy = getNumVal('startYSlider', 50);
      sz = getNumVal('startXZoomSlider', 1.0);
      ex = getNumVal('endXSlider', 60);
      ey = getNumVal('endYSlider', 40);
      ez = getNumVal('endXZoomSlider', 1.3);
    } else if (camMode === 'handheld-drift') {
      sx = ex = getNumVal('handXSlider', 50);
      sy = ey = getNumVal('handYSlider', 50);
      sz = ez = getNumVal('handZoomSlider', 1.1);
      pSway = getNumVal('driftSwaySlider', 1.4);
      pBreathe = getNumVal('driftBreatheSlider', 1.8);
    } else if (camMode === 'perspective-tilt') {
      sx = ex = getNumVal('perspXSlider', 50);
      sy = ey = getNumVal('perspYSlider', 50);
      sz = ez = getNumVal('perspZoomSlider', 1.0);
      pTiltX = getNumVal('perspTiltXSlider', 25);
      pTiltY = getNumVal('perspTiltYSlider', 0);
    }

    const bgPick = document.getElementById('canvasBgColorPicker');
    const bgTr = document.getElementById('canvasBgTransparent');
    const loopSel = document.getElementById('loopSelect');
    const optSel = document.getElementById('effectSelect');
    const tsShapeSel = document.getElementById('tsShapeSelect');
    const frChk = document.getElementById('chkModuleFrame');
    const frStyleSel = document.getElementById('frameStyleSelect');

    const state = {
      image: currentImage,
      duration: getNumVal('durationSlider', 7),
      loop: loopSel ? loopSel.value : 'once',
      camera: {
        bgColor: bgPick ? bgPick.value : '#0d1117',
        bgTransparent: bgTr ? bgTr.checked : false,
        motion: camMode,
        startX: sx,
        startY: sy,
        startZoom: sz,
        endX: ex,
        endY: ey,
        endZoom: ez,
        sway: pSway,
        breathe: pBreathe,
        tiltX: pTiltX,
        tiltY: pTiltY
      },
      optical: {
        type: optSel ? optSel.value : 'none',
        startBlur: getNumVal('startBlurSlider', 0),
        endBlur: getNumVal('endBlurSlider', 0),
        focusDur: getNumVal('focusDurSlider', 4),
        tsShape: tsShapeSel ? tsShapeSel.value : 'horizontal',
        tsPosX: getNumVal('tsPosXSlider', 50),
        tsPos: getNumVal('tsPosSlider', 50),
        tsWidth: getNumVal('tsWidthSlider', 20),
        tsBlur: getNumVal('tsBlurSlider', 12),
        mbInt: getNumVal('mbIntSlider', 15),
        mbAng: getNumVal('mbAngSlider', 0)
      },
      overlays: {
        frame: frChk ? frChk.checked : false,
        frameStyle: frStyleSel ? frStyleSel.value : 'walnut',
        frameDepth: getNumVal('frameDepthSlider', 40)
      }
    };

    window.currentRenderState = state;

    const rStart = document.getElementById('reticleStart');
    const rEnd = document.getElementById('reticleEnd');
    const rCenter = document.getElementById('reticleCenter');

    if (rStart) rStart.style.display = (camMode === 'ken-burns') ? 'flex' : 'none';
    if (rEnd) rEnd.style.display = (camMode === 'ken-burns') ? 'flex' : 'none';
    if (rCenter) {
      rCenter.style.display = (camMode !== 'ken-burns') ? 'flex' : 'none';
      rCenter.style.left = sx + '%';
      rCenter.style.top = sy + '%';
    }

    const editingNode = window.DiagramNodes ? window.DiagramNodes.activeId !== null : false;
    const editingFlow = window.DataFlows ? window.DataFlows.activeId !== null : false;
    const editingGlow = window.Glows ? window.Glows.activeId !== null : false;
    const editingRipple = window.Ripples ? window.Ripples.activeId !== null : false;

    if (!editingNode && !editingFlow && !editingGlow && !editingRipple && window.Stage) {
      window.Stage.updateReticles(sx, sy, ex, ey);
    }
    if (editingFlow && window.DataFlows) {
      window.DataFlows.updateUIOverlay();
    }
    if (editingNode && window.DiagramNodes) {
      const activeNode = window.DiagramNodes.list.find((n) => n.id === window.DiagramNodes.activeId);
      if (activeNode) window.DiagramNodes.updateUIOverlay(activeNode);
    }

    try {
      const svgContainer = document.getElementById('svgContainer');
      if (svgContainer) {
        svgContainer.innerHTML = window.buildFullSVG(state);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('SVG Render Exception:', e);
    }
  }

  window.triggerAppRender = render;

  document.getElementById('btnReplay')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const container = document.getElementById('svgContainer');
    if (!container || !window.currentRenderState) return;
    container.innerHTML = '';
    setTimeout(() => {
      container.innerHTML = window.buildFullSVG(window.currentRenderState);
    }, 10);
  });

  document.getElementById('btnDownloadSVG')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    try {
      if (!currentImage || !window.currentRenderState) return;
      const svgData = getExportSVG();
      const blob = new Blob([svgData], { type: 'image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8' });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.style.display = 'none';
      a.href = url;
      a.download = getCleanBaseName() + '_animated.svg';
      document.body.appendChild(a);
      a.click();
      setTimeout(() => {
        document.body.removeChild(a);
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      }, 150);
    } catch (err) {
      alert('SVG Download Error: ' + err.message);
    }
  });

  document.getElementById('btnDownloadHTML')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    try {
      if (!currentImage || !window.currentRenderState) return;
      const w = currentImage.width || 1280;
      const h = currentImage.height || 720;
      const bgTr = document.getElementById('canvasBgTransparent');
      const bgPick = document.getElementById('canvasBgColorPicker');
      const isTransparent = bgTr ? bgTr.checked : false;
      const bgColor = isTransparent ? 'transparent' : (bgPick ? bgPick.value : '#0d1117');
      const svgData = getExportSVG();

      const htmlData =
        '<!DOCTYPE html>\n<html lang="en">\n<head>\n  <meta charset="UTF-8">\n' +
        '  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">\n' +
        '  <title>Animated Diagram &amp; Topology</title>\n' +
        '  <style>\n' +
        '    * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }\n' +
        '    body { background: ' + bgColor + '; display: flex; justify-content: center; align-items: center; width: 100vw; height: 100vh; overflow: hidden; }\n' +
        '    .stage-frame { width: min(100vw, calc(100vh * (' + w + ' / ' + h + '))); height: min(100vh, calc(100vw * (' + h + ' / ' + w + '))); aspect-ratio: ' + w + ' / ' + h + '; display: flex; justify-content: center; align-items: center; }\n' +
        '    svg { width: 100%; height: 100%; display: block; }\n' +
        '  </style>\n</head>\n<body>\n  <div class="stage-frame">\n    ' + svgData + '\n  </div>\n</body>\n</html>';

      const blob = new Blob([htmlData], { type: 'text/html;charset=utf-8' });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.style.display = 'none';
      a.href = url;
      a.download = getCleanBaseName() + '_animated.html';
      document.body.appendChild(a);
      a.click();
      setTimeout(() => {
        document.body.removeChild(a);
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      }, 150);
    } catch (err) {
      alert('HTML Download Error: ' + err.message);
    }
  });

  if (window.DiagramNodes) window.DiagramNodes.init();
  if (window.DataFlows) window.DataFlows.init();
  if (window.Glows) window.Glows.init();
  if (window.Ripples) window.Ripples.init();
  wireModuleHooks();
  syncBackgroundUIVisibility();
  updateOrientationAndPaperUI();

  if (effSel) effSel.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
  if (chkFrame) chkFrame.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
  if (window.HistoryManager) window.HistoryManager.init();
